    @Query("UPDATE health_records SET deletedAt = :now, updatedAt = :now, syncState = :pending WHERE id = :id")
    suspend fun softDelete(id: String, now: Long, pending: SyncState = SyncState.PENDING)

    /** 同一次量測的近似重複判斷：同類型、數值幾乎相同、時間差在區間內（含已刪除的，避免刪掉的又被別的來源帶回）。 */
    @Query(
        "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM health_records WHERE type = :type AND ABS(value - :value) < 0.05 " +
            "AND ((value2 IS NULL AND :value2 IS NULL) OR ABS(IFNULL(value2, 0) - IFNULL(:value2, 0)) < 0.5) " +
            "AND measuredAt BETWEEN :from AND :to"
    )
    suspend fun countNearby(type: RecordType, value: Double, value2: Double?, from: Long, to: Long): Int

    /** 指定時間附近（未刪除）最接近的一筆；補上照片時用。 */
    @Query("SELECT * FROM health_records WHERE deletedAt IS NULL AND type = :type AND measuredAt BETWEEN :from AND :to ORDER BY ABS(measuredAt - :target) LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun nearest(type: RecordType, from: Long, to: Long, target: Long): HealthRecord?

    @Query("UPDATE health_records SET syncState = 'SYNCED' WHERE id = :id AND updatedAt = :updatedAt")
    suspend fun markSynced(id: String, updatedAt: Long): Int

    /** 待上傳的筆數（含已刪除、尚未同步的），給設定頁顯示。 */
    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM health_records WHERE syncState != 'SYNCED'")
    fun observePendingCount(): Flow<Int>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM health_records WHERE syncState != 'SYNCED'")
    suspend fun pendingSync(): List<HealthRecord>
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\prefs\UserSettings.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.prefs

import android.content.Context
import androidx.datastore.core.DataStore
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.Preferences
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.booleanPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.doublePreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.edit
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.intPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.core.stringPreferencesKey
import androidx.datastore.preferences.preferencesDataStore
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.map

enum class Sex { UNSPECIFIED, MALE, FEMALE }

/** 個人設定（單人使用）。未填的欄位為 null / 預設值。 */
data class UserSettings(
    val displayName: String = "",
    val sex: Sex = Sex.UNSPECIFIED,
    val birthYear: Int? = null,
    val heightCm: Double? = null,
    val targetWeightKg: Double? = null,
    /** 血糖目標範圍（mg/dL），由使用者依醫師建議自行設定；App 只用來畫範圍帶與統計，不判斷好壞。 */
    val glucoseLow: Double? = null,
    val glucoseHigh: Double? = null,
    /** 量測提醒：啟用、每天的提醒時間（當天第幾分鐘，0–1439）、今天已記錄血糖就不提醒。 */
    val reminderEnabled: Boolean = false,
    val reminderTimes: List<Int> = emptyList(),
    val reminderSkipIfRecorded: Boolean = true,
    /** 開啟 App 需要指紋 / 臉部 / 手機 PIN。 */
    val appLock: Boolean = false,
    /** 新增紀錄的樣式：true = 底部面板（預設）、false = 原本的對話框。 */
    val addAsSheet: Boolean = true,
    /** 總覽圖表的呈現方式：0 趨勢、1 日曆、2 時段。 */
    val overviewChart: Int = 0,
    /** App 自動檢查更新的間隔（小時）；0 = 只手動檢查。 */
    val updateIntervalHours: Int = 0,
) {
    /** BMI；身高或體重不合理時回傳 null。 */
    fun bmi(weightKg: Double): Double? {
        val h = heightCm ?: return null
        if (h !in 100.0..250.0 || weightKg !in 20.0..300.0) return null
        val m = h / 100.0
        return weightKg / (m * m)
    }
}

private val Context.settingsStore: DataStore<Preferences> by preferencesDataStore(name = "user_settings")

class UserSettingsRepository(private val context: Context) {
    private object Keys {
        val name = stringPreferencesKey("display_name")
        val sex = stringPreferencesKey("sex")
        val birthYear = intPreferencesKey("birth_year")
        val height = doublePreferencesKey("height_cm")
        val target = doublePreferencesKey("target_weight_kg")
        val glucoseLow = doublePreferencesKey("glucose_low")
        val glucoseHigh = doublePreferencesKey("glucose_high")
        val reminderEnabled = booleanPreferencesKey("reminder_enabled")
        val reminderTimes = stringPreferencesKey("reminder_times")
        val reminderSkip = booleanPreferencesKey("reminder_skip_if_recorded")
        val appLock = booleanPreferencesKey("app_lock")
        val addAsSheet = booleanPreferencesKey("add_as_sheet")
        val overviewChart = intPreferencesKey("overview_chart")
        val updateInterval = intPreferencesKey("update_interval_hours")
    }

    val settings: Flow<UserSettings> = context.settingsStore.data.map { p ->
        UserSettings(
            displayName = p[Keys.name].orEmpty(),
            sex = p[Keys.sex]?.let { runCatching { Sex.valueOf(it) }.getOrNull() } ?: Sex.UNSPECIFIED,
            birthYear = p[Keys.birthYear],
            heightCm = p[Keys.height],
            targetWeightKg = p[Keys.target],
            glucoseLow = p[Keys.glucoseLow],
            glucoseHigh = p[Keys.glucoseHigh],
            reminderEnabled = p[Keys.reminderEnabled] ?: false,
            reminderTimes = parseTimes(p[Keys.reminderTimes]),
            reminderSkipIfRecorded = p[Keys.reminderSkip] ?: true,
            appLock = p[Keys.appLock] ?: false,
            addAsSheet = p[Keys.addAsSheet] ?: true,
            overviewChart = (p[Keys.overviewChart] ?: 0).coerceIn(0, 2),
            updateIntervalHours = p[Keys.updateInterval] ?: 0,
        )
    }

    suspend fun saveUpdateInterval(hours: Int) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { it[Keys.updateInterval] = hours }
    }

    suspend fun saveAddAsSheet(value: Boolean) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { it[Keys.addAsSheet] = value }
    }

    suspend fun saveOverviewChart(mode: Int) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { it[Keys.overviewChart] = mode.coerceIn(0, 2) }
    }

    /** 只改提醒設定，不動個人資料欄位。 */
    suspend fun saveReminders(enabled: Boolean, times: List<Int>, skipIfRecorded: Boolean) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { p ->
            p[Keys.reminderEnabled] = enabled
            p[Keys.reminderTimes] = times.distinct().sorted().joinToString(",")
            p[Keys.reminderSkip] = skipIfRecorded
        }
    }

    suspend fun saveAppLock(enabled: Boolean) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { it[Keys.appLock] = enabled }
    }

    private fun parseTimes(raw: String?): List<Int> =
        raw.orEmpty().split(',').mapNotNull { it.trim().toIntOrNull() }.filter { it in 0..1439 }.distinct().sorted()

    suspend fun save(s: UserSettings) {
        context.settingsStore.edit { p ->
            p[Keys.name] = s.displayName
            p[Keys.sex] = s.sex.name
            s.birthYear?.let { p[Keys.birthYear] = it } ?: p.remove(Keys.birthYear)
            s.heightCm?.let { p[Keys.height] = it } ?: p.remove(Keys.height)
            s.targetWeightKg?.let { p[Keys.target] = it } ?: p.remove(Keys.target)
            s.glucoseLow?.let { p[Keys.glucoseLow] = it } ?: p.remove(Keys.glucoseLow)
            s.glucoseHigh?.let { p[Keys.glucoseHigh] = it } ?: p.remove(Keys.glucoseHigh)
        }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\CalendarMath.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.time.YearMonth
import java.time.ZoneId

/** 日曆熱度圖與時段點圖用的純計算。只算筆數與位置，不評斷數值好壞。 */
object CalendarMath {
    /** 月曆格子（週日起算）：前面補 null 對齊星期，之後是當月每一天。 */
    fun monthGrid(month: YearMonth): List<LocalDate?> {
        val first = month.atDay(1)
        val lead = first.dayOfWeek.value % 7 // 週日=0、週一=1…週六=6
        return List(lead) { null } + (1..month.lengthOfMonth()).map { month.atDay(it) }
    }

    fun countsByDay(records: List<HealthRecord>, zone: ZoneId): Map<LocalDate, Int> =
        records.groupingBy { Instant.ofEpochMilli(it.measuredAt).atZone(zone).toLocalDate() }.eachCount()

    /** 顏色深淺：0 筆=0、1 筆=1、2 筆=2、3 筆以上=3。 */
    fun level(count: Int): Int = count.coerceIn(0, 3)

    /** 時段點圖的一個點：row 0 = 今天、往下是更早的日子；fraction = 當天第幾分鐘 / 1440。 */
    data class DayDot(val row: Int, val fraction: Float, val record: HealthRecord)

    fun dayDots(records: List<HealthRecord>, rows: Int, today: LocalDate, zone: ZoneId): List<DayDot> =
        records.mapNotNull { r ->
            val t = Instant.ofEpochMilli(r.measuredAt).atZone(zone)
            val row = (today.toEpochDay() - t.toLocalDate().toEpochDay()).toInt()
            if (row !in 0 until rows) null
            else DayDot(row, (t.hour * 60 + t.minute) / 1440f, r)
        }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\HealthRecordRepository.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.backup.RestoreAction
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.backup.planRestore
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecordDao
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.PhotoStore
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase
import androidx.room.withTransaction
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import java.util.UUID

enum class AddOutcome { ADDED, DUPLICATE, INVALID }

/** 補照片的結果：已補上／附近的紀錄已經有照片（視為重複）／附近沒有紀錄。 */
enum class AttachOutcome { ATTACHED, HAS_PHOTO, NONE }

data class ImportResult(val inserted: Int, val skipped: Int)

data class RestoreResult(val inserted: Int, val updated: Int, val skipped: Int)

class HealthRecordRepository(private val dao: HealthRecordDao, private val db: RoomDatabase? = null) {
    /** 批次寫入合併成單一交易：全有或全無（中途失敗不留半套資料），畫面只重繪一次。 */
    private suspend fun <T> inTransaction(block: suspend () -> T): T = db?.withTransaction { block() } ?: block()

    private companion object {
        const val DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS = 2 * 60_000L
    }

    fun observeAll(): Flow<List<HealthRecord>> = dao.observeAll()

    /** 新增手動紀錄；驗證失敗回傳錯誤訊息，成功回傳 null。 */
    suspend fun addManual(
        type: RecordType,
        value: Double?,
        value2: Double?,
        measuredAt: Long,
        note: String,
        source: RecordSource = RecordSource.MANUAL,
        photoPath: String? = null,
        context: MeasureContext? = null,
        now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): String? {
        RecordValidator.validate(type, value, value2)?.let { return it }
        RecordValidator.validateTime(measuredAt, now)?.let { return it }
        dao.upsert(
            HealthRecord(
                id = UUID.randomUUID().toString(),
                type = type,
                value = value!!,
                value2 = if (type == RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE) value2 else null,
                measuredAt = measuredAt,
                source = source,
                note = note.trim(),
                syncState = SyncState.PENDING,
                createdAt = now,
                updatedAt = now,
                photoPath = photoPath,
                context = if (type == RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE) context else null,
            )
        )
        return null
    }

    /**
     * 照片的拍攝時間前後 2 分鐘內已經有血糖紀錄、而那筆還沒有照片：把照片掛上去（數值、情境不動），
     * 回傳 ATTACHED。補上照片的紀錄標為待同步、來源改為照片辨識，雲端同步時才會一併上傳照片。
     */
    suspend fun attachPhotoIfNearby(measuredAt: Long, photoPath: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): AttachOutcome {
        val near = dao.nearest(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, measuredAt - DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS, measuredAt + DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS, measuredAt) ?: return AttachOutcome.NONE
        if (near.photoPath != null && java.io.File(near.photoPath).isFile) return AttachOutcome.HAS_PHOTO // 同一時間已有紀錄和照片：不論辨識出的數字，都不再新增
        dao.upsert(
            near.copy(
                photoPath = photoPath, source = RecordSource.OCR,
                updatedAt = maxOf(now, near.updatedAt + 1), syncState = SyncState.PENDING,
            )
        )
        return AttachOutcome.ATTACHED
    }

    /** 批次照片匯入用：數值不合理回 INVALID；±2 分鐘內已有相同數值回 DUPLICATE（不重複寫入）。 */
    suspend fun addFromPhoto(value: Double, measuredAt: Long, photoPath: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): AddOutcome {
        if (RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, value, null) != null) return AddOutcome.INVALID
        if (RecordValidator.validateTime(measuredAt, now) != null) return AddOutcome.INVALID
        if (dao.countNearby(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, value, null, measuredAt - DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS, measuredAt + DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS) > 0) {
            return AddOutcome.DUPLICATE
        }
        val err = addManual(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, value, null, measuredAt, "", RecordSource.OCR, photoPath, null, now)
        return if (err == null) AddOutcome.ADDED else AddOutcome.INVALID
    }

    /**
     * 修改既有紀錄（數值、測量時間、備註）。類型、來源與照片不變。
     * 更新時間一定比原本大，並標為待同步，雲端與其他裝置才會以這次修改為準。
     */
    suspend fun update(
        id: String,
        value: Double?,
        value2: Double?,
        measuredAt: Long,
        note: String,
        context: MeasureContext? = null,
        now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): String? {
        val existing = dao.get(id) ?: return "找不到這筆紀錄"
        if (existing.deletedAt != null) return "這筆紀錄已被刪除"
        RecordValidator.validate(existing.type, value, value2)?.let { return it }
        RecordValidator.validateTime(measuredAt, now)?.let { return it }
        dao.upsert(
            existing.copy(
                value = value!!,
                value2 = if (existing.type == RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE) value2 else null,
                measuredAt = measuredAt,
                note = note.trim(),
                context = if (existing.type == RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE) context else null,
                syncState = SyncState.PENDING,
                updatedAt = maxOf(now, existing.updatedAt + 1),
            )
        )
        return null
    }

    /** 批次匯入（Health Connect／CSV）。id 是確定性的，所以重複匯入、或匯入使用者已刪除的紀錄都不會重複出現。 */
    suspend fun importRecords(records: List<HealthRecord>): ImportResult {
        return inTransaction {
        var inserted = 0
        for (r in records) {
            // 不同來源（米家畫面、Health Connect、CSV）可能是同一次量測：同類型、同數值、時間差 2 分鐘內視為重複。
            val duplicate = dao.countNearby(r.type, r.value, r.value2, r.measuredAt - DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS, r.measuredAt + DUPLICATE_WINDOW_MS) > 0
            if (!duplicate && dao.insertIgnore(r) != -1L) inserted++
        }
        ImportResult(inserted = inserted, skipped = records.size - inserted)
        }
    }

    suspend fun allForBackup(): List<HealthRecord> = dao.all()

    /** 還原備份：新增沒有的、較新的覆蓋舊的、其餘略過。還原的資料一律標為待同步。 */
    suspend fun restore(incoming: List<HealthRecord>): RestoreResult {
        return inTransaction {
        var inserted = 0
        var updated = 0
        var skipped = 0
        for (r in incoming) {
            val existing = dao.get(r.id)
            when (planRestore(existing, r)) {
                RestoreAction.INSERT -> { dao.upsert(r); inserted++ }
                RestoreAction.UPDATE -> { dao.upsert(r.copy(photoPath = existing?.photoPath)); updated++ }
                RestoreAction.SKIP -> skipped++
            }
        }
        RestoreResult(inserted, updated, skipped)
        }
    }

    /** 軟刪除並一併刪掉原始照片檔（同步端只需要知道刪除事件，不需要照片）。 */
    suspend fun delete(id: String, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()) {
        dao.get(id)?.photoPath?.let { PhotoStore.delete(it) }
        dao.softDelete(id, now)
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\HealthStandards.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.prefs.Sex
import java.time.Year

/**
 * 體重／體脂率／血糖的參考標準與判定（純計算，方便單元測試）。
 *
 * 來源與限制：
 * - BMI：台灣衛福部成人標準（18.5／24／27）與 WHO（18.5／25／30）。
 * - 體脂率：台灣常見的分齡參考值（18–39、40–59、60 歲以上，男女不同）。
 *   WHO 沒有訂定各年齡層體脂率門檻，這一項屬衛福部／國健署推廣與常見體脂計使用的參考值。
 * - 血糖：WHO／ADA 診斷參考（空腹 70–99、100–125 前期、≥126 糖尿病；餐後 <140、140–199 前期、≥200 糖尿病）。
 *   65 歲以上常有較寬鬆的控制目標，實際請依醫師指示。
 * - 未滿 18 歲的 BMI 與體脂率需使用兒童／青少年成長曲線，本程式不做成人判定。
 *
 * 這些判定僅供個人參考，不能取代醫師診斷。
 */
object HealthStandards {
    enum class Severity { LOW, NORMAL, HIGH, UNKNOWN }

    data class Assessment(val label: String, val detail: String, val severity: Severity)

    data class WeightRange(val minKg: Double, val maxKg: Double, val detail: String)

    fun ageFromBirthYear(birthYear: Int?, nowYear: Int = Year.now().value): Int? {
        val y = birthYear ?: return null
        val age = nowYear - y
        return age.takeIf { it in 0..130 }
    }

    fun bmi(weightKg: Double?, heightCm: Double?): Double? {
        val w = weightKg ?: return null
        val h = heightCm ?: return null
        if (w !in 20.0..300.0 || h !in 100.0..250.0) return null
        val m = h / 100.0
        return w / (m * m)
    }

    /** 衛福部 BMI 18.5–24 對應的標準體重範圍（公斤）。 */
    fun healthyWeightRange(heightCm: Double?, age: Int? = null): WeightRange? {
        val h = heightCm ?: return null
        if (h !in 100.0..250.0) return null
        val m = h / 100.0
        val min = 18.5 * m * m
        val max = 24.0 * m * m
        val older = if (age != null && age >= 65) "；65 歲以上可與醫師討論較寬鬆範圍（約 BMI 20–27）" else ""
        return WeightRange(min, max, "衛福部 BMI 18.5–24 對應 ${"%.1f".format(min)}–${"%.1f".format(max)} kg$older")
    }

    fun bmiAssessment(bmi: Double, age: Int? = null): Assessment {
        if (age != null && age < 18) {
            return Assessment("青少年", "未滿 18 歲請使用兒童／青少年 BMI 成長曲線，不以成人標準判定。", Severity.UNKNOWN)
        }
        val who = "WHO：18.5–25 正常、25–30 過重、≥30 肥胖"
        val older = if (age != null && age >= 65) "；65 歲以上可與醫師討論較寬鬆範圍" else ""
        return when {
            bmi < 18.5 -> Assessment("過瘦", "BMI $bmi < 18.5（衛福部成人標準；$who）$older", Severity.LOW)
            bmi < 24.0 -> Assessment("正常", "BMI $bmi 在 18.5–24 正常範圍（衛福部；$who）$older", Severity.NORMAL)
            bmi < 27.0 -> Assessment("過重", "BMI $bmi 在 24–27 過重範圍（衛福部；$who）$older", Severity.HIGH)
            else -> Assessment("肥胖", "BMI $bmi ≥ 27（衛福部；$who）$older", Severity.HIGH)
        }
    }

    /** 體脂率分齡判定；性別未指定或年齡未知時回傳 null（不猜）。 */
    fun bodyFatAssessment(percent: Double, sex: Sex, age: Int?): Assessment? {
        if (sex == Sex.UNSPECIFIED || age == null) return null
        if (age < 18) {
            return Assessment("青少年", "未滿 18 歲請使用兒童／青少年成長曲線，不以成人分齡標準判定。", Severity.UNKNOWN)
        }
        val band = fatBand(sex, age) ?: return null
        val detail = "${band.label} 正常 ${fmt(band.low)}–${fmt(band.normalHigh)}%；" +
            "過重 ${fmt(band.normalHigh)}–${fmt(band.overweightHigh)}%；肥胖 ≥${fmt(band.overweightHigh)}%（常見分齡參考）"
        return when {
            percent < band.low -> Assessment("過瘦", "體脂率 ${fmt(percent)}% 低於 ${fmt(band.low)}%；$detail", Severity.LOW)
            percent <= band.normalHigh -> Assessment("正常", "體脂率 ${fmt(percent)}% 在正常範圍；$detail", Severity.NORMAL)
            percent <= band.overweightHigh -> Assessment("過重", "體脂率 ${fmt(percent)}% 偏高；$detail", Severity.HIGH)
            else -> Assessment("肥胖", "體脂率 ${fmt(percent)}% 偏高；$detail", Severity.HIGH)
        }
    }

    fun glucoseAssessment(value: Double, context: MeasureContext?, age: Int? = null): Assessment {
        val band = glucoseBand(context)
        val older = if (age != null && age >= 65) "；65 歲以上控制目標常較寬鬆（空腹約 90–130），請依醫師指示" else ""
        val source = "WHO／ADA 參考"
        return when {
            value < band.low -> Assessment("低血糖", "血糖 ${fmt(value)} mg/dL < ${fmt(band.low)}（$source）$older", Severity.LOW)
            value <= band.normalHigh -> Assessment("正常", "血糖 ${fmt(value)} mg/dL 在 ${band.label}正常範圍 ${fmt(band.low)}–${fmt(band.normalHigh)}（$source）$older", Severity.NORMAL)
            value <= band.prediabetesHigh -> Assessment(
                "偏高（糖尿病前期）",
                "血糖 ${fmt(value)} mg/dL 在 ${band.label}偏高範圍 ${fmt(band.normalHigh + 1)}–${fmt(band.prediabetesHigh)}（$source）$older",
                Severity.HIGH,
            )
            else -> Assessment(
                "糖尿病範圍",
                "血糖 ${fmt(value)} mg/dL ≥ ${fmt(band.prediabetesHigh + 1)}（$source；請就醫確認）$older",
                Severity.HIGH,
            )
        }
    }

    fun weightAssessment(weightKg: Double, heightCm: Double?, age: Int? = null): Assessment? =
        bmi(weightKg, heightCm)?.let { bmiAssessment(it, age) }

    private data class FatBand(val low: Double, val normalHigh: Double, val overweightHigh: Double, val label: String)

    private fun fatBand(sex: Sex, age: Int): FatBand? = when (sex) {
        Sex.MALE -> when {
            age < 40 -> FatBand(8.0, 19.0, 24.0, "男性 18–39 歲")
            age < 60 -> FatBand(11.0, 21.0, 26.0, "男性 40–59 歲")
            else -> FatBand(13.0, 24.0, 29.0, "男性 60 歲以上")
        }
        Sex.FEMALE -> when {
            age < 40 -> FatBand(21.0, 32.0, 37.0, "女性 18–39 歲")
            age < 60 -> FatBand(23.0, 33.0, 38.0, "女性 40–59 歲")
            else -> FatBand(24.0, 35.0, 40.0, "女性 60 歲以上")
        }
        Sex.UNSPECIFIED -> null
    }

    private data class GlucoseBand(val low: Double, val normalHigh: Double, val prediabetesHigh: Double, val label: String)

    private fun glucoseBand(context: MeasureContext?): GlucoseBand = when (context) {
        MeasureContext.FASTING, MeasureContext.BEFORE_MEAL -> GlucoseBand(70.0, 99.0, 125.0, "空腹／餐前")
        MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL -> GlucoseBand(70.0, 139.0, 199.0, "餐後")
        else -> GlucoseBand(70.0, 139.0, 199.0, "隨機／其他")
    }

    private fun fmt(v: Double) = if (v % 1.0 == 0.0) v.toInt().toString() else "%.1f".format(v)
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\MeasureTime.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import java.time.Instant
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZoneOffset

/** 測量時間的換算（日期選擇器回傳的是 UTC 午夜毫秒，要換回當地日期再接上原本的時分）。 */
object MeasureTime {
    /** 允許比現在晚一點點（手機時鐘與血糖機時鐘的誤差），超過就視為輸入錯誤。 */
    const val FUTURE_TOLERANCE_MS = 5 * 60_000L

    fun isFuture(measuredAt: Long, now: Long): Boolean = measuredAt > now + FUTURE_TOLERANCE_MS

    /** 當地日期的 UTC 午夜毫秒，給 Material3 DatePicker 當初始選取值。 */
    fun utcMidnightOfLocalDate(measuredAt: Long, zone: ZoneId): Long =
        Instant.ofEpochMilli(measuredAt).atZone(zone).toLocalDate().atStartOfDay(ZoneOffset.UTC).toInstant().toEpochMilli()

    /** 換成選到的日期（DatePicker 回傳 UTC 午夜毫秒），保留原本的時、分。 */
    fun withDate(measuredAt: Long, pickedUtcMidnightMillis: Long, zone: ZoneId): Long {
        val picked: LocalDate = Instant.ofEpochMilli(pickedUtcMidnightMillis).atZone(ZoneOffset.UTC).toLocalDate()
        val time = Instant.ofEpochMilli(measuredAt).atZone(zone).toLocalTime().withSecond(0).withNano(0)
        return picked.atTime(time).atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
    }

    /** 換成選到的時、分，保留原本的日期。 */
    fun withTime(measuredAt: Long, hour: Int, minute: Int, zone: ZoneId): Long {
        val date = Instant.ofEpochMilli(measuredAt).atZone(zone).toLocalDate()
        return date.atTime(hour, minute).atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\OverviewMath.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.ceil
import kotlin.math.floor
import kotlin.math.log10
import kotlin.math.pow

/** 總覽畫面用的純計算：最新值、近 N 天資料、統計、圖表刻度、「多久以前」。 */
object OverviewMath {
    private const val DAY_MS = 86_400_000L

    data class Scale(val min: Double, val max: Double, val ticks: List<Double>)
    data class Stats(val count: Int, val avg: Double, val min: Double, val max: Double)

    fun latest(records: List<HealthRecord>, type: RecordType): HealthRecord? =
        records.filter { it.type == type && it.deletedAt == null }.maxByOrNull { it.measuredAt }

    /** 近 days 天（含今天）、依時間由舊到新。不補任何資料；未來太遠的時間（輸入錯誤）不納入。 */
    fun window(records: List<HealthRecord>, type: RecordType, days: Int, now: Long): List<HealthRecord> {
        val from = now - days * DAY_MS
        return records
            .filter { it.type == type && it.deletedAt == null && it.measuredAt in from..(now + MeasureTime.FUTURE_TOLERANCE_MS) }
            .sortedBy { it.measuredAt }
    }

    /** 只留下指定測量情境的紀錄；ctx 為 null 表示全部。 */
    fun byContext(list: List<HealthRecord>, ctx: com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext?): List<HealthRecord> =
        if (ctx == null) list else list.filter { it.context == ctx }

    data class RangeStat(val inRange: Int, val total: Int) {
        val percent: Int get() = if (total == 0) 0 else Math.round(inRange * 100.0 / total).toInt()
    }

    /** 落在使用者自己設定的範圍（含上下限）內的筆數與比例。沒設定範圍或沒資料回傳 null。App 只統計，不判斷好壞。 */
    fun inRange(list: List<HealthRecord>, low: Double?, high: Double?): RangeStat? {
        if (low == null || high == null || list.isEmpty()) return null
        return RangeStat(list.count { it.value in low..high }, list.size)
    }

    fun stats(list: List<HealthRecord>): Stats? {
        if (list.isEmpty()) return null
        val v = list.map { it.value }
        return Stats(list.size, v.average(), v.min(), v.max())
    }

    /** 1／2／5 × 10^n 的漂亮刻度（與網站圖表同一套規則）。 */
    fun niceScale(min: Double, max: Double, count: Int = 5): Scale {
        if (!min.isFinite() || !max.isFinite()) return Scale(0.0, 1.0, listOf(0.0, 1.0))
        var lo = min
        var hi = max
        if (lo == hi) { lo -= 1; hi += 1 }
        val rough = (hi - lo) / maxOf(1, count - 1)
        val pow = 10.0.pow(floor(log10(rough)))
        val frac = rough / pow
        val step = (if (frac <= 1) 1.0 else if (frac <= 2) 2.0 else if (frac <= 5) 5.0 else 10.0) * pow
        val start = floor(lo / step) * step
        val end = ceil(hi / step) * step
        val ticks = ArrayList<Double>()
        var t = start
        while (t <= end + step / 2) {
            ticks += Math.round(t * 1e6) / 1e6
            t += step
        }
        return Scale(ticks.first(), ticks.last(), ticks)
    }

    fun relativeAge(measuredAt: Long, now: Long): String {
        val diff = now - measuredAt
        return when {
            diff < 60_000 -> "剛剛"
            diff < 3_600_000 -> "${diff / 60_000} 分鐘前"
            diff < DAY_MS -> "${diff / 3_600_000} 小時前"
            else -> "${diff / DAY_MS} 天前"
        }
    }

    /** 兩個數值是否「差不多」（測試用的小工具，避免浮點誤差）。 */
    internal fun close(a: Double, b: Double) = abs(a - b) < 1e-9
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\RecordValidator.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType

/** 輸入範圍檢查（防手滑，例如把 70.5 打成 705）。回傳 null 表示通過，否則為錯誤訊息。 */
object RecordValidator {
    fun validate(type: RecordType, value: Double?, value2: Double?): String? {
        if (value == null || value.isNaN()) return "請輸入數值"
        return when (type) {
            RecordType.WEIGHT -> range("體重", value, 20.0, 300.0, "kg")
            RecordType.BODY_FAT -> range("體脂率", value, 2.0, 70.0, "%")
            RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE -> range("血糖", value, 20.0, 800.0, "mg/dL")
            RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE -> {
                if (value2 == null || value2.isNaN()) return "請輸入舒張壓"
                range("收縮壓", value, 50.0, 260.0, "mmHg")
                    ?: range("舒張壓", value2, 30.0, 160.0, "mmHg")
                    ?: if (value2 >= value) "舒張壓不應大於或等於收縮壓" else null
            }
        }
    }

    /** 目標範圍（mg/dL）：兩個都不填＝不設定；要填就兩個一起填，且下限小於上限。回傳 null 表示通過。 */
    fun validateGlucoseRange(low: Double?, high: Double?): String? {
        if (low == null && high == null) return null
        if (low == null || high == null) return "目標範圍請同時填下限與上限"
        if (low < 20.0 || high > 800.0) return "目標範圍請填 20–800 mg/dL"
        if (low >= high) return "下限必須小於上限"
        return null
    }

    /** 測量時間不能在未來（超過容許誤差）。回傳 null 表示通過。 */
    fun validateTime(measuredAt: Long, now: Long): String? =
        if (MeasureTime.isFuture(measuredAt, now)) "測量時間不能晚於現在" else null

    private fun range(name: String, v: Double, min: Double, max: Double, unit: String): String? =
        if (v < min || v > max) "$name 應介於 $min–$max $unit" else null
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\data\repository\ReportBuilder.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType

data class ContextRow(val label: String, val stats: OverviewMath.Stats)
data class OtherRow(val label: String, val count: Int, val latest: String, val average: String)
data class RangeInfo(val low: Double, val high: Double, val stat: OverviewMath.RangeStat)

/** 就醫摘要的內容。只整理使用者自己記錄的數值，不含任何判斷或建議。 */
data class ReportData(
    val days: Int,
    val from: Long,
    val to: Long,
    val name: String,
    val glucose: List<HealthRecord>, // 由舊到新，最多 MAX_ROWS 筆（超過時留下最新的）
    val glucoseTotal: Int,
    val overall: OverviewMath.Stats?,
    val contexts: List<ContextRow>,
    val range: RangeInfo?,
    val others: List<OtherRow>,
)

object ReportBuilder {
    const val MAX_ROWS = 600
    private const val DAY_MS = 86_400_000L

    fun build(all: List<HealthRecord>, name: String, low: Double?, high: Double?, days: Int, now: Long): ReportData {
        val from = now - days * DAY_MS
        val glucoseAll = OverviewMath.window(all, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, days, now)
        val glucose = if (glucoseAll.size > MAX_ROWS) glucoseAll.takeLast(MAX_ROWS) else glucoseAll

        val contexts = buildList {
            MeasureContext.entries.forEach { c ->
                OverviewMath.stats(glucoseAll.filter { it.context == c })?.let { add(ContextRow(c.label, it)) }
            }
            OverviewMath.stats(glucoseAll.filter { it.context == null })?.let { add(ContextRow("未標示", it)) }
        }
        val range = OverviewMath.inRange(glucoseAll, low, high)?.let { RangeInfo(low!!, high!!, it) }

        val others = listOf(RecordType.WEIGHT, RecordType.BODY_FAT, RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE).mapNotNull { t ->
            val w = OverviewMath.window(all, t, days, now)
            if (w.isEmpty()) return@mapNotNull null
            val avg = w.map { it.value }.average()
            val avg2 = w.mapNotNull { it.value2 }.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.average()
            OtherRow(
                label = t.label,
                count = w.size,
                latest = display(w.last()),
                average = if (t == RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE && avg2 != null) "${Math.round(avg)}/${Math.round(avg2)} ${t.unit}" else "${"%.1f".format(avg)} ${t.unit}",
            )
        }
        return ReportData(days, from, now, name.trim(), glucose, glucoseAll.size, OverviewMath.stats(glucoseAll), contexts, range, others)
    }

    internal fun display(r: HealthRecord): String = when (r.type) {
        RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE -> "${r.value.toInt()}/${r.value2?.toInt()} ${r.type.unit}"
        RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE -> "${r.value.toInt()} ${r.type.unit}"
        else -> "${"%.1f".format(r.value)} ${r.type.unit}"
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\importer\CsvImporter.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.importer

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.RecordValidator
import java.time.LocalDateTime
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.Locale
import java.util.UUID

data class CsvParseResult(val records: List<HealthRecord>, val errors: List<String>)

/** 確定性 id：同一筆資料不論匯入幾次都得到同一個 id，配合 insertIgnore 達成去重。 */
object ImportIds {
    fun fromHealthConnect(healthConnectRecordId: String): String =
        UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("hc:$healthConnectRecordId".toByteArray()).toString()

    fun fromMijia(type: RecordType, measuredAt: Long, value: Double): String =
        UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("mijia:${type.name}:$measuredAt:$value".toByteArray()).toString()

    fun fromCsvRow(type: RecordType, measuredAt: Long, value: Double, value2: Double?): String =
        UUID.nameUUIDFromBytes("csv:${type.name}:$measuredAt:$value:${value2 ?: ""}".toByteArray()).toString()
}

/**
 * 匯入 CSV（Excel 另存「CSV UTF-8」即可）。第一列必須是標題，欄位名稱不分大小寫、可中英文：
 * 時間（measured_at／時間／日期）、類型（type／類型／種類）、數值（value／數值）、
 * 第二數值（value2／舒張壓）、備註（note／備註）。
 * 類型可寫 WEIGHT／體重、BODY_FAT／體脂率、BLOOD_GLUCOSE／血糖、BLOOD_PRESSURE／血壓。
 * 時間可寫 2026-10-19 09:27、2026/10/19 09:27、2026-10-19T09:27:00 或毫秒 epoch；只有日期則視為當天 00:00。
 */
object CsvImporter {
    private val timeNames = setOf("measured_at", "time", "時間", "日期", "量測時間")
    private val typeNames = setOf("type", "類型", "種類")
    private val valueNames = setOf("value", "數值", "值")
    private val value2Names = setOf("value2", "舒張壓", "數值2")
    private val noteNames = setOf("note", "備註")
    private val contextNames = setOf("context", "情境", "測量情境")

    private val contextAliases: Map<String, MeasureContext> = buildMap {
        MeasureContext.entries.forEach { put(it.name.lowercase(Locale.ROOT), it); put(it.label, it) }
        put("餐後2小時", MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL)
        put("飯後", MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL)
        put("飯前", MeasureContext.BEFORE_MEAL)
    }

    private val typeAliases: Map<String, RecordType> = buildMap {
        RecordType.entries.forEach { put(it.name.lowercase(Locale.ROOT), it); put(it.label, it) }
        put("體脂", RecordType.BODY_FAT)
        put("body fat", RecordType.BODY_FAT)
        put("weight", RecordType.WEIGHT)
    }

    private val formatters = listOf(
        "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss", "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm:ss", "yyyy/MM/dd HH:mm",
        "yyyy-M-d H:mm", "yyyy/M/d H:mm", "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm:ss", "yyyy-MM-dd'T'HH:mm",
    ).map { DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern(it) }

    fun parse(
        text: String,
        zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault(),
        now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    ): CsvParseResult {
        val rows = splitRows(text.removePrefix("﻿"))
        if (rows.isEmpty()) return CsvParseResult(emptyList(), listOf("檔案是空的"))
        val header = rows.first().map { it.trim().lowercase(Locale.ROOT) }
        fun col(names: Set<String>) = header.indexOfFirst { it in names }
        val iTime = col(timeNames)
        val iType = col(typeNames)
        val iValue = col(valueNames)
        if (iTime < 0 || iType < 0 || iValue < 0) {
            return CsvParseResult(emptyList(), listOf("標題列缺少必要欄位：時間、類型、數值（第一列必須是標題）"))
        }
        val iValue2 = col(value2Names)
        val iNote = col(noteNames)
        val iContext = col(contextNames)

        val records = ArrayList<HealthRecord>()
        val errors = ArrayList<String>()
        rows.drop(1).forEachIndexed { index, cells ->
            val line = index + 2
            if (cells.all { it.isBlank() }) return@forEachIndexed
            fun cell(i: Int) = if (i in cells.indices) cells[i].trim() else ""
            val type = typeAliases[cell(iType).lowercase(Locale.ROOT)]
            val measuredAt = parseTime(cell(iTime), zone)
            val value = cell(iValue).toDoubleOrNull()
            val value2 = if (iValue2 >= 0) cell(iValue2).toDoubleOrNull() else null
            val rawContext = if (iContext >= 0) cell(iContext) else ""
            val context = if (type == RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE && rawContext.isNotBlank()) contextAliases[rawContext.lowercase(Locale.ROOT)] else null
            val problem = when {
                type == null -> "類型「${cell(iType)}」看不懂"
                measuredAt == null -> "時間「${cell(iTime)}」格式不對"
                type == RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE && rawContext.isNotBlank() && context == null -> "情境「$rawContext」看不懂（可填空腹、餐前、餐後、睡前）"
                else -> RecordValidator.validate(type, value, value2)
            }
            if (problem != null) {
                if (errors.size < MAX_ERRORS) errors += "第 $line 列：$problem"
                return@forEachIndexed
            }
            type!!; measuredAt!!; value!!
            val v2 = if (type == RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE) value2 else null
            records += HealthRecord(
                id = ImportIds.fromCsvRow(type, measuredAt, value, v2),
                type = type, value = value, value2 = v2, measuredAt = measuredAt,
                source = RecordSource.IMPORT, note = if (iNote >= 0) cell(iNote) else "",
                syncState = SyncState.PENDING, createdAt = now, updatedAt = now, context = context,
            )
        }
        return CsvParseResult(records, errors)
    }

    internal fun parseTime(s: String, zone: ZoneId): Long? {
        if (s.isEmpty()) return null
        s.toLongOrNull()?.let { return if (s.length >= 12) it else null } // 毫秒 epoch；位數太少的純數字不接受
        for (f in formatters) {
            runCatching { return LocalDateTime.parse(s, f).atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli() }
        }
        runCatching {
            return java.time.LocalDate.parse(s.replace('/', '-').let(::padDate)).atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun padDate(s: String): String {
        val p = s.split('-')
        return if (p.size == 3) "%s-%02d-%02d".format(p[0], p[1].toIntOrNull() ?: 0, p[2].toIntOrNull() ?: 0) else s
    }

    /** 最小可用的 CSV 切列：支援雙引號、引號內逗號與換行、"" 轉義。 */
    internal fun splitRows(text: String): List<List<String>> {
        val rows = ArrayList<List<String>>()
        var cells = ArrayList<String>()
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        var inQuotes = false
        var i = 0
        fun endCell() { cells.add(sb.toString()); sb.setLength(0) }
        fun endRow() { endCell(); if (!(cells.size == 1 && cells[0].isEmpty())) rows.add(cells); cells = ArrayList() }
        while (i < text.length) {
            val c = text[i]
            when {
                inQuotes && c == '"' && i + 1 < text.length && text[i + 1] == '"' -> { sb.append('"'); i++ }
                c == '"' -> inQuotes = !inQuotes
                !inQuotes && c == ',' -> endCell()
                !inQuotes && c == '\n' -> endRow()
                !inQuotes && c == '\r' -> {}
                else -> sb.append(c)
            }
            i++
        }
        if (sb.isNotEmpty() || cells.isNotEmpty()) endRow()
        return rows
    }

    private const val MAX_ERRORS = 20
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\importer\HealthConnectImporter.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.importer

import android.content.Context
import androidx.health.connect.client.HealthConnectClient
import androidx.health.connect.client.permission.HealthPermission
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.BodyFatRecord
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.WeightRecord
import androidx.health.connect.client.request.ReadRecordsRequest
import androidx.health.connect.client.time.TimeRangeFilter
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.RecordValidator
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit

/** 一筆從 Health Connect 讀到的資料（還沒轉成本機紀錄），保留來源 App 套件名以便使用者挑選。 */
data class HcSample(
    val hcId: String,
    val type: RecordType,
    val value: Double,
    val measuredAt: Long,
    val originPackage: String,
)

object HealthConnectMapping {
    /** 轉成本機紀錄；數值不合理（例如單位錯誤造成的離譜值）回傳 null，不匯入。 */
    fun toRecord(s: HcSample, now: Long): HealthRecord? {
        if (RecordValidator.validate(s.type, s.value, null) != null) return null
        return HealthRecord(
            id = ImportIds.fromHealthConnect(s.hcId),
            type = s.type,
            value = s.value,
            measuredAt = s.measuredAt,
            source = RecordSource.HEALTH_CONNECT,
            note = "來源：${s.originPackage}",
            syncState = SyncState.PENDING,
            createdAt = now,
            updatedAt = now,
        )
    }

    fun countByOrigin(samples: List<HcSample>): Map<String, Int> =
        samples.groupingBy { it.originPackage }.eachCount().toSortedMap()
}

/**
 * 從 Health Connect 讀體重與體脂率。
 * 先「掃描」列出實際有資料的來源 App，由使用者挑要匯入哪些，避免重複來源（例如多個 App 都寫入同一筆體重）。
 */
class HealthConnectImporter(private val context: Context) {
    companion object {
        val PERMISSIONS: Set<String> = setOf(
            HealthPermission.getReadPermission(WeightRecord::class),
            HealthPermission.getReadPermission(BodyFatRecord::class),
        )
        private const val LOOKBACK_DAYS = 365L
        private const val PAGE_SIZE = 1000
        private const val MAX_PAGES = 100
    }

    fun isAvailable(): Boolean =
        runCatching { HealthConnectClient.getSdkStatus(context) == HealthConnectClient.SDK_AVAILABLE }.getOrDefault(false)

    suspend fun grantedPermissions(): Set<String> = runCatching {
        HealthConnectClient.getOrCreate(context).permissionController.getGrantedPermissions()
    }.getOrDefault(emptySet())

    /** 還缺哪些權限；只要求缺少的，避免已授權體重卻被體脂率卡住、每次都重跳授權。 */
    suspend fun missingPermissions(): Set<String> = PERMISSIONS - grantedPermissions()

    suspend fun hasPermissions(): Boolean = runCatching { missingPermissions().isEmpty() }.getOrDefault(false)

    /**
     * 讀體重與體脂率。只讀「已授權」的類型（允許只授權其中一種），
     * 分頁有上限，避免 pageToken 異常時無限迴圈；結果依時間新到舊排序。
     */
    suspend fun scan(now: Instant = Instant.now()): List<HcSample> {
        val client = HealthConnectClient.getOrCreate(context)
        val granted = grantedPermissions()
        val filter = TimeRangeFilter.between(now.minus(LOOKBACK_DAYS, ChronoUnit.DAYS), now)
        val out = ArrayList<HcSample>()

        if (HealthPermission.getReadPermission(WeightRecord::class) in granted) {
            readAll(out, MAX_PAGES) { token ->
                client.readRecords(ReadRecordsRequest(WeightRecord::class, filter, pageSize = PAGE_SIZE, pageToken = token))
                    .also { resp ->
                        resp.records.mapTo(out) {
                            HcSample(it.metadata.id, RecordType.WEIGHT, it.weight.inKilograms, it.time.toEpochMilli(), it.metadata.dataOrigin.packageName)
                        }
                    }.pageToken
            }
        }
        if (HealthPermission.getReadPermission(BodyFatRecord::class) in granted) {
            readAll(out, MAX_PAGES) { token ->
                client.readRecords(ReadRecordsRequest(BodyFatRecord::class, filter, pageSize = PAGE_SIZE, pageToken = token))
                    .also { resp ->
                        resp.records.mapTo(out) {
                            HcSample(it.metadata.id, RecordType.BODY_FAT, it.percentage.value, it.time.toEpochMilli(), it.metadata.dataOrigin.packageName)
                        }
                    }.pageToken
            }
        }
        return out.distinctBy { it.hcId }.sortedByDescending { it.measuredAt }
    }

    private inline fun readAll(out: MutableList<HcSample>, maxPages: Int, read: (String?) -> String?) {
        var token: String? = null
        var pages = 0
        do {
            token = read(token)?.takeIf { it.isNotBlank() }
            pages++
        } while (token != null && pages < maxPages)
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\importer\PhotoBatchImporter.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.importer

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.MediaStore
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.AddOutcome
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.AttachOutcome
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.HealthRecordRepository
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.PhotoOcr
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.PhotoStore
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

data class BatchResult(
    val saved: Int = 0,
    /** 同一時間已有紀錄但沒有照片，這次把照片補上去。 */
    val attached: Int = 0,
    /** 已有相同數值、時間相近的紀錄（重複匯入同一批照片時會出現）。 */
    val duplicate: Int = 0,
    /** 照片裡認不出血糖數值。 */
    val noValue: Int = 0,
    /** 照片沒有拍攝時間，無法歸到正確日期，所以不寫入。 */
    val noTime: Int = 0,
    val failed: Int = 0,
) {
    val total get() = saved + attached + duplicate + noValue + noTime + failed

    fun summary(): String = buildString {
        append("共 $total 張：新增 $saved 筆")
        if (attached > 0) append("、補上照片 $attached 筆")
        if (duplicate > 0) append("、重複略過 $duplicate")
        if (noValue > 0) append("、認不出數值 $noValue")
        if (noTime > 0) append("、無拍攝時間 $noTime")
        if (failed > 0) append("、讀取失敗 $failed")
    }
}

/** 批次匯入血糖機照片：逐張 OCR，數值取最可信的一個，測量時間取照片的拍攝時間，先存在手機。 */
class PhotoBatchImporter(private val context: Context, private val records: HealthRecordRepository) {

    suspend fun run(uris: List<Uri>, onProgress: (done: Int, total: Int) -> Unit): BatchResult {
        var r = BatchResult()
        uris.forEachIndexed { i, uri ->
            onProgress(i, uris.size)
            r = try {
                one(uri, r)
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                r.copy(failed = r.failed + 1)
            }
        }
        onProgress(uris.size, uris.size)
        return r
    }

    private suspend fun one(uri: Uri, r: BatchResult): BatchResult {
        val file = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { PhotoStore.importFrom(context, uri) } ?: return r.copy(failed = r.failed + 1)
        var keep = false
        try {
            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
            // 縮圖會丟掉 EXIF，拍攝時間要先讀
            val taken = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { PhotoStore.captureTime(file) ?: mediaStoreTime(uri) }
            if (taken == null || taken > now) return r.copy(noTime = r.noTime + 1)
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { PhotoStore.compressInPlace(file) }
            // 同一時間已有紀錄但沒照片：直接把照片補上去（數值以紀錄為準，不重新辨識）
            when (records.attachPhotoIfNearby(taken, file.absolutePath, now)) {
                AttachOutcome.ATTACHED -> { keep = true; return r.copy(attached = r.attached + 1) }
                AttachOutcome.HAS_PHOTO -> return r.copy(duplicate = r.duplicate + 1)
                AttachOutcome.NONE -> {}
            }
            // 與新增紀錄用同一套辨識（含液晶七段數字讀取）。自動模式只收「讀到七段數字」或「數字旁有 mg/dL」的結果，
            // 避免把標籤、單據上的數字當成血糖。
            val best = PhotoOcr.candidates(file).firstOrNull { it.lineText == "七段數字" || it.score > 1.05 }
            val value = best?.value ?: return r.copy(noValue = r.noValue + 1)
            return when (records.addFromPhoto(value, taken, file.absolutePath, now)) {
                AddOutcome.ADDED -> { keep = true; r.copy(saved = r.saved + 1) }
                AddOutcome.DUPLICATE -> r.copy(duplicate = r.duplicate + 1)
                AddOutcome.INVALID -> r.copy(noValue = r.noValue + 1)
            }
        } finally {
            if (!keep) file.delete()
        }
    }

    /** 沒有 EXIF 時，退而求其次用系統媒體庫記的拍攝／修改時間。 */
    private fun mediaStoreTime(uri: Uri): Long? = runCatching {
        context.contentResolver.query(
            uri, arrayOf(MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATE_TAKEN, MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATE_MODIFIED), null, null, null,
        )?.use { c ->
            if (!c.moveToFirst()) return@use null
            val taken = if (c.isNull(0)) 0L else c.getLong(0)
            val modified = if (c.isNull(1)) 0L else c.getLong(1) * 1000
            (if (taken > 0) taken else modified).takeIf { it > 0 }
        }
    }.getOrNull()
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\mijia\MijiaAccessibilityService.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.mijia

import android.accessibilityservice.AccessibilityService
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityEvent
import android.view.accessibility.AccessibilityNodeInfo
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.HealthTrackerApp
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.SupervisorJob
import kotlinx.coroutines.cancel
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

/**
 * 使用者開啟米家「體脂計 S400」頁面時，讀取畫面上的體重與體脂率並存進本機。
 * 只監聽米家（見 res/xml/mijia_accessibility_config.xml），只解析體重／體脂率，不記錄其他畫面內容。
 * 必須由使用者自己到系統「無障礙」設定開啟；沒開啟就完全不運作。
 *
 * 成功率相關：
 * - 文字與 contentDescription 都會收集（米家有些版本把數字放在 contentDescription）。
 * - 先用嚴格解析；失敗再用寬鬆解析（沒有標題、沒有時間也能讀，時間用上次量測或現在推估）。
 * - 解析失敗時把畫面文字存到除錯區，設定頁可以查看；服務連線時也可從設定頁「立即讀取畫面」強制重讀。
 */
class MijiaAccessibilityService : AccessibilityService() {
    private val scope = CoroutineScope(SupervisorJob() + Dispatchers.IO)
    private var lastSignature: String? = null
    private var lastFailureSignature: String? = null

    override fun onServiceConnected() {
        super.onServiceConnected()
        MijiaCaptureStore.attach(this)
    }

    override fun onAccessibilityEvent(event: AccessibilityEvent?) {
        if (event?.packageName?.toString() != MIJIA_PACKAGE) return
        captureNow(force = false)
    }

    /** 解析目前米家畫面；回傳 true 表示這次有讀到一組體重／體脂率（不代表一定新增紀錄）。 */
    fun captureNow(force: Boolean): Boolean {
        val root = rootInActiveWindow ?: return false
        if (root.packageName?.toString() != MIJIA_PACKAGE) return false

        val texts = ArrayList<UiText>()
        collect(root, texts)
        if (texts.isEmpty()) return false

        val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
        val reading = MijiaScreenParser.parse(texts, now = now)
            ?: MijiaScreenParser.parseLoose(texts, now = now, fallbackTime = fallbackTime(now))
        if (reading == null) {
            if (MijiaScreenParser.looksLikeScale(texts)) publishFailure(texts, now)
            return false
        }
        if (reading.measuredAt > now + MAX_FUTURE_MS) return false

        val signature = "${reading.measuredAt}|${reading.weightKg}|${reading.bodyFatPercent}"
        if (!force && signature == lastSignature) return true
        lastSignature = signature

        val raw = rawLines(texts)
        val records = (application as HealthTrackerApp).records
        scope.launch {
            val result = records.importRecords(MijiaScreenParser.toRecords(reading))
            val event = MijiaCaptureStore.Event(
                seq = MijiaCaptureStore.nextSeq(),
                at = now,
                reading = reading,
                inserted = result.inserted,
                skipped = result.skipped,
                raw = raw,
            )
            MijiaCaptureStore.saveLast(this@MijiaAccessibilityService, event)
            MijiaCaptureStore.publish(event)
        }
        return true
    }

    /** 找不到時間時，用上次量測時間（2 小時內）推估，否則用現在。 */
    private fun fallbackTime(now: Long): Long {
        val last = MijiaCaptureStore.loadLast(this)?.measuredAt ?: return now
        return last.takeIf { now - it in 0..2 * 3_600_000L } ?: now
    }

    private fun publishFailure(texts: List<UiText>, now: Long) {
        val raw = rawLines(texts)
        val sig = raw.joinToString("|").take(600)
        if (sig == lastFailureSignature) return
        lastFailureSignature = sig
        MijiaCaptureStore.publish(
            MijiaCaptureStore.Event(
                seq = MijiaCaptureStore.nextSeq(),
                at = now,
                reading = null,
                raw = raw,
                message = "有看到米家體脂計頁面，但讀不到體重／體脂率。請停在量測結果頁再按「立即讀取畫面」。",
            ),
        )
    }

    private fun rawLines(texts: List<UiText>): List<String> =
        texts.asSequence().map { it.text.trim() }.filter { it.isNotEmpty() }.take(MAX_RAW_LINES)
            .map { it.take(80) }.toList()

    private fun collect(node: AccessibilityNodeInfo, out: MutableList<UiText>) {
        if (out.size >= MAX_NODES) return
        val rect = Rect()
        node.getBoundsInScreen(rect)
        val text = node.text?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        val desc = node.contentDescription?.toString()?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (text.isNotEmpty()) out += UiText(text, rect.left, rect.top, rect.right, rect.bottom)
        if (desc.isNotEmpty() && desc != text) out += UiText(desc, rect.left, rect.top, rect.right, rect.bottom)
        for (i in 0 until node.childCount) {
            val child = node.getChild(i) ?: continue
            collect(child, out)
        }
    }

    override fun onInterrupt() {}

    override fun onDestroy() {
        MijiaCaptureStore.detach(this)
        scope.cancel()
        super.onDestroy()
    }

    companion object {
        const val MIJIA_PACKAGE = "com.xiaomi.smarthome"
        const val PREFS = "mijia_capture"
        const val KEY_LAST = "last"
        private const val MAX_NODES = 600
        private const val MAX_RAW_LINES = 80
        private const val MAX_FUTURE_MS = 24 * 3_600_000L
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\mijia\MijiaCaptureStore.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.mijia

import android.content.Context
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.MutableStateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.StateFlow
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.asStateFlow
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong

/**
 * 米家畫面讀取的即時狀態。無障礙服務與 App 在同一個行程，所以用一個 process 內的 StateFlow
 * 讓設定頁可以即時看到「正在等畫面 → 讀到什麼 → 新增幾筆」，不必輪詢 SharedPreferences。
 * 最後一次結果仍會寫進 SharedPreferences，App 重開後還看得到。
 */
object MijiaCaptureStore {
    /** 一次畫面解析的結果；[reading] 為 null 表示這次有看到體脂計頁面但解析失敗。 */
    data class Event(
        val seq: Long,
        val at: Long,
        val reading: MijiaReading?,
        val inserted: Int = 0,
        val skipped: Int = 0,
        val raw: List<String> = emptyList(),
        val message: String? = null,
    )

    /** 最近一次成功讀取（從 SharedPreferences 還原）。 */
    data class Last(
        val readAt: Long,
        val measuredAt: Long,
        val weightKg: Double?,
        val bodyFatPercent: Double?,
        val inserted: Int,
        val skipped: Int,
        val timeEstimated: Boolean,
    )

    private val seq = AtomicLong(0)
    private val _event = MutableStateFlow<Event?>(null)
    val event: StateFlow<Event?> = _event.asStateFlow()

    @Volatile private var service: MijiaAccessibilityService? = null

    fun attach(service: MijiaAccessibilityService) {
        this.service = service
    }

    fun detach(service: MijiaAccessibilityService) {
        if (this.service === service) this.service = null
    }

    fun isServiceConnected(): Boolean = service != null

    /** 請服務立刻重新解析目前畫面（例如使用者剛從米家切回來）；服務沒開啟回傳 false。 */
    fun requestRefresh(): Boolean = service?.captureNow(force = true) ?: false

    fun publish(event: Event) {
        _event.value = event
    }

    fun nextSeq(): Long = seq.incrementAndGet()

    fun saveLast(context: Context, event: Event) {
        val r = event.reading ?: return
        context.getSharedPreferences(MijiaAccessibilityService.PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE).edit()
            .putString(
                MijiaAccessibilityService.KEY_LAST,
                "${event.at}|${r.measuredAt}|${r.weightKg ?: ""}|${r.bodyFatPercent ?: ""}|${event.inserted}|${event.skipped}|${if (r.timeEstimated) 1 else 0}",
            )
            .putString(KEY_RAW, event.raw.joinToString("\n").take(MAX_RAW_CHARS))
            .apply()
    }

    fun loadLast(context: Context): Last? {
        val raw = context.getSharedPreferences(MijiaAccessibilityService.PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getString(MijiaAccessibilityService.KEY_LAST, null) ?: return null
        val p = raw.split('|')
        if (p.size < 6) return null
        val readAt = p[0].toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        val measuredAt = p[1].toLongOrNull() ?: return null
        return Last(
            readAt = readAt,
            measuredAt = measuredAt,
            weightKg = p[2].takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toDoubleOrNull(),
            bodyFatPercent = p[3].takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.toDoubleOrNull(),
            inserted = p[4].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
            skipped = p[5].toIntOrNull() ?: 0,
            timeEstimated = p.getOrNull(6) == "1",
        )
    }

    fun loadRaw(context: Context): String? =
        context.getSharedPreferences(MijiaAccessibilityService.PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)
            .getString(KEY_RAW, null)

    private const val KEY_RAW = "last_raw"
    private const val MAX_RAW_CHARS = 6_000
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\mijia\MijiaLauncher.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.mijia

import android.app.ActivityOptions
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Rect
import android.os.Build

/**
 * 以「浮動視窗」開啟米家：指定較小的啟動範圍，支援自由視窗（freeform）的系統就會用小視窗開啟；
 * 不支援時系統會忽略範圍，仍以正常方式開啟米家（無障礙讀取不受影響）。
 */
object MijiaLauncher {
    fun openFloating(context: Context): Boolean {
        val intent = context.packageManager.getLaunchIntentForPackage(MijiaAccessibilityService.MIJIA_PACKAGE) ?: return false
        intent.addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_MULTIPLE_TASK)
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.N) {
            val dm = context.resources.displayMetrics
            val w = (dm.widthPixels * 0.66f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(320)
            val h = (dm.heightPixels * 0.72f).toInt().coerceAtLeast(480)
            val left = ((dm.widthPixels - w) / 2).coerceAtLeast(0)
            val top = (dm.heightPixels * 0.10f).toInt()
            val bounds = Rect(left, top, left + w, top + h)
            val options = ActivityOptions.makeBasic().apply { setLaunchBounds(bounds) }
            runCatching { context.startActivity(intent, options.toBundle()) }.onSuccess { return true }
        }
        return runCatching { context.startActivity(intent); true }.getOrDefault(false)
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\mijia\MijiaScreenParser.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.mijia

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.RecordValidator
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.importer.ImportIds
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.time.LocalDateTime
import java.time.ZoneId
import kotlin.math.abs
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/** 螢幕上一個有文字的元件與它的位置（像素）。 */
data class UiText(val text: String, val left: Int, val top: Int, val right: Int, val bottom: Int) {
    val height: Int get() = (bottom - top).coerceAtLeast(0)
    val area: Int get() = height * (right - left).coerceAtLeast(0)
}

data class MijiaReading(
    val measuredAt: Long,
    val weightKg: Double?,
    val bodyFatPercent: Double?,
    /** true = 畫面上找不到時間，改用讀取時間推估。 */
    val timeEstimated: Boolean = false,
)

/**
 * 從米家「Xiaomi 體脂計 S400」頁面的文字元件解析體重與體脂率。
 * 這頁是 React Native 外掛：體重整數與小數常分成兩個元件（"68" 與 ".4"），
 * 時間在體重正下方（"2026/10/19 06:33"），體脂率數字在「體脂率」標籤正上方。
 * 米家改版時最容易壞的就是這個檔案；解析不到就回傳 null，不會亂寫入資料。
 *
 * 為了降低漏讀率，另外提供 [parseLoose]：
 * - 標題不在無障礙畫面裡（常見）時，改用「體脂率／BMI＋肌肉量」判断這是體脂計頁面；
 * - 支援更多時間格式（-、.、今天／昨天、12 小時制、只有月日）；
 * - 找不到時間時，用 [fallbackTime] 推估（由呼叫端帶入上次量測時間或現在）。
 */
object MijiaScreenParser {
    private val pageTitleRe = Regex("""體脂計|體脂秤|體重計|Body\s*Composition|Body\s*Fat\s*Scale""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val fatLabelRe = Regex("""^(體脂率|體脂肪率|體脂|Body\s*fat(\s*(rate|percentage))?|Fat\s*%|Body\s*fat\s*%)$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val bmiLabelRe = Regex("""^(BMI|身體質量指數)$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val muscleLabelRe = Regex("""^(肌肉量|骨骼肌|Muscle(\s*mass)?|Skeletal\s*muscle)$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)

    private val wholeRe = Regex("""^(\d{1,3})\.(\d{1,2})\s*(?:kg|公斤)?$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val intRe = Regex("""^(\d{1,3})\s*(?:kg|公斤)?$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)
    private val decRe = Regex("""^\.(\d{1,2})$""")
    private val fatValueRe = Regex("""^(\d{1,2})(?:\.(\d))?\s*%?$""")
    private const val MAX_FUTURE_MS = 24 * 3_600_000L

    /** 嚴格解析：必須看得到體脂計頁面標題（保留給舊行為與測試）。 */
    fun parse(raw: List<UiText>, zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault(), now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): MijiaReading? =
        parseInternal(raw, zone, now, allowMissingTime = false, fallbackTime = now)

    /**
     * 寬鬆解析（給無障礙服務用）：標題看不到也能讀，沒有時間時用 [fallbackTime]。
     * 仍要求畫面有體脂計的明確特徵，避免在其他米家頁面亂抓數字。
     */
    fun parseLoose(
        raw: List<UiText>,
        zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault(),
        now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
        fallbackTime: Long = now,
    ): MijiaReading? = parseInternal(raw, zone, now, allowMissingTime = true, fallbackTime = fallbackTime)

    /** 畫面上是否像體脂計頁面（標題、體脂率標籤，或 BMI＋肌肉量同時出現）。 */
    fun looksLikeScale(raw: List<UiText>): Boolean {
        val texts = normalized(raw)
        if (texts.isEmpty()) return false
        if (texts.any { pageTitleRe.containsMatchIn(it.text) }) return true
        if (texts.any { fatLabelRe.matches(it.text) }) return true
        return texts.any { bmiLabelRe.matches(it.text) } && texts.any { muscleLabelRe.matches(it.text) }
    }

    private fun parseInternal(raw: List<UiText>, zone: ZoneId, now: Long, allowMissingTime: Boolean, fallbackTime: Long): MijiaReading? {
        val texts = normalized(raw)
        if (texts.isEmpty()) return null
        val titled = texts.any { pageTitleRe.containsMatchIn(it.text) }
        if (!titled) {
            if (!allowMissingTime) return null
            if (!looksLikeScale(texts)) return null
        }

        val times = texts.mapNotNull { t -> parseTimeMillis(t.text, zone, now)?.takeIf { it <= now + MAX_FUTURE_MS }?.let { t to it } }
        val paired = readWeightWithTime(texts, times)
        // 有時間時只在時間上方找體重（避免把下方的 BMI／肌肉量當體重）；
        // 真的找不到才用「字體明顯最大」的數字當備援。
        val weight = paired?.let { it.first to it.second }
            ?: if (allowMissingTime) {
                if (times.isEmpty()) readWeightLoose(texts) else readWeightLooseIfDominant(texts)
            } else {
                null
            }
        val fat = readBodyFat(texts)
        if (weight == null && fat == null) return null

        val anchorTop = weight?.first?.top ?: fat?.first?.top ?: 0
        val nearestTime = times.minByOrNull { abs(it.first.top - anchorTop) }?.second
        val chosen = paired?.third ?: nearestTime
        if (chosen == null && !allowMissingTime) return null
        val at = chosen ?: fallbackTime

        val w = weight?.second
        val f = fat?.second
        if (w == null && f == null) return null
        return MijiaReading(at, w, f, timeEstimated = chosen == null)
    }

    /** 找出體重與它對應的時間；回傳 (體重節點, 體重, 量測時間)。 */
    private fun readWeightWithTime(texts: List<UiText>, times: List<Pair<UiText, Long>>): Triple<UiText, Double, Long>? {
        var best: Triple<UiText, Double, Long>? = null
        for ((timeNode, ms) in times) {
            val pair = readWeightAbove(texts, timeNode) ?: continue
            val current = best
            if (current == null || pair.first.height > current.first.height) best = Triple(pair.first, pair.second, ms)
        }
        return best
    }

    /** 時間文字上方的體重；S400 的體重整數框會延伸到時間文字的高度，所以只要求「從時間上方開始」。 */
    private fun readWeightAbove(texts: List<UiText>, timeNode: UiText): Pair<UiText, Double>? {
        val above = texts.filter { it.top < timeNode.top }
        return combineSplit(above) ?: bestWhole(above) ?: bestInt(above)
    }

    /**
     * 有時間但體重不在時間上方時的備援：只接受字體明顯最大的數字。
     * 體重字級遠大於 BMI／體脂／肌肉量；若最大的兩個數字差不多大，就視為不可靠、不猜。
     */
    private fun readWeightLooseIfDominant(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? {
        val candidates = listOfNotNull(combineSplit(texts), bestWhole(texts), bestInt(texts))
            .distinctBy { it.first.text to it.first.top }
            .sortedByDescending { it.first.height }
        val top = candidates.firstOrNull() ?: return null
        val second = candidates.getOrNull(1)
        if (second != null && top.first.height < second.first.height * 1.35) return null
        return top
    }

    /** 沒有時間可定位時，用字體最大的數字當體重（體重數字遠大於 BMI／體脂／肌肉量）。 */
    private fun readWeightLoose(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? {
        combineSplit(texts)?.let { split ->
            val whole = bestWhole(texts)
            val int = bestInt(texts)
            val tallest = listOfNotNull(split, whole, int).maxByOrNull { it.first.height }
            if (tallest != null) return tallest
        }
        val whole = bestWhole(texts)
        val int = bestInt(texts)
        return when {
            whole != null && (int == null || whole.first.height >= int.first.height) -> whole
            int != null -> int
            else -> null
        }
    }

    /** 整數與小數分開的體重（"68" + ".4"）。小數取字體最大的，整數取與它垂直重疊且在最左邊的。 */
    private fun combineSplit(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? {
        val dec = texts.filter { decRe.matches(it.text) }.maxByOrNull { it.height } ?: return null
        val int = texts.filter {
            intRe.matches(it.text) && it.top < dec.bottom && it.bottom > dec.top && it.left < dec.left
        }.maxByOrNull { it.height } ?: return null
        val value = (int.text + dec.text).toDoubleOrNull() ?: return null
        return int to value
    }

    private fun bestWhole(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? =
        texts.mapNotNull { t ->
            wholeRe.matchEntire(t.text)?.let { m ->
                val v = "${m.groupValues[1]}.${m.groupValues[2]}".toDoubleOrNull()
                if (v != null) t to v else null
            }
        }.maxByOrNull { it.first.height }

    private fun bestInt(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? =
        texts.filter { intRe.matches(it.text) }
            .mapNotNull { t -> t.text.replace(Regex("""\s*(?:kg|公斤)$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE), "").toDoubleOrNull()?.let { t to it } }
            .maxByOrNull { it.first.height }

    /** 體脂率：找「體脂率」標籤附近的數字，避免把 BMI／肌肉量當成體脂率。 */
    private fun readBodyFat(texts: List<UiText>): Pair<UiText, Double>? {
        val labels = texts.filter { fatLabelRe.matches(it.text) }
        if (labels.isEmpty()) return null
        val values = texts.mapNotNull { t ->
            val m = fatValueRe.matchEntire(t.text) ?: return@mapNotNull null
            val v = buildString {
                append(m.groupValues[1])
                m.groupValues[2].takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }?.let { append('.').append(it) }
            }.toDoubleOrNull() ?: return@mapNotNull null
            if (v !in 2.0..70.0) return@mapNotNull null
            t to v
        }
        var best: Pair<UiText, Double>? = null
        var bestScore = Double.MAX_VALUE
        for (label in labels) {
            for ((node, v) in values) {
                val gapX = when {
                    node.right < label.left -> label.left - node.right
                    node.left > label.right -> node.left - label.right
                    else -> 0
                }
                val gapY = when {
                    node.bottom < label.top -> label.top - node.bottom
                    node.top > label.bottom -> node.top - label.bottom
                    else -> 0
                }
                // 必須在標籤附近；分數越小越可能是同一組數字。
                // 水平容差要小：S400 的 BMI／肌肉量數值可能在隔壁 30–40px，容差太大會被誤認成體脂率。
                val allowedY = max(label.height, node.height) * 1.5 + 24
                if (gapX > 16 || gapY > allowedY) continue
                val score = gapX + gapY * 2.0
                if (score < bestScore) {
                    bestScore = score
                    best = node to v
                }
            }
        }
        return best
    }

    private fun normalized(raw: List<UiText>): List<UiText> {
        val out = ArrayList<UiText>(raw.size)
        for (t in raw) {
            val s = normalize(t.text)
            if (s.isNotEmpty()) out += t.copy(text = s)
        }
        return out
    }

    /** 全形數字／標點與單位空白正規化。 */
    private fun normalize(input: String): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder(input.length)
        for (ch in input.trim()) {
            when (ch) {
                in '０'..'９' -> sb.append(('0'.code + (ch.code - '０'.code)).toChar())
                '．', '，', '、' -> sb.append('.')
                '％' -> sb.append('%')
                '　', '\u00A0' -> sb.append(' ')
                else -> sb.append(ch)
            }
        }
        return sb.toString().replace(Regex("""\s+"""), " ").trim()
    }

    /** 支援多種米家版本的時間格式；解析成功回傳 epoch millis。 */
    internal fun parseTimeMillis(text: String, zone: ZoneId, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): Long? {
        val t = normalize(text)
        Regex("""^(\d{4})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})[ T]+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?$""")
            .matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
                val (y, mo, d, h, mi) = m.groupValues.drop(1).take(5).map { it.toInt() }
                val sec = m.groupValues.getOrNull(6)?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
                return localMillis(y, mo, d, h, mi, sec, zone)
            }
        Regex("""^(\d{1,2})[/\-.](\d{1,2})\s+(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$""").matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
            val (mo, d, h, mi) = m.groupValues.drop(1).map { it.toInt() }
            return noYearMillis(mo, d, h, mi, zone, now)
        }
        Regex("""^(今天|今日|昨天|昨日)\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$""").matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
            val days = if (m.groupValues[1].startsWith("昨")) -1L else 0L
            val (h, mi) = m.groupValues.drop(2).map { it.toInt() }
            val day = Instant.ofEpochMilli(now).atZone(zone).toLocalDate().plusDays(days)
            return localMillis(day.year, day.monthValue, day.dayOfMonth, h, mi, 0, zone)
        }
        Regex("""^(\d{1,2})月(\d{1,2})日\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$""").matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
            val (mo, d, h, mi) = m.groupValues.drop(1).map { it.toInt() }
            return noYearMillis(mo, d, h, mi, zone, now)
        }
        Regex("""^(上午|早上|凌晨|下午|晚上|中午)\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$""").matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
            val (h, mi) = m.groupValues.drop(2).map { it.toInt() }
            val hh = when (m.groupValues[1]) {
                "下午", "晚上" -> if (h == 12) 12 else h + 12
                "中午" -> 12
                else -> if (h == 12) 0 else h
            }
            return todayMillis(hh, mi, zone, now)
        }
        Regex("""^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})(?::(\d{2}))?\s*(AM|PM)?$""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE).matchEntire(t)?.let { m ->
            var h = m.groupValues[1].toInt()
            val mi = m.groupValues[2].toInt()
            val sec = m.groupValues.getOrNull(3)?.toIntOrNull() ?: 0
            val ampm = m.groupValues.getOrNull(4).orEmpty().uppercase()
            if (ampm == "PM" && h != 12) h += 12
            if (ampm == "AM" && h == 12) h = 0
            return todayMillis(h, mi, zone, now, sec)
        }
        return null
    }

    private fun noYearMillis(month: Int, day: Int, hour: Int, minute: Int, zone: ZoneId, now: Long): Long? {
        val year = Instant.ofEpochMilli(now).atZone(zone).year
        var ms = localMillis(year, month, day, hour, minute, 0, zone) ?: return null
        // 只有月日時，若解析出的時間比現在晚超過一天，通常是去年的紀錄
        if (ms > now + MAX_FUTURE_MS) {
            ms = localMillis(year - 1, month, day, hour, minute, 0, zone) ?: return null
        }
        return ms
    }

    private fun todayMillis(hour: Int, minute: Int, zone: ZoneId, now: Long, second: Int = 0): Long? {
        val today = Instant.ofEpochMilli(now).atZone(zone).toLocalDate()
        var ms = localMillis(today.year, today.monthValue, today.dayOfMonth, hour, minute, second, zone) ?: return null
        if (ms > now + MAX_FUTURE_MS) {
            val yesterday = today.minusDays(1)
            ms = localMillis(yesterday.year, yesterday.monthValue, yesterday.dayOfMonth, hour, minute, second, zone) ?: return null
        }
        return ms
    }

    private fun localMillis(year: Int, month: Int, day: Int, hour: Int, minute: Int, second: Int, zone: ZoneId): Long? = runCatching {
        LocalDateTime.of(year, month, day, hour, minute, second).atZone(zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
    }.getOrNull()

    /** 轉成本機紀錄（數值不合理的略過）。id 由時間與數值決定，同一次量測重複擷取只會留一筆。 */
    fun toRecords(r: MijiaReading, now: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()): List<HealthRecord> {
        val note = if (r.timeEstimated) "來源：米家畫面（時間以讀取時間推估）" else "來源：米家畫面"
        fun make(type: RecordType, v: Double) = HealthRecord(
            id = ImportIds.fromMijia(type, r.measuredAt, v), type = type, value = v, measuredAt = r.measuredAt,
            source = RecordSource.MIJIA, note = note, syncState = SyncState.PENDING, createdAt = now, updatedAt = now,
        )
        return buildList {
            r.weightKg?.takeIf { RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.WEIGHT, it, null) == null }?.let { add(make(RecordType.WEIGHT, it)) }
            r.bodyFatPercent?.takeIf { RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BODY_FAT, it, null) == null }?.let { add(make(RecordType.BODY_FAT, it)) }
        }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\DigitLocator.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/**
 * 在整張血糖機照片裡找出「那一排七段數字」的位置，不需要文字辨識給線索。
 * 做法：取出深色線段（長條形的連通塊），找一組長度相近、上下兩層、左右連成一串的直條線段，
 * 它們的外框就是數字區。以 900 像素左右的縮圖計算（數字約 70 像素高）。
 */
object DigitLocator {
    class Roi(val x0: Int, val y0: Int, val x1: Int, val y1: Int, val ref: Int)

    private class Bar(val x: Int, val y: Int, val w: Int, val h: Int) {
        val cx get() = x + w / 2.0
        val cy get() = y + h / 2.0
        val vert get() = h >= w
    }

    private fun bars(gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int): List<Bar> {
        val blur = SevenSegmentReader.boxBlur(gray, w, h, 1)
        val bg = SevenSegmentReader.boxBlur(blur, w, h, 30)
        val sorted = blur.sorted()
        val spread = sorted[(sorted.size * 0.9).toInt()] - sorted[(sorted.size * 0.1).toInt()]
        val t = (spread * 0.18).toInt().coerceIn(12, 40)
        val mask = BooleanArray(w * h) { bg[it] - blur[it] > t }
        val out = ArrayList<Bar>()
        for (c in SevenSegmentReader.allComponents(mask, w, h)) {
            if (c.area < 40 || c.touchesEdge) continue
            val lo = max(c.bw, c.bh).toDouble()
            val sh = min(c.bw, c.bh).toDouble()
            if (lo / sh < 1.8 || lo / sh > 9) continue
            if (c.area.toDouble() / (c.bw * c.bh) < 0.45) continue // 斜條的外框填充率偏低，但至少要有這個程度
            out += Bar(c.x0, c.y0, c.bw, c.bh)
        }
        return out
    }

    /** 回傳數字區（含左右各多留一個數字寬、上下各半個線段長），座標在傳入影像的尺度；找不到回傳 null。 */
    fun locate(gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int): Roi? {
        val verts = bars(gray, w, h).filter { it.vert }
        if (verts.size < 3) return null
        var bestScore = -1.0
        var bestChain: List<Bar>? = null
        var bestRef = 0
        for (ref in verts.map { it.h }.distinct().sorted()) {
            val grp = verts.filter { it.h >= 0.82 * ref && it.h <= 1.22 * ref }
            if (grp.size < 3) continue
            // 墨最密的一段高度窗（上下兩層直條）
            var inw: List<Bar> = emptyList()
            for (y0 in grp.map { it.cy }.sorted()) {
                val cand = grp.filter { it.cy >= y0 - 0.2 * ref && it.cy <= y0 + 1.5 * ref }
                if (cand.size > inw.size) inw = cand
            }
            if (inw.size < 3) continue
            val mid = (inw.minOf { it.cy } + inw.maxOf { it.cy }) / 2
            if (inw.count { it.cy < mid - 0.15 * ref } < 1 || inw.count { it.cy > mid + 0.15 * ref } < 1) continue
            // 左右要連成一串（「1」靠格子右側，兩個相鄰的「1」相距約 1.7 倍線段長，所以間隔上限放到 2 倍）
            val sorted = inw.sortedBy { it.cx }
            val chains = ArrayList<MutableList<Bar>>().apply { add(mutableListOf(sorted[0])) }
            for (v in sorted.drop(1)) {
                if (v.cx - chains.last().last().cx <= 2.0 * ref) chains.last().add(v) else chains.add(mutableListOf(v))
            }
            val chain = chains.maxByOrNull { it.size }!!
            if (chain.size < 3) continue
            val score = chain.size * (1 + 0.02 * ref)
            if (score > bestScore) { bestScore = score; bestChain = chain; bestRef = ref }
        }
        val ch = bestChain ?: return null
        val xp = (1.5 * bestRef).toInt()
        val yp = (0.5 * bestRef).toInt()
        return Roi(
            max(0, ch.minOf { it.x } - xp), max(0, ch.minOf { it.y } - yp),
            min(w, ch.maxOf { it.x + it.w } + xp), min(h, ch.maxOf { it.y + it.h } + yp), bestRef,
        )
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\DigitSearch.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/**
 * 整張照片 → 血糖數字：先用 DigitLocator 找出數字區再讀；找不到或讀不出來，就掃上半部的 12 個重疊視窗，
 * 讀到的結果以「多數相同」為準（只有一個視窗讀到就採用它）。純影像運算，不依賴 Android，可直接單元測試。
 */
object DigitSearch {
    private const val WORK_SIDE = 900

    /** gray：0..255 灰階，w×h。回傳數字字串（2～3 位，且是合理血糖值）；讀不到回傳 null。 */
    fun search(gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int): String? {
        val scale = WORK_SIDE.toDouble() / max(w, h)
        val w9 = max(1, (w * scale).toInt())
        val h9 = max(1, (h * scale).toInt())
        val small = if (scale >= 1.0) gray else shrink(gray, w, h, w9, h9)
        val sw = if (scale >= 1.0) w else w9
        val sh = if (scale >= 1.0) h else h9
        val f = w.toDouble() / sw

        // A：先把數字區找出來，再用原解析度裁切來讀（準確度最高）
        DigitLocator.locate(small, sw, sh)?.let { r ->
            val x0 = (r.x0 * f).toInt().coerceIn(0, w - 2)
            val x1 = (r.x1 * f).toInt().coerceIn(x0 + 1, w)
            val y0 = ((r.y0 - r.ref) * f).toInt().coerceIn(0, h - 2)
            val y1 = ((r.y1 + r.ref) * f).toInt().coerceIn(y0 + 1, h)
            val roi = crop(gray, w, x0, y0, x1, y1)
            val read = SevenSegmentReader.read(roi, x1 - x0, y1 - y0)
            if (SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(read) != null) return read
            // 線段黏在一起時（光線不均、反光），改用「已知數字高度」模式再讀；高度約為直條長度的 2.35 倍
            for (mul in listOf(1.0, 0.9, 1.1)) {
                val hinted = SevenSegmentReader.read(roi, x1 - x0, y1 - y0, (r.ref * f * 2.35 * mul).toInt())
                if (SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(hinted) != null) return hinted
            }
        }

        // C：掃描上半部的重疊視窗，多數決
        val ww = (sw * 0.7).toInt()
        val wh = (sh * 0.42).toInt()
        val reads = ArrayList<String>()
        for (yi in 0..3) {
            val y0 = ((sh - wh) * yi / 3.0 * 0.75).toInt()
            for (xi in 0..2) {
                val x0 = ((sw - ww) * xi / 2.0).toInt()
                val read = SevenSegmentReader.read(crop(small, sw, x0, y0, x0 + ww, y0 + wh), ww, wh)
                if (SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(read) != null) reads += read!!
            }
        }
        if (reads.isEmpty()) return null
        val counts = reads.groupingBy { it }.eachCount()
        return reads.firstOrNull { counts.getValue(it) >= 2 } ?: reads.first()
    }

    private fun crop(src: IntArray, w: Int, x0: Int, y0: Int, x1: Int, y1: Int): IntArray {
        val cw = x1 - x0
        val ch = y1 - y0
        val out = IntArray(cw * ch)
        for (y in 0 until ch) System.arraycopy(src, (y0 + y) * w + x0, out, y * cw, cw)
        return out
    }

    /** 以區塊平均縮小。 */
    private fun shrink(src: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, nw: Int, nh: Int): IntArray {
        val out = IntArray(nw * nh)
        for (y in 0 until nh) {
            val ya = y * h / nh
            val yb = max(ya + 1, min(h, (y + 1) * h / nh))
            for (x in 0 until nw) {
                val xa = x * w / nw
                val xb = max(xa + 1, min(w, (x + 1) * w / nw))
                var sum = 0L
                for (yy in ya until yb) for (xx in xa until xb) sum += src[yy * w + xx]
                out[y * nw + x] = (sum / ((yb - ya) * (xb - xa))).toInt()
            }
        }
        return out
    }
}
