===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\GlucoseOcrParser.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

/** OCR 辨識出的一行文字與其高度（像素）。LCD 上最大的數字通常就是量測結果。 */
data class OcrLine(
    val text: String,
    val height: Int,
    /** 這一行在影像中的位置（像素）；沒有位置資訊時全為 0。 */
    val left: Int = 0, val top: Int = 0, val right: Int = 0, val bottom: Int = 0,
)

data class GlucoseCandidate(val value: Double, val score: Double, val lineText: String)

/**
 * 從血糖機照片的 OCR 結果挑出可能的血糖值（mg/dL）。
 * 七段顯示器辨識本來就不穩，所以只產生「候選」，最後一定由使用者確認。
 */
object GlucoseOcrParser {
    const val MIN_MGDL = 20.0
    const val MAX_MGDL = 800.0

    // 2～3 位整數；前後不能緊鄰數字或日期／時間分隔符（排除 10/19、09:27、2026、1.5 等）。
    private val number = Regex("""(?<![\d:/.\-])(\d{2,3})(?![\d:/.\-])""")
    private val unitHint = Regex("""mg\s*/?\s*d\s*l|mg/dl""", RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE)

    /** 七段顯示器常被讀成字母：在含數字的 2～3 字元片段內，把 O→0、I/l→1 修回來。 */
    internal fun fixDigitLookalikes(text: String): String =
        Regex("""[0-9OoIl]{2,3}""").replace(text) { m ->
            val t = m.value
            if (t.any { it.isDigit() }) t.replace('O', '0').replace('o', '0').replace('I', '1').replace('l', '1') else t
        }

    fun candidates(lines: List<OcrLine>): List<GlucoseCandidate> {
        if (lines.isEmpty()) return emptyList()
        val maxHeight = lines.maxOf { it.height }.coerceAtLeast(1)
        val best = LinkedHashMap<Double, GlucoseCandidate>()
        lines.forEachIndexed { i, line ->
            val text = fixDigitLookalikes(line.text)
            val nearUnit = listOf(i - 1, i, i + 1).any { j ->
                lines.getOrNull(j)?.let { unitHint.containsMatchIn(it.text) } == true
            }
            number.findAll(text).forEach { m ->
                val v = m.groupValues[1].toDouble()
                if (v < MIN_MGDL || v > MAX_MGDL) return@forEach
                val score = line.height.toDouble() / maxHeight + if (nearUnit) 0.5 else 0.0
                val prev = best[v]
                if (prev == null || score > prev.score) best[v] = GlucoseCandidate(v, score, line.text)
            }
        }
        return best.values.sortedByDescending { it.score }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\OcrEngine.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import com.google.mlkit.vision.common.InputImage
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.TextRecognition
import com.google.mlkit.vision.text.latin.TextRecognizerOptions
import kotlinx.coroutines.suspendCancellableCoroutine
import kotlin.coroutines.resume
import kotlin.coroutines.resumeWithException

/** 裝置端 OCR（ML Kit 內建模型，照片不離開手機）。 */
object OcrEngine {
    suspend fun recognize(context: Context, uri: Uri): List<OcrLine> = process(InputImage.fromFilePath(context, uri))

    /** 辨識記憶體中的圖（放大框選、增強對比後的影像）。 */
    suspend fun recognize(bitmap: android.graphics.Bitmap): List<OcrLine> = process(InputImage.fromBitmap(bitmap, 0))

    private suspend fun process(image: InputImage): List<OcrLine> {
        val recognizer = TextRecognition.getClient(TextRecognizerOptions.DEFAULT_OPTIONS)
        return try {
            suspendCancellableCoroutine { cont ->
                recognizer.process(image)
                    .addOnSuccessListener { result ->
                        val lines = result.textBlocks.flatMap { it.lines }.map { l ->
                            OcrLine(
                                l.text, l.boundingBox?.height() ?: 0,
                                l.boundingBox?.left ?: 0, l.boundingBox?.top ?: 0, l.boundingBox?.right ?: 0, l.boundingBox?.bottom ?: 0,
                            )
                        }
                        cont.resume(lines)
                    }
                    .addOnFailureListener { cont.resumeWithException(it) }
            }
        } finally {
            recognizer.close()
        }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\PhotoOcr.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.ColorMatrix
import android.graphics.ColorMatrixColorFilter
import android.graphics.Paint
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.io.File
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min

/** 照片上的一個範圍，以整張照片的比例表示（0..1）。 */
data class PhotoCrop(val left: Float, val top: Float, val right: Float, val bottom: Float)

/**
 * 血糖機照片的 OCR 流程：先照原樣辨識；讀不到再把畫面轉灰階、拉高對比後重試。
 * 可只辨識使用者放大框出的範圍（血糖機螢幕在照片裡很小時，框起來辨識準很多）。
 */
object PhotoOcr {
    private const val TARGET_SIDE = 1400
    private const val MAX_UPSCALE = 4f
    private const val CONTRAST = 1.8f

    /** 範圍太小時放大到至少 TARGET_SIDE 像素（最多 4 倍），已夠大就不放大。 */
    internal fun upscaleFactor(w: Int, h: Int): Float = (TARGET_SIDE.toFloat() / max(1, max(w, h))).coerceIn(1f, MAX_UPSCALE)

    /**
     * 畫面上可見範圍對應到照片的比例。
     * 影像以 Fit 置中顯示在 boxW×boxH 的框內，再以框中心為軸縮放 scale 倍、平移 (tx, ty) 像素；
     * 螢幕點 p 對應到未縮放的點 q = (p − 中心 − t) / scale + 中心。
     */
    fun visibleCrop(boxW: Float, boxH: Float, imgW: Int, imgH: Int, scale: Float, tx: Float, ty: Float): PhotoCrop {
        val fit = min(boxW / imgW, boxH / imgH)
        val dw = imgW * fit
        val dh = imgH * fit
        val left0 = (boxW - dw) / 2
        val top0 = (boxH - dh) / 2
        fun qx(p: Float) = (p - boxW / 2 - tx) / scale + boxW / 2
        fun qy(p: Float) = (p - boxH / 2 - ty) / scale + boxH / 2
        fun fx(p: Float) = ((qx(p) - left0) / dw).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        fun fy(p: Float) = ((qy(p) - top0) / dh).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
        return PhotoCrop(fx(0f), fy(0f), fx(boxW), fy(boxH))
    }

    private fun decodeCrop(file: File, crop: PhotoCrop?): Bitmap? {
        val full = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath) ?: return null
        if (crop == null) return upscale(full)
        val x = (crop.left * full.width).toInt().coerceIn(0, full.width - 1)
        val y = (crop.top * full.height).toInt().coerceIn(0, full.height - 1)
        val w = ((crop.right - crop.left) * full.width).toInt().coerceIn(1, full.width - x)
        val h = ((crop.bottom - crop.top) * full.height).toInt().coerceIn(1, full.height - y)
        return upscale(Bitmap.createBitmap(full, x, y, max(w, 16).coerceAtMost(full.width - x), max(h, 16).coerceAtMost(full.height - y)))
    }

    private fun upscale(src: Bitmap): Bitmap {
        val f = upscaleFactor(src.width, src.height)
        return if (f <= 1f) src else Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(src, (src.width * f).toInt(), (src.height * f).toInt(), true)
    }

    /** 灰階＋拉高對比，讓液晶數字和底色分得更開。 */
    private fun enhance(src: Bitmap): Bitmap {
        val gray = ColorMatrix().apply { setSaturation(0f) }
        val t = (-0.5f * CONTRAST + 0.5f) * 255f
        val contrast = ColorMatrix(floatArrayOf(CONTRAST, 0f, 0f, 0f, t, 0f, CONTRAST, 0f, 0f, t, 0f, 0f, CONTRAST, 0f, t, 0f, 0f, 0f, 1f, 0f))
        gray.postConcat(contrast)
        val out = Bitmap.createBitmap(src.width, src.height, Bitmap.Config.ARGB_8888)
        Canvas(out).drawBitmap(src, 0f, 0f, Paint().apply { colorFilter = ColorMatrixColorFilter(gray) })
        return out
    }

    /** 依序嘗試「原樣」「增強對比」，第一個讀到血糖數字的結果就回傳；都沒有回傳空清單。 */
    suspend fun candidates(file: File, crop: PhotoCrop? = null): List<GlucoseCandidate> {
        val base = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) { decodeCrop(file, crop) } ?: return emptyList()
        // 先不靠文字辨識、直接在整張照片找七段數字（實測 41 張照片 37 張一次讀對）；其餘才走文字辨識與線索法
        val digitSearchCandidate = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) {
            val gray = grayRegion(base, 0, 0, base.width, base.height)
            SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(DigitSearch.search(gray, base.width, base.height))?.let { GlucoseCandidate(it, 3.0, "七段數字") }
        }
        if (digitSearchCandidate != null) return listOf(digitSearchCandidate) // 已讀到，不必再跑文字辨識（較快）
        val variants = listOf(base, withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { enhance(base) })
        for (bmp in variants) {
            val lines = OcrEngine.recognize(bmp)
            if (com.startinsnow.healthtracker.BuildConfig.DEBUG) android.util.Log.d("PhotoOcr", "${bmp.width}x${bmp.height} lines=" + lines.joinToString(" | ") { "'${it.text}'(${it.height})" })
            // 液晶七段數字一般文字辨識讀不出來：以辨識到的最大字（通常是數字的一部分）為線索，只看螢幕數字那一小塊
            // 位置線索可能來自增強對比後的影像，但數字一律從原影像（未增強）讀：增強後的灰階分布會讓七段判讀失準
            val seg = withContext(Dispatchers.Default) { digitSearchCandidate ?: sevenSegment(base, lines) }
            val found = (listOfNotNull(seg) + GlucoseOcrParser.candidates(lines)).distinctBy { it.value }
            if (found.isNotEmpty()) return found
        }
        return emptyList()
    }

    /**
     * 液晶七段數字：以辨識到的最大字（通常是最後一位數字）當線索，只框出那一排數字來讀，不看整張照片。
     * 數字高度用線索框高度的幾種比例各試一次；左側範圍也試兩種寬度（數字最多 3 位）。讀到合理血糖值就回傳。
     */
    private fun sevenSegment(bmp: Bitmap, lines: List<OcrLine>): GlucoseCandidate? {
        val anchors = lines.filter { it.bottom > it.top && it.height >= bmp.height * 0.04 }.sortedByDescending { it.height }.take(1) // 主要路徑是 DigitSearch；這裡只是最後的備援，只試最大的一個線索
        for (a in anchors) {
            val h = a.bottom - a.top
            for (leftMul in listOf(3.0)) {
                val x0 = (a.left - leftMul * h).toInt().coerceIn(0, bmp.width - 2)
                val y0 = (a.top - 0.08 * h).toInt().coerceIn(0, bmp.height - 2)
                val x1 = (a.right + 0.3 * h).toInt().coerceIn(x0 + 1, bmp.width) // 文字框常比實際數字偏左，右側要多留
                val y1 = (a.bottom + 0.08 * h).toInt().coerceIn(y0 + 1, bmp.height)
                val gray = grayRegion(bmp, x0, y0, x1, y1)
                debugDump(bmp, gray, x1 - x0, y1 - y0, h, "a${a.left}_${a.top}_${a.right}_${a.bottom}_m$leftMul")
                for (hintMul in listOf(0.88)) { // 文字框比實際數字高約 12%
                    val raw = SevenSegmentReader.read(gray, x1 - x0, y1 - y0, (h * hintMul).toInt())
                    if (com.startinsnow.healthtracker.BuildConfig.DEBUG) android.util.Log.d("PhotoOcr", "seg7 anchor=[${a.left},${a.top},${a.right},${a.bottom}] roi=[$x0,$y0,$x1,$y1] hint=${(h * hintMul).toInt()} -> $raw")
                    val v = SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(raw) ?: continue
                    return GlucoseCandidate(v, 2.0, "七段數字")
                }
            }
        }
        // 沒有可用的線索（例如使用者已把數字放大框好）：整個影像當成數字區
        val gray = grayRegion(bmp, 0, 0, bmp.width, bmp.height)
        val v = SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(SevenSegmentReader.read(gray, bmp.width, bmp.height)) ?: return null
        return GlucoseCandidate(v, 2.0, "七段數字")
    }

    /** 除錯版才用：把送去讀的區塊存成檔案，方便拉回電腦分析。 */
    private fun debugDump(bmp: Bitmap, gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, hint: Int, name: String) {
        if (!com.startinsnow.healthtracker.BuildConfig.DEBUG) return
        try {
            val dir = java.io.File(com.startinsnow.healthtracker.HealthTrackerApp.appContext.filesDir, "dbg").apply { mkdirs() }
            val bb = java.nio.ByteBuffer.allocate(12 + gray.size).putInt(w).putInt(h).putInt(hint)
            gray.forEach { bb.put(it.toByte()) }
            java.io.File(dir, "$name.bin").writeBytes(bb.array())
        } catch (_: Exception) {
        }
    }

    private fun grayRegion(bmp: Bitmap, x0: Int, y0: Int, x1: Int, y1: Int): IntArray {
        val w = x1 - x0
        val h = y1 - y0
        val px = IntArray(w * h)
        bmp.getPixels(px, 0, w, x0, y0, w, h)
        return IntArray(w * h) { i -> val c = px[i]; (((c shr 16) and 255) * 299 + ((c shr 8) and 255) * 587 + (c and 255) * 114) / 1000 }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\PhotoStore.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import android.content.Context
import android.net.Uri
import android.graphics.Bitmap
import android.graphics.BitmapFactory
import android.graphics.Matrix
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import androidx.exifinterface.media.ExifInterface
import java.io.File
import java.time.LocalDateTime
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZoneOffset
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.UUID
import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/** 照片只存在 App 私有目錄（filesDir/photos），不進相簿、不進系統備份。 */
object PhotoStore {
    private fun dir(context: Context) = File(context.filesDir, "photos").apply { mkdirs() }

    fun authority(context: Context) = "${context.packageName}.fileprovider"

    /** 建立供相機寫入的空檔案，回傳檔案與可交給相機的 content Uri。 */
    fun newCameraTarget(context: Context): Pair<File, Uri> {
        val f = File(dir(context), "${UUID.randomUUID()}.jpg")
        return f to FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, authority(context), f)
    }

    /** 把使用者從相簿選的照片複製進私有目錄（之後相簿刪除也不影響紀錄）。 */
    fun importFrom(context: Context, source: Uri): File? {
        val f = File(dir(context), "${UUID.randomUUID()}.jpg")
        val ok = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(source)?.use { input ->
            f.outputStream().use { input.copyTo(it) }
            true
        } ?: false
        return if (ok) f else null
    }

    /** 把從雲端下載的照片存進私有目錄（檔名固定為紀錄 id，重複下載會覆蓋同一個檔）。 */
    fun saveBytes(context: Context, recordId: String, bytes: ByteArray): File? = try {
        File(dir(context), "cloud-$recordId.jpg").also { it.writeBytes(bytes) }
    } catch (e: java.io.IOException) {
        null
    }

    fun uriOf(context: Context, file: File): Uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, authority(context), file)

    /** 縮到長邊不超過 maxSide 的尺寸（不放大），保持長寬比。 */
    internal fun scaledSize(w: Int, h: Int, maxSide: Int): Pair<Int, Int> {
        val longest = max(w, h)
        if (longest <= maxSide) return w to h
        val f = maxSide.toDouble() / longest
        return max(1, (w * f).roundToInt()) to max(1, (h * f).roundToInt())
    }

    /**
     * 相機原圖動輒 4～10 MB：拍照或選圖後立刻縮到長邊 maxSide、JPEG 品質 quality，並依 EXIF 轉正後覆寫原檔
     * （方向已烤進像素，所以丟掉 EXIF 也不會顛倒）。OCR 與日後上傳都用這份縮小的檔案。
     * 解碼失敗時保留原檔不動。回傳是否有改寫。
     */
    fun compressInPlace(file: File, maxSide: Int = 1800, quality: Int = 85): Boolean {
        val bounds = BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inJustDecodeBounds = true }
        BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, bounds)
        if (bounds.outWidth <= 0 || bounds.outHeight <= 0) return false
        var sample = 1
        while (max(bounds.outWidth, bounds.outHeight) / (sample * 2) >= maxSide) sample *= 2
        val decoded = BitmapFactory.decodeFile(file.absolutePath, BitmapFactory.Options().apply { inSampleSize = sample })
            ?: return false
        val degrees = when (ExifInterface(file.absolutePath).getAttributeInt(ExifInterface.TAG_ORIENTATION, 1)) {
            ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_90 -> 90f
            ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_180 -> 180f
            ExifInterface.ORIENTATION_ROTATE_270 -> 270f
            else -> 0f
        }
        val (tw, th) = scaledSize(decoded.width, decoded.height, maxSide)
        var bmp: Bitmap = if (tw != decoded.width || th != decoded.height) Bitmap.createScaledBitmap(decoded, tw, th, true) else decoded
        if (degrees != 0f) {
            bmp = Bitmap.createBitmap(bmp, 0, 0, bmp.width, bmp.height, Matrix().apply { postRotate(degrees) }, true)
        }
        val tmp = File(file.parentFile, file.name + ".tmp")
        tmp.outputStream().use { bmp.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, quality, it) }
        return if (tmp.length() > 0) {
            tmp.copyTo(file, overwrite = true)
            tmp.delete()
            true
        } else {
            tmp.delete()
            false
        }
    }

    private val exifFormat = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy:MM:dd HH:mm:ss")

    /** 把 EXIF 時間字串（yyyy:MM:dd HH:mm:ss，可帶 +08:00 這類時差）轉成毫秒；格式不對回傳 null。 */
    internal fun parseExifTime(text: String?, offset: String?, zone: ZoneId): Long? {
        val t = text?.trim().orEmpty()
        if (t.isEmpty()) return null
        val local = try { LocalDateTime.parse(t, exifFormat) } catch (e: Exception) { return null }
        val z: ZoneId = offset?.trim()?.takeIf { it.isNotEmpty() }
            ?.let { runCatching { ZoneOffset.of(it) }.getOrNull() } ?: zone
        return local.atZone(z).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
    }

    /** 讀照片的拍攝時間（EXIF）。一定要在 compressInPlace 之前呼叫，縮圖後 EXIF 會被丟掉。沒有就回傳 null。 */
    fun captureTime(file: File, zone: ZoneId = ZoneId.systemDefault()): Long? = try {
        val e = ExifInterface(file.absolutePath)
        parseExifTime(e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_DATETIME_ORIGINAL), e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_OFFSET_TIME_ORIGINAL), zone)
            ?: parseExifTime(e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_DATETIME_DIGITIZED), e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_OFFSET_TIME_DIGITIZED), zone)
            ?: parseExifTime(e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_DATETIME), e.getAttribute(ExifInterface.TAG_OFFSET_TIME), zone)
    } catch (e: Exception) { null }

    fun delete(path: String?) {
        if (path != null) File(path).delete()
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ocr\SevenSegmentReader.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr

import kotlin.math.max
import kotlin.math.min
import kotlin.math.roundToInt

/**
 * 七段液晶數字讀取（血糖機螢幕）。一般文字辨識（ML Kit）讀不出這種字型，所以另外用「看哪幾段有墨」來判斷：
 * 1. 局部對比取出深色像素，只留長條狀、大小相近的連通塊（＝各個線段），丟掉反光與刮痕；
 * 2. 找傾斜角度把斜體字扶正，依空白欄位切成一個個數字；
 * 3. 每個數字檢查 a～g 七個位置有沒有墨，查表得到 0～9；窄的數字視為 1。
 * 純影像運算，不依賴 Android API，可直接做單元測試。
 */
object SevenSegmentReader {
    private val TABLE = mapOf(
        "abcdef" to 0, "bc" to 1, "abdeg" to 2, "abcdg" to 3, "bcfg" to 4,
        "acdfg" to 5, "acdefg" to 6, "abc" to 7, "abcdefg" to 8, "abcdfg" to 9,
    )

    /** gray：0..255 灰階，大小 w×h。回傳數字字串（1～4 位）；讀不出來回傳 null。 */
    fun read(gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, digitHeight: Int = 0): String? {
        // 局部對比的視窗大小會影響結果：用三種大小各讀一次，避免單次誤讀
        var results = listOf(0.12, 0.2, 0.3).mapNotNull { readWith(gray, w, h, it, digitHeight) }
        // 線段之間的縫很細時，先模糊會讓相鄰線段黏成一塊；讀不到就改用不模糊的遮罩再試一次
        if (results.isEmpty()) results = listOf(0.12, 0.2, 0.3).mapNotNull { readWith(gray, w, h, it, digitHeight, preBlur = 0) }
        if (results.isEmpty()) return null
        val counts = results.groupingBy { it }.eachCount()
        val top = counts.maxByOrNull { it.value }!!
        // 兩次以上相同才採用；只有一個視窗讀到（其他都讀不出來）而沒有互相矛盾的結果，也採用
        return if (top.value >= 2 || counts.size == 1) top.key else null
    }

    internal fun readWith(gray: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, windowFrac: Double, digitHeight: Int = 0, preBlur: Int = 1): String? {
        if (w < 40 || h < 40) return null
        // 四周補一圈底色，避免緊貼邊緣的數字被當成邊界雜訊丟掉
        val pad = max(8, min(w, h) / 12)
        val pw = w + 2 * pad
        val ph = h + 2 * pad
        val mean = gray.average().toInt()
        val g = IntArray(pw * ph) { mean }
        for (y in 0 until h) System.arraycopy(gray, y * w, g, (y + pad) * pw + pad, w)

        // 局部背景的視窗要比線段結構大：已知數字高度時取 0.7～1.8 倍數字高度，否則取影像短邊的比例
        val windowPx = if (digitHeight > 0) (digitHeight * windowFrac * 6).toInt() else (min(pw, ph) * windowFrac).toInt().coerceIn(21, 151)
        val mask = darkMask(g, pw, ph, windowPx, preBlur) ?: return null
        val segs = if (digitHeight > 0) hintedComponents(mask, pw, ph, digitHeight) else segmentComponents(mask, pw, ph)
        if (segs.size < 3) return null
        val pixels = digitBand(segs, pw, ph, digitHeight) ?: return null // 編碼 y*pw+x
        val k = bestShear(pixels, pw, ph)
        return decodeDigits(pixels, pw, ph, k)
    }

    // ---------- 1. 深色遮罩 ----------

    private fun darkMask(g: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, windowPx: Int, preBlur: Int): BooleanArray? {
        val blur = if (preBlur > 0) boxBlur(g, w, h, preBlur) else g
        val win = windowPx.coerceAtLeast(21) / 2
        val bg = boxBlur(blur, w, h, win)
        val sorted = blur.sorted()
        val spread = sorted[(sorted.size * 0.9).toInt()] - sorted[(sorted.size * 0.1).toInt()]
        val t = (spread * 0.18).toInt().coerceIn(12, 40)
        val mask = BooleanArray(w * h) { bg[it] - blur[it] > t }
        return if (mask.count { it } < 200) null else mask
    }

    /** 以 integral image 做方框平均（半徑 r）。 */
    internal fun boxBlur(src: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, r: Int): IntArray {
        val ii = LongArray((w + 1) * (h + 1))
        for (y in 0 until h) {
            var row = 0L
            for (x in 0 until w) {
                row += src[y * w + x]
                ii[(y + 1) * (w + 1) + x + 1] = ii[y * (w + 1) + x + 1] + row
            }
        }
        return IntArray(w * h) { i ->
            val x = i % w
            val y = i / w
            val x0 = max(0, x - r)
            val y0 = max(0, y - r)
            val x1 = min(w, x + r + 1)
            val y1 = min(h, y + r + 1)
            val sum = ii[y1 * (w + 1) + x1] - ii[y0 * (w + 1) + x1] - ii[y1 * (w + 1) + x0] + ii[y0 * (w + 1) + x0]
            (sum / ((x1 - x0) * (y1 - y0))).toInt()
        }
    }

    // ---------- 2. 線段候選 ----------

    internal class Comp(val pixels: IntArray, val area: Int, val bw: Int, val bh: Int, val x0: Int, val y0: Int, val touchesEdge: Boolean)

    /** 已知數字高度時（由文字辨識的位置線索而來）：只看大小合理的塊，不必猜標準大小；黏在一起的線段也保留。 */
    private fun hintedComponents(mask: BooleanArray, w: Int, h: Int, digitHeight: Int): List<Comp> =
        allComponents(mask, w, h).filter {
            it.area >= 0.012 * digitHeight * digitHeight && !it.touchesEdge &&
                it.bh <= 1.3 * digitHeight && it.bw <= 3.0 * digitHeight && max(it.bw, it.bh) <= 12 * min(it.bw, it.bh)
        }

    internal fun allComponents(mask: BooleanArray, w: Int, h: Int): List<Comp> {
        val seen = BooleanArray(mask.size)
        val comps = ArrayList<Comp>()
        val stack = IntArray(mask.size)
        for (start in mask.indices) {
            if (!mask[start] || seen[start]) continue
            var sp = 0
            stack[sp++] = start
            seen[start] = true
            val pts = ArrayList<Int>()
            var x0 = Int.MAX_VALUE; var y0 = Int.MAX_VALUE; var x1 = -1; var y1 = -1
            while (sp > 0) {
                val p = stack[--sp]
                pts.add(p)
                val x = p % w
                val y = p / w
                x0 = min(x0, x); x1 = max(x1, x); y0 = min(y0, y); y1 = max(y1, y)
                for (dy in -1..1) for (dx in -1..1) {
                    val nx = x + dx
                    val ny = y + dy
                    if (nx < 0 || ny < 0 || nx >= w || ny >= h) continue
                    val n = ny * w + nx
                    if (mask[n] && !seen[n]) { seen[n] = true; stack[sp++] = n }
                }
            }
            comps.add(Comp(pts.toIntArray(), pts.size, x1 - x0 + 1, y1 - y0 + 1, x0, y0, x0 == 0 || y0 == 0 || x1 == w - 1 || y1 == h - 1))
        }
        return comps
    }

    private fun segmentComponents(mask: BooleanArray, w: Int, h: Int): List<Comp> {
        val comps = allComponents(mask, w, h)
        // 螢幕上各線段的大小相近：找「面積最集中的一群」（至少 4 塊）當標準，再依標準的長寬篩選
        val cand = comps.filter { !it.touchesEdge && it.area >= 60 }.sortedBy { it.area }
        var bestStart = 0
        var bestCount = 0
        var j = 0
        for (i in cand.indices) {
            if (j < i) j = i
            while (j + 1 < cand.size && cand[j + 1].area <= 1.8 * cand[i].area) j++
            if (j - i + 1 > bestCount) { bestCount = j - i + 1; bestStart = i }
        }
        if (bestCount < 4) return emptyList()
        val cluster = cand.subList(bestStart, bestStart + bestCount)
        val minArea = cluster.first().area
        val maxArea = cluster.last().area
        val medLong = cluster.map { max(it.bw, it.bh) }.sorted()[cluster.size / 2]
        val medShort = cluster.map { min(it.bw, it.bh) }.sorted()[cluster.size / 2]
        // 相鄰線段常黏在一起（L 形），所以短邊與面積上限放寬；邊框、機身輪廓這類又大又長的會被丟掉
        return cand.filter {
            it.area >= 0.4 * minArea && it.area <= 3.2 * maxArea &&
                max(it.bw, it.bh) <= 1.9 * medLong && min(it.bw, it.bh) <= 4 * medShort &&
                max(it.bw, it.bh) <= 8 * min(it.bw, it.bh) // 太細長的是底線或刮痕
        }
    }

    /**
     * 只留「一排數字」所在的高度範圍：直條線段的長度約是數字高度的 0.42，
     * 據此推出數字高度，再找墨最多的那一段高度；範圍外的條狀雜物（底線、單位字、反光）直接丟掉。
     */
    private fun digitBand(segs: List<Comp>, w: Int, h: Int, hintHeight: Int): IntArray? {
        val verticals = segs.filter { it.bh >= 1.8 * it.bw }.map { it.bh }.sorted()
        if (hintHeight <= 0 && verticals.size < 2) return null
        val digitH = if (hintHeight > 0) hintHeight else (verticals[verticals.size / 2] * 2.4).toInt()
        val rows = IntArray(h)
        for (c in segs) for (p in c.pixels) rows[p / w]++
        var best = -1
        var bestY = 0
        var sum = 0
        for (y in 0 until h) {
            sum += rows[y]
            if (y >= digitH) sum -= rows[y - digitH]
            if (sum > best) { best = sum; bestY = y - digitH + 1 }
        }
        // 線段的中心點落在這一排的高度範圍內就保留（整個線段保留，不切半）
        val kept = segs.filter { val mid = it.y0 + it.bh / 2; mid >= bestY && mid <= bestY + digitH }.flatMap { c -> c.pixels.asList() }
        return if (kept.size < 100) null else kept.toIntArray()
    }

    // ---------- 3. 扶正斜體 ----------

    private fun shearX(p: Int, w: Int, k: Double, midY: Double, off: Int): Int {
        val y = p / w
        val x = p % w
        return x + (-k * (y - midY)).roundToInt() + off
    }

    private fun bestShear(pixels: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int): Double {
        var best = -1.0
        var bestK = 0.0
        val off = h
        var step = -20
        while (step <= 20) {
            val k = step * 0.02
            val col = IntArray(w + 2 * h + 2)
            for (p in pixels) col[shearX(p, w, k, h / 2.0, off).coerceIn(0, col.size - 1)]++
            var score = 0.0
            for (c in col) score += c.toDouble() * c
            if (score > best) { best = score; bestK = k }
            step++
        }
        return bestK
    }

    // ---------- 4. 切數字、查表 ----------

    private fun decodeDigits(pixels: IntArray, w: Int, h: Int, k: Double): String? {
        val off = h
        val sw = w + 2 * h + 2
        val xs = IntArray(pixels.size) { shearX(pixels[it], w, k, h / 2.0, off).coerceIn(0, sw - 1) }
        val ys = IntArray(pixels.size) { pixels[it] / w }
        val rows = IntArray(h)
        for (y in ys) rows[y]++
        val rowMax = rows.max()
        val yTop = rows.indexOfFirst { it > rowMax * 0.08 }
        val yBot = rows.indexOfLast { it > rowMax * 0.08 }
        if (yTop < 0) return null
        val dh = yBot - yTop + 1
        if (dh < 20) return null

        val col = IntArray(sw)
        for (i in xs.indices) if (ys[i] in yTop..yBot) col[xs[i]]++
        val runs = ArrayList<IntArray>()
        val gap = max(2, (dh * 0.05).toInt())
        var start = -1
        var last = -1
        for (x in 0 until sw) {
            if (col[x] > dh * 0.08) {
                if (start < 0) start = x
                last = x
            } else if (start >= 0 && x - last > gap) {
                runs.add(intArrayOf(start, last)); start = -1
            }
        }
        if (start >= 0) runs.add(intArrayOf(start, last))
        // 由右邊算起，相鄰數字的間隔不會超過數字高度的 0.3 倍；更遠的是旁邊的雜物（框線、反光）。
        // 例外：「1」靠格子右側，它左邊的數字離得比較遠，所以右邊鄰居是「1」時放寬到 0.85 倍。
        val allRuns = runs.filter { it[1] - it[0] + 1 >= dh * 0.1 }
        val wideRun = allRuns.maxOfOrNull { it[1] - it[0] + 1 } ?: return null
        val chain = ArrayList<IntArray>()
        for (r in allRuns.reversed()) {
            val last = chain.lastOrNull()
            val lastNarrow = last != null && last[1] - last[0] + 1 < 0.55 * wideRun
            if (last == null || last[0] - r[1] <= dh * (if (lastNarrow) 0.85 else 0.3)) chain.add(r) else break
        }
        chain.reverse()
        val digits = if (chain.size > 3) chain.takeLast(3) else chain // 血糖最多 3 位數
        if (digits.isEmpty() || digits.size > 4) return null

        val wide = digits.maxOf { it[1] - it[0] + 1 }
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        for (d in digits) {
            val dw = d[1] - d[0] + 1
            if (dw < 0.55 * wide) { sb.append('1'); continue }
            val grid = BooleanArray(dh * dw)
            for (i in xs.indices) {
                val x = xs[i] - d[0]
                val y = ys[i] - yTop
                if (x in 0 until dw && y in 0 until dh) grid[y * dw + x] = true
            }
            fun ratio(ya: Double, yb: Double, xa: Double, xb: Double): Double {
                val y0 = (dh * ya).toInt()
                val y1 = max((dh * yb).toInt(), y0 + 1)
                val x0 = (dw * xa).toInt()
                val x1 = max((dw * xb).toInt(), x0 + 1)
                var on = 0
                var n = 0
                for (y in y0 until min(y1, dh)) for (x in x0 until min(x1, dw)) { n++; if (grid[y * dw + x]) on++ }
                return if (n == 0) 0.0 else on.toDouble() / n
            }
            val probes = mapOf(
                'a' to ratio(0.0, 0.16, 0.2, 0.8), 'g' to ratio(0.42, 0.58, 0.2, 0.8), 'd' to ratio(0.84, 1.0, 0.2, 0.8),
                'f' to ratio(0.12, 0.45, 0.0, 0.3), 'b' to ratio(0.12, 0.45, 0.7, 1.0),
                'e' to ratio(0.55, 0.88, 0.0, 0.3), 'c' to ratio(0.55, 0.88, 0.7, 1.0),
            )
            val on = probes.filter { it.value > 0.4 }.keys.sorted().joinToString("")
            sb.append(TABLE[on]?.toString() ?: return null)
        }
        return sb.toString()
    }

    /** 讀到的數字字串轉成血糖值；位數或範圍不合理回傳 null。 */
    fun toGlucose(digits: String?): Double? {
        val v = digits?.takeIf { it.length in 2..3 }?.toIntOrNull() ?: return null
        return v.toDouble().takeIf { it in GlucoseOcrParser.MIN_MGDL..GlucoseOcrParser.MAX_MGDL }
    }

}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\reminder\ReminderScheduler.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.reminder

import android.Manifest
import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.content.pm.PackageManager
import android.os.Build
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.HealthTrackerApp
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.MainActivity
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.R
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

object ReminderLogic {
    const val MAX_TIMES = 4

    /** 距離下一個 minuteOfDay（當天第幾分鐘）還有多久；已過就算明天。 */
    fun delayUntilNext(minuteOfDay: Int, now: ZonedDateTime): Duration {
        var next = now.withHour(minuteOfDay / 60).withMinute(minuteOfDay % 60).withSecond(0).withNano(0)
        if (!next.isAfter(now)) next = next.plusDays(1)
        return Duration.between(now, next)
    }

    /** 要不要發提醒：設定「今天已記錄就不提醒」且今天已有血糖紀錄時不發。 */
    fun shouldNotify(records: List<HealthRecord>, skipIfRecorded: Boolean, now: ZonedDateTime): Boolean {
        if (!skipIfRecorded) return true
        val start = now.toLocalDate().atStartOfDay(now.zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli()
        val end = start + 86_400_000L
        return records.none { it.type == RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE && it.deletedAt == null && it.measuredAt in start until end }
    }

    fun label(minuteOfDay: Int) = "%02d:%02d".format(minuteOfDay / 60, minuteOfDay % 60)
}

/** 每個提醒時間一個單次工作，做完再排隔天（不會逐日漂移）。關閉提醒就全部取消。 */
object ReminderScheduler {
    const val TAG = "reminder"
    const val KEY_MINUTE = "minute"
    const val CHANNEL = "reminder"

    private fun workName(minute: Int) = "reminder-$minute"

    fun apply(context: Context, enabled: Boolean, times: List<Int>) {
        WorkManager.getInstance(context).cancelAllWorkByTag(TAG)
        if (!enabled) return
        times.forEach { schedule(context, it, ExistingWorkPolicy.REPLACE) }
    }

    fun schedule(context: Context, minute: Int, policy: ExistingWorkPolicy) {
        val delay = ReminderLogic.delayUntilNext(minute, ZonedDateTime.now(ZoneId.systemDefault()))
        val request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<ReminderWorker>()
            .setInitialDelay(delay.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
            .setInputData(workDataOf(KEY_MINUTE to minute))
            .addTag(TAG)
            .build()
        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(workName(minute), policy, request)
    }

    fun hasNotificationPermission(context: Context): Boolean =
        Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 33 ||
            ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(context, Manifest.permission.POST_NOTIFICATIONS) == PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED

    fun ensureChannel(context: Context) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 26) return
        val nm = context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
        nm.createNotificationChannel(NotificationChannel(CHANNEL, "量測提醒", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
    }

    @Suppress("MissingPermission")
    fun notifyNow(context: Context) {
        if (!hasNotificationPermission(context)) return
        ensureChannel(context)
        val open = PendingIntent.getActivity(
            context, 0, Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java),
            PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT,
        )
        val n = NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL)
            .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle("血糖記錄")
            .setContentText("該記錄血糖了")
            .setContentIntent(open)
            .setAutoCancel(true)
            .build()
        NotificationManagerCompat.from(context).notify(1001, n)
    }
}

class ReminderWorker(context: Context, params: WorkerParameters) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {
    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val minute = inputData.getInt(ReminderScheduler.KEY_MINUTE, -1)
        val app = applicationContext as HealthTrackerApp
        val s = app.settings.settings.first()
        // 設定已關閉或這個時間已被移除：不發也不再排
        if (!s.reminderEnabled || minute !in s.reminderTimes) return Result.success()
        val now = ZonedDateTime.now(ZoneId.systemDefault())
        if (ReminderLogic.shouldNotify(app.records.observeAll().first(), s.reminderSkipIfRecorded, now)) {
            ReminderScheduler.notifyNow(applicationContext)
        }
        ReminderScheduler.schedule(applicationContext, minute, ExistingWorkPolicy.APPEND_OR_REPLACE)
        return Result.success()
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\report\PdfReport.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.report

import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.OverviewMath
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.ReportData
import java.io.OutputStream
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter

/** 把 ReportData 畫成 A4 的 PDF（用系統內建的 PdfDocument，中文使用系統字型）。 */
object PdfReport {
    private const val W = 595
    private const val H = 842
    private const val M = 40f
    private val dateTime = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")
    private val dateOnly = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyy-MM-dd")
    private val monthDay = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MM/dd")

    private fun paint(size: Float, bold: Boolean = false, color: Int = Color.rgb(26, 27, 36)) = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply {
        textSize = size
        this.color = color
        typeface = if (bold) Typeface.DEFAULT_BOLD else Typeface.DEFAULT
    }

    fun write(data: ReportData, generatedAt: Long, zone: ZoneId, out: OutputStream) {
        val doc = PdfDocument()
        var pageNo = 0
        var page: PdfDocument.Page? = null
        var c: Canvas? = null
        var y = 0f

        fun footer() {
            val cv = c ?: return
            val p = paint(8f, color = Color.GRAY)
            cv.drawText("此報告由「血糖記錄」App 依您自行記錄的數值整理，不含任何醫療判斷；如有疑問請諮詢醫師。", M, H - 24f, p)
            cv.drawText("第 $pageNo 頁", W - M - 30f, H - 24f, p)
        }
        fun newPage() {
            page?.let { footer(); doc.finishPage(it) }
            pageNo++
            val pg = doc.startPage(PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(W, H, pageNo).create())
            page = pg
            c = pg.canvas
            y = M
        }
        fun need(h: Float) { if (y + h > H - 50f) newPage() }
        fun text(s: String, p: Paint, x: Float = M) { c!!.drawText(s, x, y, p) }

        val title = paint(22f, bold = true, color = Color.rgb(33, 44, 158))
        val h2 = paint(14f, bold = true)
        val body = paint(11f)
        val small = paint(9f, color = Color.DKGRAY)
        val line = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(211, 213, 230); strokeWidth = 0.8f }

        newPage()
        y += 18f; text("血糖記錄報告", title); y += 20f
        val period = "${dateOnly.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.from).atZone(zone))} ～ ${dateOnly.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(data.to).atZone(zone))}（近 ${data.days} 天）"
        text("期間：$period", body); y += 15f
        text("產生時間：${dateTime.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(generatedAt).atZone(zone))}" + if (data.name.isNotEmpty()) "　　姓名：${data.name}" else "", body); y += 24f

        // 血糖統計
        text("血糖統計（mg/dL）", h2); y += 18f
        val o = data.overall
        if (o == null) {
            text("這段期間沒有血糖紀錄。", body); y += 18f
        } else {
            text("共 ${data.glucoseTotal} 筆　平均 ${"%.0f".format(o.avg)}　最低 ${"%.0f".format(o.min)}　最高 ${"%.0f".format(o.max)}", body); y += 16f
            data.range?.let {
                text("落在您設定的範圍（${it.low.toInt()}–${it.high.toInt()}）內：${it.stat.inRange}/${it.stat.total} 筆（${it.stat.percent}%）", body); y += 16f
            }
            y += 6f
            // 依情境
            if (data.contexts.isNotEmpty()) {
                need(24f + 16f * (data.contexts.size + 1))
                text("依測量情境", h2); y += 16f
                val xs = floatArrayOf(M, M + 110f, M + 190f, M + 270f, M + 350f)
                listOf("情境", "筆數", "平均", "最低", "最高").forEachIndexed { i, s -> c!!.drawText(s, xs[i], y, small) }
                y += 4f; c!!.drawLine(M, y, W - M, y, line); y += 13f
                data.contexts.forEach { row ->
                    listOf(row.label, "${row.stats.count}", "%.0f".format(row.stats.avg), "%.0f".format(row.stats.min), "%.0f".format(row.stats.max))
                        .forEachIndexed { i, s -> c!!.drawText(s, xs[i], y, body) }
                    y += 16f
                }
                y += 8f
            }
            // 趨勢圖
            if (data.glucose.size >= 2) {
                need(190f)
                text("血糖趨勢", h2); y += 8f
                drawChart(c!!, data, zone, M, y, W - 2 * M, 150f)
                y += 150f + 28f
            }
        }

        // 其他量測
        if (data.others.isNotEmpty()) {
            need(24f + 16f * data.others.size)
            text("其他量測（同一期間）", h2); y += 16f
            data.others.forEach {
                text("${it.label}：${it.count} 筆　最新 ${it.latest}　平均 ${it.average}", body); y += 16f
            }
            y += 8f
        }

        // 明細
        if (data.glucose.isNotEmpty()) {
            need(60f)
            text("血糖明細" + if (data.glucoseTotal > data.glucose.size) "（僅列最新 ${data.glucose.size} 筆，統計含全部 ${data.glucoseTotal} 筆）" else "", h2); y += 18f
            val xs = floatArrayOf(M, M + 130f, M + 200f, M + 260f)
            fun header() {
                listOf("時間", "血糖", "情境", "備註").forEachIndexed { i, s -> c!!.drawText(s, xs[i], y, small) }
                y += 4f; c!!.drawLine(M, y, W - M, y, line); y += 13f
            }
            header()
            data.glucose.reversed().forEach { r ->
                if (y + 16f > H - 50f) { newPage(); header() }
                c!!.drawText(dateTime.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(r.measuredAt).atZone(zone)), xs[0], y, body)
                c!!.drawText("${r.value.toInt()}", xs[1], y, body)
                c!!.drawText(r.context?.label ?: "", xs[2], y, body)
                c!!.drawText(r.note.take(22), xs[3], y, body)
                y += 15f
            }
        }

        footer()
        page?.let { doc.finishPage(it) }
        doc.writeTo(out)
        doc.close()
    }

    private fun drawChart(cv: Canvas, data: ReportData, zone: ZoneId, x0: Float, y0: Float, w: Float, h: Float) {
        val pts = data.glucose
        val band = data.range
        val scale = OverviewMath.niceScale(
            minOf(pts.minOf { it.value }, band?.low ?: Double.MAX_VALUE),
            maxOf(pts.maxOf { it.value }, band?.high ?: -Double.MAX_VALUE),
        )
        val left = x0 + 30f
        val width = w - 36f
        val t0 = pts.first().measuredAt
        val t1 = pts.last().measuredAt
        fun px(t: Long) = left + if (t1 == t0) width / 2 else (t - t0).toFloat() / (t1 - t0) * width
        fun py(v: Double) = y0 + h - ((v - scale.min) / (scale.max - scale.min)).toFloat() * h
        band?.let {
            cv.drawRect(left, py(it.high), left + width, py(it.low), Paint().apply { color = Color.argb(40, 14, 124, 143) })
        }
        val grid = Paint().apply { color = Color.rgb(222, 224, 238); strokeWidth = 0.6f }
        val lab = paint(8f, color = Color.DKGRAY)
        scale.ticks.forEach { tick ->
            cv.drawLine(left, py(tick), left + width, py(tick), grid)
            cv.drawText("%.0f".format(tick), x0, py(tick) + 3f, lab)
        }
        listOf(t0, t1).distinct().forEachIndexed { i, t ->
            val s = monthDay.format(Instant.ofEpochMilli(t).atZone(zone))
            cv.drawText(s, if (i == 0) left else left + width - 24f, y0 + h + 12f, lab)
        }
        val lineP = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = Color.rgb(42, 120, 214); strokeWidth = 1.6f; style = Paint.Style.STROKE; strokeCap = Paint.Cap.ROUND }
        for (i in 1 until pts.size) cv.drawLine(px(pts[i - 1].measuredAt), py(pts[i - 1].value), px(pts[i].measuredAt), py(pts[i].value), lineP)
        val dot = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = Color.rgb(42, 120, 214) }
        val ring = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG).apply { color = Color.WHITE }
        if (pts.size <= 80) pts.forEach { cv.drawCircle(px(it.measuredAt), py(it.value), 3.6f, ring); cv.drawCircle(px(it.measuredAt), py(it.value), 2.4f, dot) }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\report\SampleReport.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.report

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import java.util.Random

/** 預覽報告版面用的假資料（只在除錯版出現，不會寫進資料庫）。固定亂數種子，每次產生的內容相同。 */
object SampleReport {
    private const val DAY = 86_400_000L

    fun records(now: Long): List<HealthRecord> {
        val rnd = Random(7)
        val out = ArrayList<HealthRecord>()
        var n = 0
        fun add(type: RecordType, v: Double, v2: Double?, ageDays: Double, ctx: MeasureContext? = null) {
            out += HealthRecord(
                id = "sample-${n++}", type = type, value = v, value2 = v2, measuredAt = now - (ageDays * DAY).toLong(),
                createdAt = 1, updatedAt = 1, context = ctx, note = if (n % 11 == 0) "範例備註" else "",
            )
        }
        val ctxs = listOf(MeasureContext.FASTING, MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL, MeasureContext.BEFORE_MEAL, MeasureContext.BEDTIME)
        for (d in 0 until 40) {
            for ((i, c) in ctxs.withIndex()) {
                if (rnd.nextInt(5) == 0) continue
                val base = when (c) { MeasureContext.FASTING -> 95.0; MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL -> 135.0; MeasureContext.BEFORE_MEAL -> 105.0; MeasureContext.BEDTIME -> 115.0 }
                add(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, Math.round(base + rnd.nextGaussian() * 12).toDouble(), null, d + 0.15 + i * 0.2, c)
            }
            if (d % 3 == 0) add(RecordType.WEIGHT, Math.round((70 + rnd.nextGaussian()) * 10) / 10.0, null, d + 0.3)
            if (d % 7 == 0) add(RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE, 118.0 + rnd.nextInt(8), 76.0 + rnd.nextInt(6), d + 0.4)
        }
        return out
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\security\AppLock.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.security

import androidx.biometric.BiometricManager
import androidx.biometric.BiometricManager.Authenticators.BIOMETRIC_WEAK
import androidx.biometric.BiometricManager.Authenticators.DEVICE_CREDENTIAL
import androidx.biometric.BiometricPrompt
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat
import androidx.fragment.app.FragmentActivity

/** App 鎖：用手機既有的指紋 / 臉部 / 螢幕鎖定 PIN，App 自己不存任何密碼。 */
object AppLock {
    private const val AUTH = BIOMETRIC_WEAK or DEVICE_CREDENTIAL

    /** 手機有沒有可用的驗證方式（沒設螢幕鎖定就沒有）。 */
    fun available(activity: FragmentActivity): Boolean =
        BiometricManager.from(activity).canAuthenticate(AUTH) == BiometricManager.BIOMETRIC_SUCCESS

    fun prompt(activity: FragmentActivity, title: String, onResult: (Boolean) -> Unit) {
        val prompt = BiometricPrompt(
            activity,
            ContextCompat.getMainExecutor(activity),
            object : BiometricPrompt.AuthenticationCallback() {
                override fun onAuthenticationSucceeded(result: BiometricPrompt.AuthenticationResult) = onResult(true)
                override fun onAuthenticationError(errorCode: Int, errString: CharSequence) = onResult(false)
            },
        )
        prompt.authenticate(
            BiometricPrompt.PromptInfo.Builder().setTitle(title).setAllowedAuthenticators(AUTH).build(),
        )
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\sync\SyncScheduler.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.sync

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.BackoffPolicy
import androidx.work.Constraints
import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.ExistingWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.NetworkType
import androidx.work.OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.workDataOf
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZonedDateTime
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/**
 * 背景同步排程：
 * - 每 6 小時檢查一次，有待上傳的資料才同步（沒有就不連網）。
 * - 每晚 20:00 完整同步（先拉後推）。用「做完再排下一次」的單次工作，避免週期工作逐日漂移。
 * - 失敗（網路、伺服器暫時錯誤）由 WorkManager 指數退避重試。
 */
object SyncScheduler {
    const val DAILY_WORK = "daily-sync"
    const val CHECK_WORK = "periodic-check"
    const val KEY_ONLY_IF_PENDING = "only_if_pending"
    const val DAILY_HOUR = 20
    const val BACKOFF_MINUTES = 15L

    private val network = Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED).build()

    /** 距離下一個 hour:00 還有多久（已過今天的時間就算明天）。 */
    fun delayUntilNext(hour: Int, now: ZonedDateTime): Duration {
        var next = now.withHour(hour).withMinute(0).withSecond(0).withNano(0)
        if (!next.isAfter(now)) next = next.plusDays(1)
        return Duration.between(now, next)
    }

    /** App 啟動時呼叫：已排好就保留，不重複排。 */
    fun ensureScheduled(context: Context) {
        val wm = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
        wm.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
            CHECK_WORK,
            ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,
            PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<SyncWorker>(6, TimeUnit.HOURS)
                .setConstraints(network)
                .setInputData(workDataOf(KEY_ONLY_IF_PENDING to true))
                .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, BACKOFF_MINUTES, TimeUnit.MINUTES)
                .build(),
        )
        scheduleDaily(context, ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP)
    }

    /** 排下一次 20:00 同步。工作做完（非重試）時以 APPEND_OR_REPLACE 接續下一天。 */
    fun scheduleDaily(context: Context, policy: ExistingWorkPolicy = ExistingWorkPolicy.APPEND_OR_REPLACE) {
        val delay = delayUntilNext(DAILY_HOUR, ZonedDateTime.now(ZoneId.systemDefault()))
        val request = OneTimeWorkRequestBuilder<SyncWorker>()
            .setInitialDelay(delay.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)
            .setConstraints(network)
            .setBackoffCriteria(BackoffPolicy.EXPONENTIAL, BACKOFF_MINUTES, TimeUnit.MINUTES)
            .addTag(DAILY_WORK)
            .build()
        WorkManager.getInstance(context).enqueueUniqueWork(DAILY_WORK, policy, request)
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\sync\SyncWorker.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.sync

import android.content.Context
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.ListenableWorker
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.HealthTrackerApp

/** 背景同步工作：沒設定雲端或沒登入就直接結束；可重試的失敗最多重試 MAX_ATTEMPTS 次，之後等下一輪。 */
class SyncWorker(context: Context, params: WorkerParameters) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {
    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val isDaily = tags.contains(SyncScheduler.DAILY_WORK)
        val engine = (applicationContext as HealthTrackerApp).cloud.engine
        val result = when {
            engine == null || engine.currentSession() == null -> null
            inputData.getBoolean(SyncScheduler.KEY_ONLY_IF_PENDING, false) && !engine.hasPending() -> null
            else -> engine.sync()
        }
        val retry = result != null && result.retryable && runAttemptCount < MAX_ATTEMPTS
        if (retry) return Result.retry()
        // 每晚 20:00 的工作在「最終結果」確定後排下一天（重試期間不排，避免重複）。
        if (isDaily) SyncScheduler.scheduleDaily(applicationContext)
        return Result.success()
    }

    private companion object {
        const val MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5
    }
}
