@Composable
fun HealthTrackerTheme(content: @Composable () -> Unit) {
    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = if (isSystemInDarkTheme()) DarkColors else LightColors,
        typography = AppTypography,
        shapes = AppShapes,
        content = content,
    )
}

/** 統一的卡片：白底、細邊框、無陰影（背景略帶藍灰，卡片自然浮出來）。有 onClick 就可點。 */
@Composable
fun AppCard(modifier: Modifier = Modifier, onClick: (() -> Unit)? = null, content: @Composable ColumnScope.() -> Unit) {
    val colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface)
    val border = BorderStroke(1.dp, MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outlineVariant)
    val elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 0.dp)
    if (onClick == null) {
        Card(modifier, colors = colors, border = border, elevation = elevation, content = content)
    } else {
        Card(onClick = onClick, modifier = modifier, colors = colors, border = border, elevation = elevation, content = content)
    }
}

/** 各類型的代表色與圖示（只用來幫助掃讀，不代表數值好壞）。 */
@Composable
fun recordAccent(type: RecordType): Color = when (type) {
    RecordType.WEIGHT -> MaterialTheme.colorScheme.primary
    RecordType.BODY_FAT -> MaterialTheme.colorScheme.tertiary
    RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE -> if (isSystemInDarkTheme()) Color(0xFFFF8A8A) else BrandRed
    RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE -> if (isSystemInDarkTheme()) Color(0xFFD0A8FF) else Color(0xFF8E4EC6)
}

fun recordIcon(type: RecordType): ImageVector = when (type) {
    RecordType.WEIGHT -> Icons.Filled.MonitorWeight
    RecordType.BODY_FAT -> Icons.Filled.Percent
    RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE -> Icons.Filled.WaterDrop
    RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE -> Icons.Filled.Favorite
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\update\AppUpdater.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.net.Uri
import android.provider.Settings
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud.CloudErrorKind
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud.CloudException
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud.SyncEngine
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import kotlinx.serialization.SerialName
import kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import okhttp3.OkHttpClient
import okhttp3.Request
import java.io.File
import java.io.IOException
import java.security.MessageDigest
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/** 雲端放的最新版資訊（app-updates bucket 裡的 latest.json）。 */
@Serializable
data class UpdateInfo(
    val versionCode: Int,
    val versionName: String,
    /** bucket 內的 APK 檔名。 */
    val apk: String,
    /** APK 的 SHA-256（小寫十六進位），下載後必須吻合才會安裝。 */
    val sha256: String,
    val size: Long = 0,
    val notes: String = "",
    @SerialName("package") val packageName: String = "",
)

object UpdateLogic {
    private val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }

    fun parse(text: String): UpdateInfo? = try {
        json.decodeFromString(UpdateInfo.serializer(), text).takeIf { it.versionCode > 0 && it.apk.isNotBlank() && it.sha256.length == 64 }
    } catch (e: Exception) {
        null
    }

    fun isNewer(remote: UpdateInfo, currentVersionCode: Int) = remote.versionCode > currentVersionCode

    /** 檔名只允許單層安全字元，避免 latest.json 被竄改成指向其他路徑。 */
    fun isSafeApkName(name: String) = Regex("""[A-Za-z0-9._\-]{1,80}\.apk""").matches(name)

    fun sha256Hex(file: File): String {
        val md = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
        file.inputStream().use { input ->
            val buf = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
            while (true) {
                val n = input.read(buf)
                if (n < 0) break
                md.update(buf, 0, n)
            }
        }
        return md.digest().joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
    }
}

/**
 * App 內雲端更新：從你自己 Supabase 的私人 bucket「app-updates」讀最新版資訊與 APK（要登入才讀得到）。
 * 下載後比對 SHA-256，再交給系統安裝程式；Android 只允許用同一把簽章金鑰的 APK 覆蓋更新，
 * 資料不會被清掉。最後一步的安裝確認一定由你在手機上按。
 */
class AppUpdater(
    private val engine: SyncEngine,
    private val baseUrl: String,
    private val anonKey: String,
    private val client: OkHttpClient = OkHttpClient.Builder()
        .connectTimeout(15, TimeUnit.SECONDS).readTimeout(60, TimeUnit.SECONDS).build(),
) {
    private fun objectRequest(token: String, name: String) = Request.Builder()
        .url("${baseUrl.trim().trimEnd('/')}/storage/v1/object/authenticated/$BUCKET/$name")
        .header("apikey", anonKey).header("Authorization", "Bearer $token").build()

    /** 讀雲端最新版資訊；雲端還沒放版本檔回傳 null。 */
    suspend fun fetchLatest(): UpdateInfo? = engine.withSession { s ->
        withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                client.newCall(objectRequest(s.accessToken, "latest.json")).execute().use { r ->
                    when {
                        r.code == 404 || r.code == 400 -> null
                        r.code == 401 -> throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.UNAUTHORIZED, "登入已過期", 401)
                        !r.isSuccessful -> throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "讀取版本資訊失敗（HTTP ${r.code}）", r.code)
                        else -> UpdateLogic.parse(r.body?.string().orEmpty())
                            ?: throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "雲端的版本資訊格式不正確")
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: IOException) {
                throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "無法連線到雲端，請檢查網路後再試")
            }
        }
    }

    /** 下載 APK 到 dest，邊下載邊回報進度；完成後驗證 SHA-256，不吻合就刪檔並丟出錯誤。 */
    suspend fun download(info: UpdateInfo, dest: File, onProgress: (done: Long, total: Long) -> Unit) {
        if (!UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName(info.apk)) throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "雲端的版本資訊不安全，已中止")
        engine.withSession { s ->
            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
                try {
                    dest.parentFile?.mkdirs()
                    client.newCall(objectRequest(s.accessToken, info.apk)).execute().use { r ->
                        if (r.code == 401) throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.UNAUTHORIZED, "登入已過期", 401)
                        if (!r.isSuccessful) throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "下載失敗（HTTP ${r.code}）", r.code)
                        val body = r.body ?: throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "下載內容是空的")
                        val total = body.contentLength().takeIf { it > 0 } ?: info.size
                        var done = 0L
                        body.byteStream().use { input ->
                            dest.outputStream().use { out ->
                                val buf = ByteArray(64 * 1024)
                                while (true) {
                                    val n = input.read(buf)
                                    if (n < 0) break
                                    out.write(buf, 0, n)
                                    done += n
                                    onProgress(done, total)
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                } catch (e: IOException) {
                    dest.delete()
                    throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "下載中斷，請檢查網路後再試")
                }
                if (UpdateLogic.sha256Hex(dest) != info.sha256.lowercase()) {
                    dest.delete()
                    throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "檔案檢查碼不符，已丟棄下載的檔案")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    companion object {
        const val BUCKET = "app-updates"

        fun apkFile(context: Context) = File(File(context.cacheDir, "updates"), "update.apk")

        fun canInstall(context: Context) = context.packageManager.canRequestPackageInstalls()

        /** 要使用者先允許「安裝未知應用程式」：開系統設定頁。 */
        fun openInstallPermissionSettings(context: Context) {
            context.startActivity(
                Intent(Settings.ACTION_MANAGE_UNKNOWN_APP_SOURCES, Uri.parse("package:${context.packageName}")).addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK),
            )
        }

        /** 交給系統安裝程式；系統會跳出安裝確認，由使用者按下。 */
        fun install(context: Context, apk: File) {
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(context, "${context.packageName}.fileprovider", apk)
            context.startActivity(
                Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).setDataAndType(uri, "application/vnd.android.package-archive")
                    .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK),
            )
        }
    }
}


===== app\src\main\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\update\UpdateScheduler.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update

import android.app.NotificationChannel
import android.app.NotificationManager
import android.app.PendingIntent
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.os.Build
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.core.app.NotificationCompat
import androidx.core.app.NotificationManagerCompat
import androidx.work.Constraints
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.NetworkType
import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.BuildConfig
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.HealthTrackerApp
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.MainActivity
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.R
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud.CloudErrorKind
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud.CloudException
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.reminder.ReminderScheduler
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

/** 檢查間隔的選項（小時）；0 = 只手動檢查。 */
object UpdateIntervals {
    val OPTIONS = listOf(0, 6, 12, 24, 72, 168)

    fun label(hours: Int) = when (hours) {
        0 -> "僅手動"
        6 -> "每 6 小時"
        12 -> "每 12 小時"
        24 -> "每天"
        72 -> "每 3 天"
        168 -> "每週"
        else -> "每 $hours 小時"
    }

    fun sanitize(hours: Int) = if (hours in OPTIONS) hours else 0
}

/** 收到通知後要把畫面帶到「設定 > 關於」的訊號。 */
object UpdateNav {
    var openUpdate by mutableStateOf(false)
    const val EXTRA = "open_update"
}

/** 自動檢查的紀錄（SharedPreferences），讓設定頁看得到上次檢查的結果與已下載的新版。 */
object UpdateState {
    private const val PREFS = "update_state"

    private fun p(c: Context) = c.getSharedPreferences(PREFS, Context.MODE_PRIVATE)

    fun downloadedCode(c: Context) = p(c).getInt("dl_code", 0)
    fun downloadedName(c: Context) = p(c).getString("dl_name", "").orEmpty()
    fun notifiedCode(c: Context) = p(c).getInt("notified_code", 0)
    fun lastCheck(c: Context): Pair<Long, String>? =
        p(c).getString("last_check", null)?.split('|', limit = 2)?.takeIf { it.size == 2 }?.let { it[0].toLong() to it[1] }

    fun setDownloaded(c: Context, code: Int, name: String) = p(c).edit().putInt("dl_code", code).putString("dl_name", name).apply()
    fun setNotified(c: Context, code: Int) = p(c).edit().putInt("notified_code", code).apply()
    fun setLastCheck(c: Context, message: String) = p(c).edit().putString("last_check", "${System.currentTimeMillis()}|$message").apply()
}

object UpdateLogicAuto {
    /** 已下載的檔案要比目前安裝的版本新、而且檔案還在，才算「可以安裝」。 */
    fun pendingInstall(downloadedCode: Int, fileExists: Boolean, currentCode: Int) = downloadedCode > currentCode && fileExists

    /** 同一個新版本只通知一次。 */
    fun shouldNotify(latestCode: Int, notifiedCode: Int, currentCode: Int) = latestCode > currentCode && latestCode != notifiedCode

    /** 只有版本號變了或檔案不在才需要（重新）下載。 */
    fun shouldDownload(latestCode: Int, downloadedCode: Int, fileExists: Boolean) = latestCode != downloadedCode || !fileExists
}

/**
 * 自動檢查更新：依設定的間隔在背景（只在 Wi‑Fi 等不計量網路）檢查雲端有沒有新版，有就先下載並通知你。
 * Android 不允許 App 靜默安裝，所以最後的安裝仍要你點通知回到 App、再在系統畫面確認。
 */
object UpdateScheduler {
    const val WORK = "app-update-check"
    const val CHANNEL = "app_update"

    fun apply(context: Context, intervalHours: Int, policy: ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy = ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.UPDATE) {
        val wm = WorkManager.getInstance(context)
        val hours = UpdateIntervals.sanitize(intervalHours)
        if (hours == 0) {
            wm.cancelUniqueWork(WORK)
            return
        }
        val request = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<UpdateWorker>(hours.toLong(), TimeUnit.HOURS)
            .setConstraints(Constraints.Builder().setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.UNMETERED).setRequiresBatteryNotLow(true).build())
            .build()
        wm.enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(WORK, policy, request)
    }

    fun ensureChannel(context: Context) {
        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < 26) return
        context.getSystemService(NotificationManager::class.java)
            .createNotificationChannel(NotificationChannel(CHANNEL, "App 更新", NotificationManager.IMPORTANCE_DEFAULT))
    }

    @Suppress("MissingPermission")
    fun notifyDownloaded(context: Context, versionName: String) {
        if (!ReminderScheduler.hasNotificationPermission(context)) return
        ensureChannel(context)
        val intent = Intent(context, MainActivity::class.java).putExtra(UpdateNav.EXTRA, true)
            .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_SINGLE_TOP or Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_CLEAR_TOP)
        val open = PendingIntent.getActivity(context, 1, intent, PendingIntent.FLAG_IMMUTABLE or PendingIntent.FLAG_UPDATE_CURRENT)
        val n = NotificationCompat.Builder(context, CHANNEL)
            .setSmallIcon(R.mipmap.ic_launcher)
            .setContentTitle("血糖記錄有新版本")
            .setContentText("$versionName 已下載，點一下回到 App 安裝")
            .setContentIntent(open)
            .setAutoCancel(true)
            .build()
        NotificationManagerCompat.from(context).notify(1002, n)
    }
}

class UpdateWorker(context: Context, params: WorkerParameters) : CoroutineWorker(context, params) {
    override suspend fun doWork(): Result {
        val app = applicationContext as HealthTrackerApp
        if (app.settings.settings.first().updateIntervalHours <= 0) return Result.success()
        val engine = app.cloud.engine ?: return Result.success()
        if (engine.currentSession() == null) {
            UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, "尚未登入雲端")
            return Result.success()
        }
        val updater = AppUpdater(engine, BuildConfig.SUPABASE_URL, BuildConfig.SUPABASE_ANON_KEY)
        return try {
            val info = updater.fetchLatest()
            val current = BuildConfig.VERSION_CODE
            if (info == null) {
                UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, "雲端還沒有放新版本")
            } else if (!UpdateLogic.isNewer(info, current)) {
                UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, "已是最新版（${BuildConfig.VERSION_NAME}）")
            } else {
                val file = AppUpdater.apkFile(applicationContext)
                if (UpdateLogicAuto.shouldDownload(info.versionCode, UpdateState.downloadedCode(applicationContext), file.isFile)) {
                    updater.download(info, file) { _, _ -> }
                    UpdateState.setDownloaded(applicationContext, info.versionCode, info.versionName)
                }
                UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, "新版本 ${info.versionName} 已下載，等你安裝")
                if (UpdateLogicAuto.shouldNotify(info.versionCode, UpdateState.notifiedCode(applicationContext), current)) {
                    UpdateScheduler.notifyDownloaded(applicationContext, info.versionName)
                    UpdateState.setNotified(applicationContext, info.versionCode)
                }
            }
            Result.success()
        } catch (e: CloudException) {
            UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, e.message ?: "檢查失敗")
            if (e.kind == CloudErrorKind.NETWORK && runAttemptCount < 3) Result.retry() else Result.success()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            UpdateState.setLastCheck(applicationContext, "檢查失敗：${e.message}")
            Result.success()
        }
    }
}

===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\CalendarMathTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.CalendarMath
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.LocalDate
import java.time.YearMonth
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

class CalendarMathTest {
    private val zone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")

    private fun rec(y: Int, m: Int, d: Int, h: Int, min: Int) = HealthRecord(
        id = "$y$m$d$h$min", type = RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, value = 100.0,
        measuredAt = ZonedDateTime.of(y, m, d, h, min, 0, 0, zone).toInstant().toEpochMilli(), createdAt = 1, updatedAt = 1,
    )

    @Test fun monthGridStartsOnSunday() {
        // 2026-10-01 是星期四 → 前面補 4 格（日一二三）
        val g = CalendarMath.monthGrid(YearMonth.of(2026, 10))
        assertEquals(4, g.takeWhile { it == null }.size)
        assertEquals(LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1), g[4])
        assertEquals(4 + 31, g.size)
    }

    @Test fun monthStartingOnSundayHasNoLeadingBlanks() {
        val g = CalendarMath.monthGrid(YearMonth.of(2026, 2)) // 2026-02-01 是星期日
        assertEquals(LocalDate.of(2026, 2, 1), g[0])
        assertEquals(28, g.size)
    }

    @Test fun countsGroupByLocalDay() {
        val c = CalendarMath.countsByDay(listOf(rec(2026, 10, 20, 7, 30), rec(2026, 10, 20, 23, 59), rec(2026, 10, 19, 8, 0)), zone)
        assertEquals(2, c[LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 20)])
        assertEquals(1, c[LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 19)])
        assertNull(c[LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 18)])
    }

    @Test fun levelCapsAtThree() {
        assertEquals(0, CalendarMath.level(0))
        assertEquals(2, CalendarMath.level(2))
        assertEquals(3, CalendarMath.level(9))
    }

    @Test fun dayDotsPlaceRowAndTimeFraction() {
        val today = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 20)
        val dots = CalendarMath.dayDots(listOf(rec(2026, 10, 20, 12, 0), rec(2026, 10, 18, 6, 0), rec(2026, 10, 1, 6, 0)), 7, today, zone)
        assertEquals(2, dots.size) // 10/01 超出 7 列
        assertEquals(0, dots[0].row)
        assertEquals(0.5f, dots[0].fraction, 0.0001f)
        assertEquals(2, dots[1].row)
        assertEquals(0.25f, dots[1].fraction, 0.0001f)
    }

    @Test fun futureDaysAreDropped() {
        val dots = CalendarMath.dayDots(listOf(rec(2026, 10, 21, 12, 0)), 7, LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 20), zone)
        assertEquals(0, dots.size)
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\GlucoseOcrParserTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.GlucoseOcrParser
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.OcrLine
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class GlucoseOcrParserTest {
    private fun values(vararg lines: OcrLine) = GlucoseOcrParser.candidates(lines.toList()).map { it.value }

    @Test fun biggestDigitsWin() {
        val v = values(OcrLine("10/19 09:27", 20), OcrLine("112", 120), OcrLine("mg/dL", 25))
        assertEquals(listOf(112.0), v)
    }

    @Test fun datesTimesAndYearsAreIgnored() {
        assertTrue(values(OcrLine("2026/10/19", 30), OcrLine("09:27", 30), OcrLine("10-19", 30)).isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun unitNeighbourBoostsRanking() {
        // 兩行同高，緊鄰 mg/dL 的那行應排前面。
        val v = values(OcrLine("88", 50), OcrLine("134", 50), OcrLine("mg/dL", 20))
        assertEquals(134.0, v.first(), 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun lookalikeLettersAreFixed() {
        assertEquals(listOf(105.0), values(OcrLine("1O5", 100)))
        assertEquals(listOf(110.0), values(OcrLine("ll0", 100)))
    }

    @Test fun pureLettersNotConverted() {
        assertTrue(values(OcrLine("OIl", 100), OcrLine("FORA", 40)).isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun outOfRangeDropped() {
        assertTrue(values(OcrLine("10", 100), OcrLine("999", 100)).isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun duplicatesCollapsed() {
        assertEquals(listOf(120.0), values(OcrLine("120", 100), OcrLine("120", 40)))
    }

    @Test fun emptyInput() = assertTrue(GlucoseOcrParser.candidates(emptyList()).isEmpty())
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\HealthStandardsTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.prefs.Sex
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.HealthStandards
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class HealthStandardsTest {
    @Test fun bmiUsesTaiwanCutoffs() {
        assertEquals("過瘦", HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(17.0, 30).label)
        assertEquals("正常", HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(22.0, 30).label)
        assertEquals("過重", HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(25.0, 30).label)
        assertEquals("肥胖", HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(28.0, 30).label)
    }

    @Test fun childrenAreNotJudgedByAdultBmi() {
        assertEquals("青少年", HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(22.0, 12).label)
        assertEquals(HealthStandards.Severity.UNKNOWN, HealthStandards.bmiAssessment(22.0, 12).severity)
    }

    @Test fun healthyWeightRangeForHeight() {
        val r = HealthStandards.healthyWeightRange(170.0)!!
        assertEquals(53.5, r.minKg, 0.1)
        assertEquals(69.4, r.maxKg, 0.1)
        assertNull(HealthStandards.healthyWeightRange(null))
    }

    @Test fun bodyFatUsesAgeAndSexBands() {
        assertEquals("過瘦", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(7.0, Sex.MALE, 30)!!.label)
        assertEquals("正常", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(18.0, Sex.MALE, 30)!!.label)
        assertEquals("過重", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(22.0, Sex.MALE, 30)!!.label)
        assertEquals("肥胖", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(30.0, Sex.MALE, 30)!!.label)
        assertEquals("正常", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(30.0, Sex.FEMALE, 45)!!.label)
        assertEquals("肥胖", HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(42.0, Sex.FEMALE, 65)!!.label)
        assertNull(HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(22.0, Sex.UNSPECIFIED, 30))
        assertNull(HealthStandards.bodyFatAssessment(22.0, Sex.MALE, null))
    }

    @Test fun fastingGlucoseUsesWhoAdaRanges() {
        assertEquals("低血糖", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(60.0, MeasureContext.FASTING).label)
        assertEquals("正常", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(90.0, MeasureContext.FASTING).label)
        assertEquals("偏高（糖尿病前期）", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(110.0, MeasureContext.FASTING).label)
        assertEquals("糖尿病範圍", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(130.0, MeasureContext.FASTING).label)
    }

    @Test fun afterMealGlucoseUsesPostprandialRanges() {
        assertEquals("正常", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(120.0, MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL).label)
        assertEquals("偏高（糖尿病前期）", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(150.0, MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL).label)
        assertEquals("糖尿病範圍", HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(210.0, MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL).label)
    }

    @Test fun elderlyNoteIsAdded() {
        val a = HealthStandards.glucoseAssessment(100.0, MeasureContext.FASTING, 70)
        assertTrue(a.detail.contains("65 歲以上"))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\MeasureTimeTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.MeasureTime
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.RecordValidator
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneId

class MeasureTimeTest {
    private val taipei = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")
    private fun ms(iso: String) = Instant.parse(iso).toEpochMilli()

    @Test fun futureToleranceIsFiveMinutes() {
        val now = ms("2026-10-19T04:00:00Z")
        assertFalse(MeasureTime.isFuture(now + 4 * 60_000, now))
        assertFalse(MeasureTime.isFuture(now + 5 * 60_000, now))
        assertTrue(MeasureTime.isFuture(now + 5 * 60_000 + 1, now))
        assertFalse(MeasureTime.isFuture(now - 86_400_000, now))
    }

    @Test fun datePickerMidnightMapsToLocalDate() {
        // 台北 2026-10-19 06:33 = UTC 10/18 22:33；當地日期是 10/19，DatePicker 要的是 10/19 的 UTC 午夜
        val m = ms("2026-10-18T22:33:00Z")
        assertEquals(ms("2026-10-19T00:00:00Z"), MeasureTime.utcMidnightOfLocalDate(m, taipei))
    }

    @Test fun changingDateKeepsTimeOfDay() {
        val m = ms("2026-10-18T22:33:00Z") // 台北 10/19 06:33
        val picked = ms("2026-10-15T00:00:00Z") // 選 10/15
        assertEquals(ms("2026-10-14T22:33:00Z"), MeasureTime.withDate(m, picked, taipei)) // 台北 10/15 06:33
    }

    @Test fun changingTimeKeepsLocalDate() {
        val m = ms("2026-10-18T22:33:00Z") // 台北 10/19 06:33
        assertEquals(ms("2026-10-19T12:05:00Z"), MeasureTime.withTime(m, 20, 5, taipei)) // 台北 10/19 20:05
    }

    @Test fun crossingMidnightBackwardsStaysOnTheSameLocalDay() {
        val m = ms("2026-10-19T15:30:00Z") // 台北 10/19 23:30
        assertEquals(ms("2026-10-18T16:10:00Z"), MeasureTime.withTime(m, 0, 10, taipei)) // 台北 10/19 00:10
    }

    @Test fun validatorRejectsFutureTimes() {
        val now = ms("2026-10-19T04:00:00Z")
        assertNull(RecordValidator.validateTime(now, now))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateTime(now + 3_600_000, now))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\OverviewMathTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.OverviewMath
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

private const val DAY = 86_400_000L
private const val NOW = 1_800_000_000_000L

private fun rec(id: String, type: RecordType, value: Double, ageDays: Double, deleted: Boolean = false) = HealthRecord(
    id = id, type = type, value = value, measuredAt = NOW - (ageDays * DAY).toLong(),
    createdAt = 1, updatedAt = 1, deletedAt = if (deleted) 5L else null,
)

class OverviewMathTest {
    private val all = listOf(
        rec("a", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 0.1),
        rec("b", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 120.0, 2.0),
        rec("c", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 90.0, 10.0),
        rec("d", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 300.0, 1.0, deleted = true),
        rec("e", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.0, 0.5),
    )

    @Test fun latestIgnoresDeletedAndOtherTypes() {
        assertEquals("a", OverviewMath.latest(all, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE)!!.id)
        assertEquals("e", OverviewMath.latest(all, RecordType.WEIGHT)!!.id)
        assertNull(OverviewMath.latest(all, RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE))
    }

    @Test fun windowKeepsOnlyRecentSortedOldestFirst() {
        assertEquals(listOf("b", "a"), OverviewMath.window(all, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 7, NOW).map { it.id })
        assertEquals(listOf("c", "b", "a"), OverviewMath.window(all, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 30, NOW).map { it.id })
    }

    @Test fun windowDropsTimesFarInTheFuture() {
        val bad = all + rec("f", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 111.0, -3.0)
        assertTrue(OverviewMath.window(bad, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 7, NOW).none { it.id == "f" })
    }

    @Test fun statsOverTheWindow() {
        val s = OverviewMath.stats(OverviewMath.window(all, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 30, NOW))!!
        assertEquals(3, s.count)
        assertEquals((100.0 + 120.0 + 90.0) / 3, s.avg, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(90.0, s.min, 0.0)
        assertEquals(120.0, s.max, 0.0)
        assertNull(OverviewMath.stats(emptyList()))
    }

    @Test fun niceScaleCoversRangeWithRoundTicks() {
        val s = OverviewMath.niceScale(88.0, 131.0)
        assertTrue(s.min <= 88.0 && s.max >= 131.0)
        val step = s.ticks[1] - s.ticks[0]
        assertTrue(listOf(5.0, 10.0, 20.0, 25.0, 50.0).any { OverviewMath.close(step, it) })
        assertTrue(s.ticks.size in 3..8)
    }

    @Test fun niceScaleHandlesFlatAndInvalidInput() {
        val flat = OverviewMath.niceScale(100.0, 100.0)
        assertTrue(flat.max > flat.min)
        assertEquals(listOf(0.0, 1.0), OverviewMath.niceScale(Double.NaN, 3.0).ticks)
    }

    @Test fun byContextFiltersOnlyWhenAContextIsGiven() {
        val ctxList = listOf(
            rec("f", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 90.0, 1.0).copy(context = com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.FASTING),
            rec("m", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 140.0, 1.0).copy(context = com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL),
            rec("n", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 1.0),
        )
        assertEquals(3, OverviewMath.byContext(ctxList, null).size)
        assertEquals(listOf("f"), OverviewMath.byContext(ctxList, com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.FASTING).map { it.id })
        assertTrue(OverviewMath.byContext(ctxList, com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.BEDTIME).isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun inRangeCountsValuesInsideTheUsersOwnRangeInclusive() {
        val l = listOf(70.0, 100.0, 140.0, 141.0, 180.0).mapIndexed { i, v -> rec("r$i", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, v, 1.0) }
        val r = OverviewMath.inRange(l, 70.0, 140.0)!!
        assertEquals(3, r.inRange)
        assertEquals(5, r.total)
        assertEquals(60, r.percent)
    }

    @Test fun inRangeIsNullWithoutRangeOrData() {
        assertNull(OverviewMath.inRange(emptyList(), 70.0, 140.0))
        assertNull(OverviewMath.inRange(listOf(rec("a", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 1.0)), null, 140.0))
    }

    @Test fun relativeAgeWording() {
        assertEquals("剛剛", OverviewMath.relativeAge(NOW - 30_000, NOW))
        assertEquals("5 分鐘前", OverviewMath.relativeAge(NOW - 5 * 60_000, NOW))
        assertEquals("3 小時前", OverviewMath.relativeAge(NOW - 3 * 3_600_000L, NOW))
        assertEquals("2 天前", OverviewMath.relativeAge(NOW - 2 * DAY - 1000, NOW))
        assertEquals("剛剛", OverviewMath.relativeAge(NOW + 1000, NOW))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\PhotoOcrTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.PhotoOcr
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

class PhotoOcrTest {
    @Test fun noZoomShowsWholePhoto() {
        val c = PhotoOcr.visibleCrop(1000f, 2000f, 1000, 2000, 1f, 0f, 0f)
        assertEquals(0f, c.left, 0.001f); assertEquals(0f, c.top, 0.001f)
        assertEquals(1f, c.right, 0.001f); assertEquals(1f, c.bottom, 0.001f)
    }

    @Test fun zoomTwiceCentredShowsMiddleHalf() {
        val c = PhotoOcr.visibleCrop(1000f, 2000f, 1000, 2000, 2f, 0f, 0f)
        assertEquals(0.25f, c.left, 0.001f); assertEquals(0.25f, c.top, 0.001f)
        assertEquals(0.75f, c.right, 0.001f); assertEquals(0.75f, c.bottom, 0.001f)
    }

    @Test fun panningMovesTheVisibleWindow() {
        // 放大 2 倍後把照片往右拖 250px → 看到的是照片更左邊的部分
        val c = PhotoOcr.visibleCrop(1000f, 2000f, 1000, 2000, 2f, 250f, 0f)
        assertEquals(0.125f, c.left, 0.001f)
        assertEquals(0.625f, c.right, 0.001f)
    }

    @Test fun letterboxedPhotoIsMappedByItsDisplayedArea() {
        // 橫向框內放直向照片（左右留黑）：不放大時整張照片都看得到
        val c = PhotoOcr.visibleCrop(2000f, 1000f, 500, 1000, 1f, 0f, 0f)
        assertEquals(0f, c.left, 0.001f); assertEquals(1f, c.right, 0.001f)
        assertEquals(0f, c.top, 0.001f); assertEquals(1f, c.bottom, 0.001f)
    }

    @Test fun draggingFarOutsideIsClampedToThePhoto() {
        val c = PhotoOcr.visibleCrop(1000f, 2000f, 1000, 2000, 2f, 5000f, 5000f)
        assertEquals(0f, c.left, 0.001f); assertEquals(0f, c.top, 0.001f)
    }

    @Test fun upscaleOnlyForSmallImages() {
        assertEquals(1f, PhotoOcr.upscaleFactor(2000, 1000), 0.001f)
        assertEquals(2f, PhotoOcr.upscaleFactor(700, 400), 0.001f)
        assertEquals(4f, PhotoOcr.upscaleFactor(50, 40), 0.001f)
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\PhotoStoreTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.PhotoStore
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals

class PhotoStoreTest {
    @Test fun smallerImagesAreNotUpscaled() = assertEquals(1200 to 800, PhotoStore.scaledSize(1200, 800, 1800))

    @Test fun landscapeIsScaledByWidth() = assertEquals(1800 to 1350, PhotoStore.scaledSize(4000, 3000, 1800))

    @Test fun portraitIsScaledByHeight() = assertEquals(1350 to 1800, PhotoStore.scaledSize(3000, 4000, 1800))

    @Test fun neverCollapsesToZero() = assertEquals(1 to 1800, PhotoStore.scaledSize(1, 100000, 1800))

    @Test fun exactlyAtLimitIsUnchanged() = assertEquals(1800 to 900, PhotoStore.scaledSize(1800, 900, 1800))

    private val taipei = java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")

    @Test fun exifTimeUsesPhoneZoneWhenNoOffset() =
        assertEquals(1_760_000_000_000L, PhotoStore.parseExifTime("2025:10:09 16:53:20", null, taipei))

    @Test fun exifTimeHonoursOffset() =
        assertEquals(1_760_000_000_000L, PhotoStore.parseExifTime("2025:10:09 08:53:20", "+00:00", taipei))

    @Test fun badExifTimeIsNull() {
        assertEquals(null, PhotoStore.parseExifTime("0000:00:00 00:00:00", null, taipei))
        assertEquals(null, PhotoStore.parseExifTime("", null, taipei))
        assertEquals(null, PhotoStore.parseExifTime(null, null, taipei))
