}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\RecordValidatorTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.prefs.UserSettings
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.RecordValidator
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test

class RecordValidatorTest {
    @Test fun weightInRangePasses() = assertNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.5, null))

    @Test fun weightTypoRejected() = assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.WEIGHT, 705.0, null))

    @Test fun missingValueRejected() = assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, null, null))

    @Test fun pressureNeedsBothValues() = assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE, 120.0, null))

    @Test fun pressureSystolicMustExceedDiastolic() =
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE, 80.0, 120.0))

    @Test fun normalPressurePasses() = assertNull(RecordValidator.validate(RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE, 120.0, 80.0))

    @Test fun glucoseRangeValidation() {
        assertNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(null, null))
        assertNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(70.0, 140.0))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(70.0, null))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(null, 140.0))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(140.0, 70.0))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(70.0, 70.0))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(5.0, 140.0))
        assertNotNull(RecordValidator.validateGlucoseRange(70.0, 900.0))
    }

    @Test fun bmiComputedFromHeight() {
        val bmi = UserSettings(heightCm = 170.0).bmi(72.25)!!
        assertEquals(25.0, bmi, 0.01)
    }

    @Test fun bmiNullWithoutHeight() = assertNull(UserSettings().bmi(70.0))
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ReminderLogicTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.reminder.ReminderLogic
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

class ReminderLogicTest {
    private val zone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")
    private val now = ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 10, 19, 9, 0, 0, 0, zone)

    private fun rec(type: RecordType, at: ZonedDateTime, deleted: Boolean = false) = HealthRecord(
        id = "x${at.toEpochSecond()}$type", type = type, value = 100.0, measuredAt = at.toInstant().toEpochMilli(),
        createdAt = 1, updatedAt = 1, deletedAt = if (deleted) 5L else null,
    )

    @Test fun laterTodayIsSameDay() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(1), ReminderLogic.delayUntilNext(10 * 60, now))

    @Test fun passedTimeGoesToTomorrow() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(23), ReminderLogic.delayUntilNext(8 * 60, now))

    @Test fun exactlyNowGoesToTomorrow() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(24), ReminderLogic.delayUntilNext(9 * 60, now))

    @Test fun notifiesWhenNothingRecordedToday() =
        assertTrue(ReminderLogic.shouldNotify(listOf(rec(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, now.minusDays(1))), true, now))

    @Test fun skipsWhenGlucoseRecordedToday() =
        assertFalse(ReminderLogic.shouldNotify(listOf(rec(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, now.minusHours(2))), true, now))

    @Test fun otherTypesOrDeletedDoNotCount() {
        val list = listOf(rec(RecordType.WEIGHT, now.minusHours(2)), rec(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, now.minusHours(1), deleted = true))
        assertTrue(ReminderLogic.shouldNotify(list, true, now))
    }

    @Test fun alwaysNotifiesWhenSkipOff() =
        assertTrue(ReminderLogic.shouldNotify(listOf(rec(RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, now.minusHours(2))), false, now))

    @Test fun labelPadsZeros() = assertEquals("07:05", ReminderLogic.label(7 * 60 + 5))
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ReportBuilderTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.repository.ReportBuilder
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

private const val DAY = 86_400_000L
private const val NOW = 1_800_000_000_000L

private fun r(
    id: String, type: RecordType, v: Double, ageDays: Double,
    ctx: MeasureContext? = null, v2: Double? = null, deleted: Boolean = false,
) = HealthRecord(
    id = id, type = type, value = v, value2 = v2, measuredAt = NOW - (ageDays * DAY).toLong(),
    createdAt = 1, updatedAt = 1, deletedAt = if (deleted) 9L else null, context = ctx,
)

class ReportBuilderTest {
    private val all = listOf(
        r("a", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 90.0, 1.0, MeasureContext.FASTING),
        r("b", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 130.0, 1.0, MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL),
        r("c", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 2.0, MeasureContext.FASTING),
        r("d", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 110.0, 3.0),
        r("e", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 500.0, 1.0, deleted = true),
        r("old", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 80.0, 50.0),
        r("w1", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.0, 1.0),
        r("w2", RecordType.WEIGHT, 72.0, 5.0),
        r("bp", RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE, 120.0, 2.0, v2 = 80.0),
    )

    @Test fun onlyTheRequestedPeriodAndNeverDeletedRecords() {
        val d30 = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW)
        assertEquals(listOf("d", "c", "a", "b").sorted(), d30.glucose.map { it.id }.sorted())
        assertEquals(4, d30.glucoseTotal)
        assertEquals(5, ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 90, NOW).glucoseTotal)
    }

    @Test fun glucoseRowsAreOldestFirst() {
        val ids = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW).glucose.map { it.id }
        assertEquals("d", ids.first())
    }

    @Test fun overallStats() {
        val s = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW).overall!!
        assertEquals(4, s.count)
        assertEquals(107.5, s.avg, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(90.0, s.min, 0.0)
        assertEquals(130.0, s.max, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun perContextStatsIncludeUnlabeledAndSkipEmptyContexts() {
        val rows = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW).contexts
        assertEquals(listOf("空腹", "餐後", "未標示"), rows.map { it.label })
        assertEquals(95.0, rows.first().stats.avg, 1e-9)
        assertEquals(2, rows.first().stats.count)
    }

    @Test fun rangeInfoOnlyWhenTheUserSetOne() {
        assertNull(ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW).range)
        val ri = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", 90.0, 120.0, 30, NOW).range!!
        assertEquals(3, ri.stat.inRange)
        assertEquals(4, ri.stat.total)
    }

    @Test fun otherMeasurementsAreSummarised() {
        val o = ReportBuilder.build(all, "", null, null, 30, NOW).others
        assertEquals(listOf("體重", "血壓"), o.map { it.label })
        assertEquals("70.0 kg", o[0].latest)
        assertEquals("71.0 kg", o[0].average)
        assertEquals("120/80 mmHg", o[1].latest)
    }

    @Test fun emptyDataGivesEmptyReportNotFakeNumbers() {
        val d = ReportBuilder.build(emptyList(), "A", null, null, 7, NOW)
        assertTrue(d.glucose.isEmpty())
        assertNull(d.overall)
        assertTrue(d.contexts.isEmpty() && d.others.isEmpty())
        assertEquals("A", d.name)
    }

    @Test fun veryLongPeriodsKeepTheNewestRowsButCountEverything() {
        val many = (0 until ReportBuilder.MAX_ROWS + 50).map { r("g$it", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 0.001 * it + 0.01) }
        val d = ReportBuilder.build(many, "", null, null, 30, NOW)
        assertEquals(ReportBuilder.MAX_ROWS, d.glucose.size)
        assertEquals(ReportBuilder.MAX_ROWS + 50, d.glucoseTotal)
        assertNotNull(d.overall)
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\SevenSegmentReaderTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.SevenSegmentReader
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Test
import java.util.Random

/** 用程式畫出斜體七段數字（模擬血糖機液晶），驗證能讀回正確數字。 */
class SevenSegmentReaderTest {
    private val segments = mapOf(
        0 to "abcdef", 1 to "bc", 2 to "abdeg", 3 to "abcdg", 4 to "bcfg",
        5 to "acdfg", 6 to "acdefg", 7 to "abc", 8 to "abcdefg", 9 to "abcdfg",
    )

    private class Img(val w: Int, val h: Int, val px: IntArray)

    private fun render(text: String, slant: Double = 0.14, seed: Long = 1, noise: Boolean = true): Img {
        val dh = 150
        val cw = 85
        val t = 14
        val gap = 3
        val pitch = cw + 24
        val margin = 50
        val w = margin * 2 + pitch * text.length + 60
        val h = dh + margin * 2
        val rnd = Random(seed)
        // 底色有左暗右亮的光線漸層
        val px = IntArray(w * h) { i -> 150 - ((w - i % w) * 25 / w) }
        fun fill(x0: Double, y0: Double, x1: Double, y1: Double, ox: Double) {
            for (y in y0.toInt()..y1.toInt()) {
                val shift = slant * (dh / 2.0 - y)
                for (x in (x0 + shift + ox).toInt()..(x1 + shift + ox).toInt()) {
                    if (x in 0 until w && y + margin in 0 until h) px[(y + margin) * w + x] = 55
                }
            }
        }
        text.forEachIndexed { i, ch ->
            val ox = margin + i * pitch.toDouble()
            val on = segments.getValue(ch - '0')
            val d = dh.toDouble()
            val c = cw.toDouble()
            // 橫條與直條在水平方向錯開（模擬液晶的斜切接縫），各自是獨立的線段
            val hx0 = t + 6.0
            val hx1 = c - t - 6
            if ('a' in on) fill(hx0, 0.0, hx1, t.toDouble(), ox)
            if ('g' in on) fill(hx0, d / 2 - t / 2, hx1, d / 2 + t / 2, ox)
            if ('d' in on) fill(hx0, d - t, hx1, d, ox)
            val top = 4.0
            val mid1 = d / 2 - t / 2 - 3
            val mid2 = d / 2 + t / 2 + 3
            val bot = d - 4
            if ('f' in on) fill(0.0, top, t.toDouble(), mid1, ox)
            if ('b' in on) fill(c - t, top, c, mid1, ox)
            if ('e' in on) fill(0.0, mid2, t.toDouble(), bot, ox)
            if ('c' in on) fill(c - t, mid2, c, bot, ox)
        }
        if (noise) {
            // 單位底線、左下的刮痕線、散落的小點（都很細，不該被當成線段）
            for (x in margin + 100 until margin + 190) for (y in h - 40 until h - 38) px[y * w + x] = 70
            for (x in 5 until 90) for (y in h - 60 until h - 58) px[y * w + x] = 60
            repeat(40) { val x = rnd.nextInt(w - 3); val y = rnd.nextInt(h - 3); px[y * w + x] = 70 }
        }
        return Img(w, h, px)
    }

    private fun read(text: String, slant: Double = 0.14, noise: Boolean = true): String? {
        val i = render(text, slant, noise = noise)
        return SevenSegmentReader.read(i.px, i.w, i.h)
    }

    @Test fun readsTheCommonCase() = assertEquals("108", read("108"))

    @Test fun readsEveryDigit() {
        val wrong = listOf("234", "567", "890", "123", "456", "789").filter { read(it) != it }.map { "$it→${read(it)}" }
        assertEquals("全部讀對", emptyList<String>(), wrong)
    }

    @Test fun readsTwoDigitValues() {
        assertEquals("95", read("95"))
        assertEquals("72", read("72"))
    }

    @Test fun readsWithoutSlantAndWithMoreSlant() {
        assertEquals("187", read("187", slant = 0.0))
        assertEquals("187", read("187", slant = 0.22))
    }

    @Test fun readsLeadingOnes() {
        assertEquals("112", read("112"))
        assertEquals("311", read("311"))
    }

    @Test fun blankImageGivesNull() {
        assertNull(SevenSegmentReader.read(IntArray(200 * 200) { 140 }, 200, 200))
    }

    @Test fun glucoseConversionChecksLengthAndRange() {
        assertEquals(108.0, SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose("108")!!, 0.0)
        assertNull(SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose("8"))
        assertNull(SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose("1234"))
        assertNull(SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose("09"))
        assertNull(SevenSegmentReader.toGlucose(null))
    }

    @Test fun digitSearchFindsTheDigitsInAWholeImage() {
        for (text in listOf("108", "95", "117")) {
            val i = render(text)
            assertEquals(text, com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.DigitSearch.search(i.px, i.w, i.h))
        }
    }

    @Test fun digitSearchGivesNullWhenThereAreNoDigits() {
        assertNull(com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ocr.DigitSearch.search(IntArray(300 * 300) { 130 }, 300, 300))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\UpdateAutoLogicTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update.UpdateIntervals
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update.UpdateLogicAuto
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

class UpdateAutoLogicTest {
    @Test fun pendingInstallNeedsNewerVersionAndFile() {
        assertTrue(UpdateLogicAuto.pendingInstall(330, true, 329))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.pendingInstall(330, true, 330)) // 已經裝了
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.pendingInstall(330, false, 329)) // 檔案被清掉
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.pendingInstall(0, false, 329))
    }

    @Test fun notifiesOncePerVersion() {
        assertTrue(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldNotify(331, 330, 329))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldNotify(331, 331, 329))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldNotify(329, 0, 329))
    }

    @Test fun downloadsOnlyWhenVersionChangedOrFileMissing() {
        assertTrue(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldDownload(331, 330, true))
        assertTrue(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldDownload(331, 331, false))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogicAuto.shouldDownload(331, 331, true))
    }

    @Test fun intervalOptionsAndLabels() {
        assertEquals(listOf(0, 6, 12, 24, 72, 168), UpdateIntervals.OPTIONS)
        assertEquals("僅手動", UpdateIntervals.label(0))
        assertEquals("每週", UpdateIntervals.label(168))
        assertEquals(0, UpdateIntervals.sanitize(5))
        assertEquals(24, UpdateIntervals.sanitize(24))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\UpdateLogicTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update.UpdateInfo
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.update.UpdateLogic
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.io.File

class UpdateLogicTest {
    private val sha = "a".repeat(64)

    @Test fun parsesValidInfo() {
        val i = UpdateLogic.parse("""{"versionCode":330,"versionName":"1.0.330","apk":"app-1.0.330.apk","sha256":"$sha","size":33000000,"notes":"圖表","package":"x.y"}""")
        assertNotNull(i)
        assertEquals(330, i!!.versionCode)
        assertEquals("x.y", i.packageName)
    }

    @Test fun ignoresUnknownFields() =
        assertNotNull(UpdateLogic.parse("""{"versionCode":1,"versionName":"a","apk":"a.apk","sha256":"$sha","future":true}"""))

    @Test fun rejectsBadOrIncompleteInfo() {
        assertNull(UpdateLogic.parse("not json"))
        assertNull(UpdateLogic.parse("""{"versionCode":0,"versionName":"a","apk":"a.apk","sha256":"$sha"}"""))
        assertNull(UpdateLogic.parse("""{"versionCode":5,"versionName":"a","apk":"","sha256":"$sha"}"""))
        assertNull(UpdateLogic.parse("""{"versionCode":5,"versionName":"a","apk":"a.apk","sha256":"short"}"""))
    }

    @Test fun newerOnlyWhenVersionCodeIsHigher() {
        val i = UpdateInfo(330, "1.0.330", "a.apk", sha)
        assertTrue(UpdateLogic.isNewer(i, 329))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isNewer(i, 330))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isNewer(i, 331))
    }

    @Test fun apkNameMustBeASafeSingleFile() {
        assertTrue(UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName("app-1.0.330_arm64.apk"))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName("../evil.apk"))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName("a/b.apk"))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName("evil.sh"))
        assertFalse(UpdateLogic.isSafeApkName(""))
    }

    @Test fun sha256OfKnownContent() {
        val f = File.createTempFile("sha", ".bin")
        try {
            f.writeText("abc")
            assertEquals("ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad", UpdateLogic.sha256Hex(f))
        } finally {
            f.delete()
        }
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\backup\BackupCodecTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.backup

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.fail
import org.junit.Test

private fun rec(
    id: String,
    updatedAt: Long = 100,
    deletedAt: Long? = null,
    type: RecordType = RecordType.WEIGHT,
    note: String = "",
    photoPath: String? = null,
) = HealthRecord(
    id = id, type = type, value = 70.5, value2 = if (type == RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE) 80.0 else null,
    measuredAt = 1_760_000_000_000, source = RecordSource.MIJIA, note = note, syncState = SyncState.SYNCED,
    createdAt = 10, updatedAt = updatedAt, deletedAt = deletedAt, photoPath = photoPath,
)

class BackupCodecTest {
    private val sample = listOf(
        rec("a", note = "晨間，含逗號與\"引號\"與換行\n第二行"),
        rec("b", type = RecordType.BLOOD_PRESSURE),
        rec("c", deletedAt = 150, updatedAt = 150),
    )

    @Test fun plainRoundTripKeepsEveryFieldIncludingDeleted() {
        val bytes = BackupCodec.encode(sample, null)
        assertFalse(BackupCodec.isEncrypted(bytes))
        val back = BackupCodec.decode(bytes, null).mapNotNull(BackupCodec::toHealthRecord)
        assertEquals(sample.map { it.copy(syncState = SyncState.PENDING, photoPath = null) }, back)
    }

    @Test fun measurementContextSurvivesBackupAndRestore() {
        val g = rec("g", type = RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE).copy(context = com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL)
        val back = BackupCodec.decode(BackupCodec.encode(listOf(g), "pw"), "pw").mapNotNull(BackupCodec::toHealthRecord)
        assertEquals(com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL, back.single().context)
    }

    @Test fun backupFromBeforeContextExistedStillRestores() {
        val json = """{"format":"healthtracker-backup","version":1,"createdAt":0,"encrypted":false,"records":[
            {"id":"y","type":"BLOOD_GLUCOSE","value":100.0,"measuredAt":1,"source":"MANUAL","createdAt":1,"updatedAt":1}]}"""
        assertNull(BackupCodec.decode(json.toByteArray(), null).mapNotNull(BackupCodec::toHealthRecord).single().context)
    }

    @Test fun photoPathAndSyncStateAreNotStoredInTheFile() {
        val text = String(BackupCodec.encode(listOf(rec("a", photoPath = "/data/user/0/x/photos/secret.jpg")), null), Charsets.UTF_8)
        assertFalse(text.contains("secret.jpg"))
        assertFalse(text.contains("syncState"))
        assertFalse(text.contains("photoPath"))
    }

    @Test fun encryptedRoundTrip() {
        val bytes = BackupCodec.encode(sample, "correct horse")
        assertTrue(BackupCodec.isEncrypted(bytes))
        assertEquals(sample.map { it.id }, BackupCodec.decode(bytes, "correct horse").map { it.id })
    }

    @Test fun encryptedFileHidesTheContent() {
        val text = String(BackupCodec.encode(listOf(rec("a", note = "很私密的備註")), "pw12345"), Charsets.UTF_8)
        assertFalse(text.contains("很私密的備註"))
        assertFalse(text.contains("70.5"))
    }

    @Test fun twoEncryptionsOfTheSameDataDiffer() {
        val a = String(BackupCodec.encode(sample, "pw"), Charsets.UTF_8)
        val b = String(BackupCodec.encode(sample, "pw"), Charsets.UTF_8)
        assertTrue(a != b) // 每次隨機 salt 與 iv
    }

    private fun expectError(contains: String, block: () -> Unit) {
        try {
            block()
            fail("expected BackupException containing $contains")
        } catch (e: BackupException) {
            assertTrue("實際訊息：${e.message}", e.message!!.contains(contains))
        }
    }

    @Test fun wrongPasswordIsRejected() {
        val bytes = BackupCodec.encode(sample, "right")
        expectError("密碼錯誤") { BackupCodec.decode(bytes, "wrong") }
    }

    @Test fun missingPasswordForEncryptedBackupAsksForIt() {
        val bytes = BackupCodec.encode(sample, "right")
        expectError("請輸入密碼") { BackupCodec.decode(bytes, "") }
        expectError("請輸入密碼") { BackupCodec.decode(bytes, null) }
    }

    @Test fun tamperedCiphertextIsRejectedInsteadOfRestoringWrongData() {
        val text = String(BackupCodec.encode(sample, "pw"), Charsets.UTF_8)
        val marker = "\"data\":\""
        val i = text.indexOf(marker) + marker.length
        val flipped = if (text[i] == 'A') 'B' else 'A'
        val tampered = (text.substring(0, i) + flipped + text.substring(i + 1)).toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8)
        try {
            BackupCodec.decode(tampered, "pw")
            fail("tampered data must not decode")
        } catch (e: BackupException) {
            // 預期：密碼錯誤或檔案損毀
        }
    }

    @Test fun garbageAndForeignFilesAreRejected() {
        expectError("不是有效的備份檔") { BackupCodec.decode("hello".toByteArray(), null) }
        expectError("不是有效的備份檔") { BackupCodec.decode(ByteArray(0), null) }
        expectError("不是本 App 的備份檔") { BackupCodec.decode("""{"format":"other","version":1,"createdAt":0,"encrypted":false,"records":[]}""".toByteArray(), null) }
    }

    @Test fun newerVersionIsRejectedWithUpdateHint() {
        expectError("較新的版本") {
            BackupCodec.decode("""{"format":"healthtracker-backup","version":99,"createdAt":0,"encrypted":false,"records":[]}""".toByteArray(), null)
        }
    }

    @Test fun unreasonableKdfParametersAreRejected() {
        val bytes = """{"format":"healthtracker-backup","version":1,"createdAt":0,"encrypted":true,"kdf":"PBKDF2WithHmacSHA256","iterations":1000000000,"salt":"AAAA","iv":"AAAA","data":"AAAA"}""".toByteArray()
        expectError("不合理") { BackupCodec.decode(bytes, "pw") }
    }

    @Test fun unknownRecordTypeFromNewerVersionIsSkippedNotFatal() {
        val json = """{"format":"healthtracker-backup","version":1,"createdAt":0,"encrypted":false,"records":[
            {"id":"x","type":"STEPS","value":1.0,"measuredAt":1,"source":"MANUAL","createdAt":1,"updatedAt":1},
            {"id":"y","type":"WEIGHT","value":70.0,"measuredAt":1,"source":"FUTURE_SOURCE","createdAt":1,"updatedAt":1}]}"""
        val decoded = BackupCodec.decode(json.toByteArray(), null)
        val mapped = decoded.mapNotNull(BackupCodec::toHealthRecord)
        assertEquals(2, decoded.size)
        assertEquals(listOf("y"), mapped.map { it.id })
        assertEquals(RecordSource.IMPORT, mapped[0].source)
    }

    @Test fun restoredRecordsAreMarkedPendingSoTheyUploadAgain() {
        val back = BackupCodec.decode(BackupCodec.encode(listOf(rec("a")), null), null).mapNotNull(BackupCodec::toHealthRecord)
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, back.single().syncState)
        assertNull(back.single().photoPath)
    }

    @Test fun planRestoreInsertsMissingUpdatesNewerAndSkipsOlderOrEqual() {
        assertEquals(RestoreAction.INSERT, planRestore(null, rec("a")))
        assertEquals(RestoreAction.UPDATE, planRestore(rec("a", updatedAt = 100), rec("a", updatedAt = 200)))
        assertEquals(RestoreAction.SKIP, planRestore(rec("a", updatedAt = 200), rec("a", updatedAt = 100)))
        assertEquals(RestoreAction.SKIP, planRestore(rec("a", updatedAt = 100), rec("a", updatedAt = 100)))
    }

    @Test fun oldBackupDoesNotResurrectARecordDeletedLater() {
        val deletedLocally = rec("a", updatedAt = 500, deletedAt = 500)
        val fromBackup = rec("a", updatedAt = 100)
        assertEquals(RestoreAction.SKIP, planRestore(deletedLocally, fromBackup))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\cloud\CloudUrlTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

/** 使用者貼上 /rest/v1 等端點時，必須自動收斂成專案根網址，避免 /rest/v1/rest/v1 造成 404。 */
class CloudUrlTest {
    @Test fun stripsApiSuffixesAndPaths() {
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/rest/v1"))
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/rest/v1/"))
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/auth/v1"))
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/storage/v1"))
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/rest/v1/health_records"))
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl("https://abc.supabase.co/"))
    }

    @Test fun keepsPlainBaseAndTrimsWhitespace() {
        assertEquals("https://abc.supabase.co", normalizeSupabaseUrl(" https://abc.supabase.co "))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\cloud\SupabaseApiTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud

import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import kotlinx.serialization.json.Json
import kotlinx.serialization.json.JsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonObject
import kotlinx.serialization.json.jsonPrimitive
import okhttp3.mockwebserver.MockResponse
import okhttp3.mockwebserver.MockWebServer
import org.junit.After
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Assert.fail
import org.junit.Before
import org.junit.Test

class SupabaseApiTest {
    private lateinit var server: MockWebServer
    private lateinit var api: SupabaseApi

    @Before fun setUp() {
        server = MockWebServer().apply { start() }
        api = SupabaseApi(server.url("/").toString().trimEnd('/'), "anon-key", clock = { 5_000L })
    }

    @After fun tearDown() = server.shutdown()

    private fun recordJson(id: String, syncedAt: String) =
        """{"id":"$id","user_id":"u","type":"WEIGHT","value":70.5,"value2":null,"measured_at":1,"source":"MANUAL","note":"","created_at":2,"updated_at":3,"deleted_at":null,"photo_object":null,"synced_at":"$syncedAt"}"""

    private suspend fun expectKind(kind: CloudErrorKind, block: suspend () -> Unit) {
        try {
            block()
            fail("expected CloudException($kind)")
        } catch (e: CloudException) {
            assertEquals(kind, e.kind)
        }
    }

    @Test fun signInPostsCredentialsAndParsesSession() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody("""{"access_token":"A","refresh_token":"R","expires_in":3600,"token_type":"bearer","user":{"id":"uid","email":"me@example.com"}}"""))
        val s = api.signIn("me@example.com", "pw")
        val req = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password", req.path)
        assertEquals("anon-key", req.getHeader("apikey"))
        val body = Json.parseToJsonElement(req.body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        assertEquals("me@example.com", body["email"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
        assertEquals("pw", body["password"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
        assertEquals(Session("A", "R", 5_000L + 3_600_000L, "uid", "me@example.com"), s)
    }

    @Test fun refreshUsesRefreshTokenGrant() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody("""{"access_token":"A2","refresh_token":"R2","expires_in":60,"user":{"id":"uid"}}"""))
        val s = api.refresh("R1")
        val req = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("/auth/v1/token?grant_type=refresh_token", req.path)
        assertEquals("R1", Json.parseToJsonElement(req.body.readUtf8()).jsonObject["refresh_token"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
        assertEquals("A2", s.accessToken)
        assertNull(s.email)
    }

    @Test fun wrongPasswordIsAuthFailed() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(400).setBody("""{"error_code":"invalid_credentials"}"""))
        expectKind(CloudErrorKind.AUTH_FAILED) { api.signIn("a@b.c", "bad") }
    }

    @Test fun fetchChangesSendsAuthAndCursorEncoded() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody("[" + recordJson("r1", "2026-10-19T00:00:01+00:00") + "]"))
        val rows = api.fetchChanges("tok", "2026-10-19T00:00:00.5+00:00")
        val req = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("Bearer tok", req.getHeader("Authorization"))
        assertEquals("anon-key", req.getHeader("apikey"))
        assertEquals("order=synced_at.asc", req.requestUrl!!.encodedQuery!!.split("&").first { it.startsWith("order") })
        assertEquals("gte.2026-10-19T00:00:00.5+00:00", req.requestUrl!!.queryParameter("synced_at"))
        assertTrue("加號必須編碼成 %2B，否則時區會被讀成空白", req.path!!.contains("%2B00%3A00"))
        assertEquals(1, rows.size)
        assertEquals(70.5, rows[0].value, 0.0)
        assertNull(rows[0].value2)
    }

    @Test fun fetchChangesWithoutCursorOmitsFilter() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody("[]"))
        api.fetchChanges("tok", null)
        assertNull(server.takeRequest().requestUrl!!.queryParameter("synced_at"))
    }

    @Test fun fetchChangesFollowsPages() = runTest {
        val page1 = (1..500).joinToString(",", "[", "]") { recordJson("a$it", "2026-10-19T00:00:01+00:00") }
        val page2 = "[" + recordJson("b1", "2026-10-19T00:00:02+00:00") + "]"
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody(page1))
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setBody(page2))
        val rows = api.fetchChanges("tok", null)
        assertEquals(501, rows.size)
        server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("gte.2026-10-19T00:00:01+00:00", server.takeRequest().requestUrl!!.queryParameter("synced_at"))
    }

    @Test fun unauthorizedMapsToUnauthorized() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(401).setBody("""{"code":"PGRST301"}"""))
        expectKind(CloudErrorKind.UNAUTHORIZED) { api.fetchChanges("old", null) }
    }

    @Test fun missingTableHintsToRunSchema() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(404).setBody("{}"))
        try {
            api.fetchChanges("tok", null)
            fail()
        } catch (e: CloudException) {
            assertEquals(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, e.kind)
            assertTrue(e.message!!.contains("schema.sql"))
        }
    }

    @Test fun upsertUsesMergeDuplicatesAndOmitsSyncedAt() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(201))
        api.upsert("tok", RemoteRecord("r1", "WEIGHT", 70.0, null, 1, "MANUAL", "", 2, 3, null, null, syncedAt = "ignored"))
        val req = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("POST", req.method)
        assertEquals("/rest/v1/health_records?on_conflict=id", req.path)
        assertEquals("resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal", req.getHeader("Prefer"))
        val body: JsonObject = Json.parseToJsonElement(req.body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        assertFalse(body.containsKey("synced_at"))
        assertTrue("null 欄位要明確送出，避免部分更新", body.containsKey("deleted_at") && body.containsKey("value2"))
        assertEquals("r1", body["id"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
    }

    @Test fun upsertOmitsPhotoObjectWhenNullAndSendsItWhenSet() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(201))
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(201))
        api.upsert("tok", RemoteRecord("r1", "WEIGHT", 70.0, null, 1, "MANUAL", "", 2, 3, null, null))
        api.upsert("tok", RemoteRecord("r2", "BLOOD_GLUCOSE", 100.0, null, 1, "OCR", "", 2, 3, null, "u/r2.jpg"))
        val first: JsonObject = Json.parseToJsonElement(server.takeRequest().body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        val second: JsonObject = Json.parseToJsonElement(server.takeRequest().body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        assertFalse("省略欄位才不會蓋掉雲端原有的照片連結", first.containsKey("photo_object"))
        assertEquals("u/r2.jpg", second["photo_object"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
    }

    @Test fun upsertSendsContextOnlyWhenSet() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(201))
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(201))
        api.upsert("tok", RemoteRecord("r1", "BLOOD_GLUCOSE", 100.0, null, 1, "MANUAL", "", 2, 3, null, null))
        api.upsert("tok", RemoteRecord("r2", "BLOOD_GLUCOSE", 100.0, null, 1, "MANUAL", "", 2, 3, null, null, context = "FASTING"))
        val first: JsonObject = Json.parseToJsonElement(server.takeRequest().body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        val second: JsonObject = Json.parseToJsonElement(server.takeRequest().body.readUtf8()).jsonObject
        assertFalse("沒填情境就省略，雲端沒有 context 欄位時也能同步", first.containsKey("context"))
        assertEquals("FASTING", second["context"]!!.jsonPrimitive.content)
    }

    @Test fun uploadPhotoSendsBytesWithUpsertHeader() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(200))
        api.uploadPhoto("tok", "uid/r1.jpg", byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3))
        val req = server.takeRequest()
        assertEquals("/storage/v1/object/meter-photos/uid/r1.jpg", req.path)
        assertEquals("true", req.getHeader("x-upsert"))
        assertEquals("image/jpeg", req.getHeader("Content-Type"))
        assertEquals(3L, req.bodySize)
    }

    @Test fun deletePhotoTreats404AsSuccess() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(404))
        api.deletePhoto("tok", "uid/gone.jpg")
        assertEquals("DELETE", server.takeRequest().method)
    }

    @Test fun deletePhotoServerErrorIsReported() = runTest {
        server.enqueue(MockResponse().setResponseCode(500))
        expectKind(CloudErrorKind.SERVER) { api.deletePhoto("tok", "uid/x.jpg") }
    }

    @Test fun unreachableServerIsNetworkError() = runTest {
        server.shutdown()
        expectKind(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK) { api.fetchChanges("tok", null) }
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\cloud\SyncEngineTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.cloud

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import kotlinx.coroutines.test.runTest
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertFalse
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test

private const val NOW = 1_000_000_000L
private const val USER = "user-1"

private fun local(
    id: String,
    updatedAt: Long = 100,
    state: SyncState = SyncState.PENDING,
    source: RecordSource = RecordSource.MANUAL,
    deletedAt: Long? = null,
    photoPath: String? = null,
    value: Double = 70.0,
) = HealthRecord(
    id = id, type = RecordType.WEIGHT, value = value, measuredAt = 50, source = source,
    syncState = state, createdAt = 10, updatedAt = updatedAt, deletedAt = deletedAt, photoPath = photoPath,
)

private fun remote(id: String, updatedAt: Long = 100, syncedAt: String? = "2026-10-19T00:00:01+00:00", type: String = "WEIGHT", value: Double = 71.0) =
    RemoteRecord(id = id, type = type, value = value, measuredAt = 50, createdAt = 10, updatedAt = updatedAt, syncedAt = syncedAt)

private class FakeStore : SyncStore {
    val rows = LinkedHashMap<String, HealthRecord>()
    /** 模擬「推送期間使用者又改了這筆」：upsert 時改動 updatedAt。 */
    var onUpsert: (String) -> Unit = {}
    override suspend fun pending() = rows.values.filter { it.syncState != SyncState.SYNCED }
    override suspend fun get(id: String) = rows[id]
    override suspend fun saveFromRemote(record: HealthRecord) { rows[record.id] = record }
    override suspend fun markSynced(id: String, updatedAt: Long): Boolean {
        val r = rows[id] ?: return false
        if (r.updatedAt != updatedAt) return false
        rows[id] = r.copy(syncState = SyncState.SYNCED)
        return true
    }
}

private class FakeSessions(var session: Session? = null) : SessionStore {
    var cursorValue: String? = null
    var lastSync: Long? = null
    var lastResultValue: String? = null
    override suspend fun load() = session
    override suspend fun save(session: Session?) { this.session = session }
    override suspend fun cursor() = cursorValue
    override suspend fun setCursor(cursor: String?) { cursorValue = cursor }
    override suspend fun lastSyncAt() = lastSync
    override suspend fun setLastSyncAt(millis: Long) { lastSync = millis }
    override suspend fun lastResult() = lastResultValue
    override suspend fun setLastResult(message: String?) { lastResultValue = message }
}

private class FakeApi(val store: FakeStore? = null) : RemoteApi {
    val remoteRows = LinkedHashMap<String, RemoteRecord>()
    val upserted = ArrayList<RemoteRecord>()
    val uploaded = ArrayList<Pair<String, Int>>()
    val deletedPhotos = ArrayList<String>()
    val fetchCursors = ArrayList<String?>()
    var tokensSeen = ArrayList<String>()
    var refreshCalls = 0
    var upsertFailures = HashMap<String, CloudException>()
    var fetchFailure: CloudException? = null
    var unauthorizedOnce = false
    var refreshFailure: CloudException? = null
    var onUpsert: (String) -> Unit = {}

    override suspend fun signIn(email: String, password: String) = Session("a0", "r0", NOW + 3_600_000, USER, email)
    override suspend fun refresh(refreshToken: String): Session {
        refreshCalls++
        refreshFailure?.let { throw it }
        return Session("a${refreshCalls}", "r${refreshCalls}", NOW + 3_600_000, USER, "me@example.com")
    }
    override suspend fun fetchChanges(accessToken: String, sinceCursor: String?): List<RemoteRecord> {
        tokensSeen += accessToken
        if (unauthorizedOnce) { unauthorizedOnce = false; throw CloudException(CloudErrorKind.UNAUTHORIZED, "expired", 401) }
        fetchFailure?.let { throw it }
        fetchCursors += sinceCursor
        return remoteRows.values.toList()
    }
    override suspend fun upsert(accessToken: String, record: RemoteRecord) {
        upsertFailures[record.id]?.let { throw it }
        upserted += record
        remoteRows[record.id] = record
        onUpsert(record.id)
    }
    override suspend fun uploadPhoto(accessToken: String, objectPath: String, bytes: ByteArray) { uploaded += objectPath to bytes.size }
    override suspend fun deletePhoto(accessToken: String, objectPath: String) { deletedPhotos += objectPath }
    val cloudPhotos = HashMap<String, ByteArray>()
    val downloaded = ArrayList<String>()
    var downloadFailure: CloudException? = null
    override suspend fun downloadPhoto(accessToken: String, objectPath: String): ByteArray? {
        downloadFailure?.let { throw it }
        downloaded += objectPath
        return cloudPhotos[objectPath]
    }
}

class SyncEngineTest {
    private val store = FakeStore()
    private val sessions = FakeSessions(Session("a", "r", NOW + 3_600_000, USER, "me@example.com"))
    private val api = FakeApi()
    private fun engine(readPhoto: (String) -> ByteArray? = { null }) = SyncEngine(store, api, sessions, readPhoto) { NOW }

    @Test fun notSignedInReturnsErrorWithoutCalling() = runTest {
        sessions.session = null
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals("尚未登入雲端", r.error)
        assertTrue(api.fetchCursors.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun pullInsertsNewRemoteRecordsAsSynced() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["r1"] = remote("r1")
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertEquals(1, r.pulled)
        assertEquals(SyncState.SYNCED, store.rows["r1"]!!.syncState)
        assertEquals(71.0, store.rows["r1"]!!.value, 0.0)
        assertEquals(NOW, sessions.lastSync)
    }

    private fun engineWithPhotos(saved: MutableMap<String, String>) = SyncEngine(
        store, api, sessions,
        savePhoto = { id, _ -> "/photos/$id.jpg".also { saved[id] = it } },
        photoExists = { it != null },
        clock = { NOW },
    )

    @Test fun newDevicePullDownloadsPhotosOfOldRecords() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["p1"] = remote("p1").copy(source = "OCR", photoObject = "$USER/p1.jpg")
        api.cloudPhotos["$USER/p1.jpg"] = ByteArray(10)
        val saved = HashMap<String, String>()
        val r = engineWithPhotos(saved).sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertEquals("/photos/p1.jpg", store.rows["p1"]!!.photoPath)
        assertEquals(SyncState.SYNCED, store.rows["p1"]!!.syncState)
    }

    @Test fun existingLocalPhotoIsNotDownloadedAgain() = runTest {
        store.rows["p2"] = local("p2", state = SyncState.SYNCED, photoPath = "/photos/p2.jpg")
        api.remoteRows["p2"] = remote("p2").copy(photoObject = "$USER/p2.jpg")
        engineWithPhotos(HashMap()).sync()
        assertTrue(api.downloaded.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun missingCloudPhotoStillKeepsTheRecord() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["p3"] = remote("p3").copy(photoObject = "$USER/p3.jpg")
        val r = engineWithPhotos(HashMap()).sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertNull(store.rows["p3"]!!.photoPath)
        assertNotNull(store.rows["p3"])
    }

    @Test fun photoServerErrorDoesNotFailTheSync() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["p4"] = remote("p4").copy(photoObject = "$USER/p4.jpg")
        api.downloadFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "boom", 500)
        val r = engineWithPhotos(HashMap()).sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertNotNull(store.rows["p4"])
    }

    @Test fun photoNetworkErrorAbortsSoItRetriesLater() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["p5"] = remote("p5").copy(photoObject = "$USER/p5.jpg")
        api.downloadFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "offline")
        val r = engineWithPhotos(HashMap()).sync()
        assertFalse(r.ok)
        assertNull(sessions.cursorValue) // 游標沒前進，下次會重新拉
    }

    @Test fun remoteNewerOverwritesLocalButKeepsLocalPhoto() = runTest {
        store.rows["x"] = local("x", updatedAt = 100, state = SyncState.SYNCED, photoPath = "/p/x.jpg")
        api.remoteRows["x"] = remote("x", updatedAt = 200)
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals(200, store.rows["x"]!!.updatedAt)
        assertEquals("/p/x.jpg", store.rows["x"]!!.photoPath)
    }

    @Test fun localNewerIsPushedNotOverwritten() = runTest {
        store.rows["x"] = local("x", updatedAt = 300, value = 65.0)
        api.remoteRows["x"] = remote("x", updatedAt = 200)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals(65.0, store.rows["x"]!!.value, 0.0)
        assertEquals(1, r.pushed)
        assertEquals(65.0, api.remoteRows["x"]!!.value, 0.0)
        assertEquals(SyncState.SYNCED, store.rows["x"]!!.syncState)
    }

    @Test fun sameVersionOnBothSidesJustMarksSynced() = runTest {
        store.rows["x"] = local("x", updatedAt = 100)
        api.remoteRows["x"] = remote("x", updatedAt = 100)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals(SyncState.SYNCED, store.rows["x"]!!.syncState)
        assertEquals(0, r.pushed) // 拉取時已標為同步，不必再推
    }

    @Test fun unknownRemoteTypeIsSkipped() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["u"] = remote("u", type = "STEPS")
        api.remoteRows["k"] = remote("k")
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertNull(store.rows["u"])
        assertNotNull(store.rows["k"])
    }

    @Test fun cursorAdvancesAndIsSentNextTime() = runTest {
        api.remoteRows["a"] = remote("a", syncedAt = "2026-10-19T00:00:01+00:00")
        api.remoteRows["b"] = remote("b", syncedAt = "2026-10-19T00:00:09+00:00")
        val e = engine()
        e.sync()
        e.sync()
        assertEquals(listOf<String?>(null, "2026-10-19T00:00:09+00:00"), api.fetchCursors)
    }

    @Test fun pendingRecordsArePushedAndMarkedSynced() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        store.rows["b"] = local("b", state = SyncState.LOCAL_ONLY)
        store.rows["c"] = local("c", state = SyncState.SYNCED)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals(2, r.pushed)
        assertEquals(setOf("a", "b"), api.upserted.map { it.id }.toSet())
        assertTrue(store.rows.values.all { it.syncState == SyncState.SYNCED })
    }

    @Test fun editDuringPushStaysPending() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a", updatedAt = 100)
        api.onUpsert = { id -> store.rows[id] = store.rows[id]!!.copy(updatedAt = 150) }
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, store.rows["a"]!!.syncState)
    }

    @Test fun ocrRecordUploadsPhotoToUserFolder() = runTest {
        store.rows["g"] = local("g", source = RecordSource.OCR, photoPath = "/p/g.jpg")
        engine { byteArrayOf(1, 2, 3) }.sync()
        assertEquals(listOf("$USER/g.jpg" to 3), api.uploaded)
        assertEquals("$USER/g.jpg", api.upserted.single().photoObject)
    }

    @Test fun missingPhotoFileStillSyncsRecord() = runTest {
        store.rows["g"] = local("g", source = RecordSource.OCR, photoPath = "/gone.jpg")
        val r = engine { null }.sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertTrue(api.uploaded.isEmpty())
        assertEquals(1, api.upserted.size)
        assertNull("沒有真的上傳照片，就不能帶照片連結", api.upserted.single().photoObject)
    }

    @Test fun restoredOcrRecordWithoutLocalPhotoSendsNoPhotoLink() = runTest {
        store.rows["g"] = local("g", source = RecordSource.OCR, photoPath = null)
        engine { error("不該讀照片") }.sync()
        assertTrue(api.uploaded.isEmpty())
        assertNull(api.upserted.single().photoObject)
    }

    @Test fun contextIsPushedAndPulled() = runTest {
        store.rows["c"] = local("c").copy(context = com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.BEDTIME)
        api.remoteRows["p"] = remote("p").copy(type = "BLOOD_GLUCOSE", context = "AFTER_MEAL")
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals("BEDTIME", api.upserted.single { it.id == "c" }.context)
        assertEquals(com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL, store.rows["p"]!!.context)
    }

    @Test fun deletedOcrRecordRemovesRemotePhoto() = runTest {
        store.rows["g"] = local("g", source = RecordSource.OCR, deletedAt = 120, photoPath = "/p/g.jpg")
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals(listOf("$USER/g.jpg"), api.deletedPhotos)
        assertEquals(120L, api.upserted.single().deletedAt)
    }

    @Test fun manualRecordPushHasNoPhotoObject() = runTest {
        store.rows["m"] = local("m")
        engine().sync()
        assertNull(api.upserted.single().photoObject)
        assertTrue(api.deletedPhotos.isEmpty())
    }

    @Test fun networkErrorAbortsAndKeepsPending() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        api.fetchFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "無法連線到雲端")
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals("無法連線到雲端", r.error)
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, store.rows["a"]!!.syncState)
        assertNull(sessions.lastSync)
    }

    @Test fun networkErrorDuringPushStopsRemaining() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        store.rows["b"] = local("b")
        api.upsertFailures["a"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "斷線")
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals("斷線", r.error)
        assertTrue(api.upserted.isEmpty())
        assertEquals(0, r.pushed)
    }

    @Test fun oneServerErrorDoesNotBlockOthers() = runTest {
        store.rows["bad"] = local("bad")
        store.rows["good"] = local("good")
        api.upsertFailures["bad"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "伺服器錯誤（HTTP 500）", 500)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals(1, r.pushed)
        assertEquals(1, r.failed)
        assertFalse(r.ok)
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, store.rows["bad"]!!.syncState)
        assertEquals(SyncState.SYNCED, store.rows["good"]!!.syncState)
    }

    @Test fun expiredTokenIsRefreshedBeforeUse() = runTest {
        sessions.session = Session("old", "r", NOW + 10_000, USER, null) // 不到 60 秒就過期
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals(1, api.refreshCalls)
        assertEquals("a1", api.tokensSeen.first())
        assertEquals("a1", sessions.session!!.accessToken)
    }

    @Test fun unauthorizedTriggersOneRefreshAndRetry() = runTest {
        api.unauthorizedOnce = true
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertTrue(r.ok)
        assertEquals(1, api.refreshCalls)
        assertEquals(listOf("a", "a1"), api.tokensSeen)
    }

    @Test fun failedRefreshClearsSessionAndAsksToSignInAgain() = runTest {
        sessions.session = Session("old", "r", NOW - 1, USER, null)
        api.refreshFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.AUTH_FAILED, "bad refresh", 400)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals("登入已失效，請重新登入", r.error)
        assertNull(sessions.session)
    }

    @Test fun networkFailureIsRetryableAndAuthFailureIsNot() = runTest {
        api.fetchFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "斷線")
        assertTrue(engine().sync().retryable)
        api.fetchFailure = null
        sessions.session = null
        assertFalse(engine().sync().retryable)
    }

    @Test fun serverErrorOnOneRecordIsRetryable() = runTest {
        store.rows["bad"] = local("bad")
        api.upsertFailures["bad"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "伺服器錯誤（HTTP 500）", 500)
        assertTrue(engine().sync().retryable)
    }

    @Test fun permissionAndMissingTableErrorsAreNotRetryable() = runTest {
        for (code in listOf(400, 403, 404, 409)) {
            store.rows["bad"] = local("bad")
            api.upsertFailures["bad"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "HTTP $code", code)
            val r = engine().sync()
            assertFalse("HTTP $code 不該重試", r.retryable)
            assertEquals(1, r.failed)
            api.fetchFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "HTTP $code", code)
            assertFalse("拉取 HTTP $code 不該重試", engine().sync().retryable)
            api.fetchFailure = null
        }
    }

    @Test fun serverOverloadAndRateLimitAreRetryable() = runTest {
        for (code in listOf(408, 429, 500, 502, 503)) {
            api.fetchFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "HTTP $code", code)
            assertTrue("HTTP $code 應該重試", engine().sync().retryable)
        }
    }

    @Test fun mixedFailuresAreRetryableIfAnyIs() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        store.rows["b"] = local("b")
        api.upsertFailures["a"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "HTTP 403", 403)
        api.upsertFailures["b"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "HTTP 503", 503)
        val r = engine().sync()
        assertEquals(2, r.failed)
        assertTrue(r.retryable)
    }

    @Test fun successfulSyncIsNotRetryable() = runTest {
        assertFalse(engine().sync().retryable)
    }

    @Test fun lastResultIsRecordedForBothSuccessAndFailure() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals("同步完成：下載 0 筆、上傳 1 筆", sessions.lastResultValue)
        api.fetchFailure = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.NETWORK, "無法連線到雲端")
        engine().sync()
        assertEquals("無法連線到雲端", sessions.lastResultValue)
    }

    @Test fun hasPendingReflectsStore() = runTest {
        assertFalse(engine().hasPending())
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        assertTrue(engine().hasPending())
    }

    @Test fun failedSyncDoesNotAdvanceLastSyncTime() = runTest {
        store.rows["bad"] = local("bad")
        api.upsertFailures["bad"] = CloudException(CloudErrorKind.SERVER, "x", 500)
        engine().sync()
        assertNull(sessions.lastSync)
    }

    @Test fun signOutClearsSessionAndCursorButKeepsLocalData() = runTest {
        store.rows["a"] = local("a")
        sessions.cursorValue = "c"
        engine().signOut()
        assertNull(sessions.session)
        assertNull(sessions.cursorValue)
        assertEquals(1, store.rows.size)
    }

    @Test fun signInStoresSessionAndResetsCursor() = runTest {
        sessions.session = null
        sessions.cursorValue = "stale"
        engine().signIn("  me@example.com ", "pw")
        assertEquals("me@example.com", sessions.session!!.email)
        assertNull(sessions.cursorValue)
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\importer\ImporterTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.importer

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.SyncState
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.ZoneId

private val TAIPEI = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")
private const val NOW = 1_700_000_000_000L

class CsvImporterTest {
    private fun parse(text: String) = CsvImporter.parse(text, TAIPEI, NOW)

    @Test fun parsesEnglishHeaderAndRows() {
        val r = parse("measured_at,type,value,note\n2026-10-19 09:27,WEIGHT,70.5,晨起\n2026-10-19 09:30,BODY_FAT,21.3,\n")
        assertTrue(r.errors.isEmpty())
        assertEquals(2, r.records.size)
        val w = r.records[0]
        assertEquals(RecordType.WEIGHT, w.type)
        assertEquals(70.5, w.value, 0.0)
        assertEquals("晨起", w.note)
        assertEquals(RecordSource.IMPORT, w.source)
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, w.syncState)
        // 2026-10-19 09:27 台北時間 = 01:27 UTC
        assertEquals(java.time.Instant.parse("2026-10-19T01:27:00Z").toEpochMilli(), w.measuredAt)
    }

    @Test fun parsesChineseHeaderAndTypes() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026/10/19 08:00,體重,69.8\n2026/10/19 08:01,體脂率,20\n2026/10/19 08:05,血糖,95")
        assertTrue(r.errors.isEmpty())
        assertEquals(listOf(RecordType.WEIGHT, RecordType.BODY_FAT, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE), r.records.map { it.type })
    }

    @Test fun glucoseContextColumnIsParsedByLabelOrCode() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值,情境\n2026-10-19 07:00,血糖,95,空腹\n2026-10-19 09:00,血糖,130,after_meal\n2026-10-19 10:00,血糖,100,\n2026-10-19 10:05,體重,70,空腹")
        assertTrue(r.errors.isEmpty())
        assertEquals(
            listOf(com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.FASTING, com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.MeasureContext.AFTER_MEAL, null, null),
            r.records.map { it.context },
        )
    }

    @Test fun unknownContextIsReportedWithLineNumber() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值,情境\n2026-10-19 07:00,血糖,95,下午茶")
        assertTrue(r.records.isEmpty())
        assertTrue(r.errors.single().startsWith("第 2 列") && r.errors.single().contains("情境"))
    }

    @Test fun bloodPressureNeedsValue2() {
        val r = parse("time,type,value,value2\n2026-10-19 08:00,血壓,120,80\n2026-10-19 08:10,血壓,120,")
        assertEquals(1, r.records.size)
        assertEquals(80.0, r.records[0].value2!!, 0.0)
        assertEquals(1, r.errors.size)
        assertTrue(r.errors[0].startsWith("第 3 列"))
    }

    @Test fun badRowsReportedWithLineNumbersAndOthersStillImported() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,705\n昨天,體重,70\n2026-10-19 08:00,心情,5\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70")
        assertEquals(1, r.records.size)
        assertEquals(3, r.errors.size)
        assertTrue(r.errors[0].startsWith("第 2 列"))
        assertTrue(r.errors[1].startsWith("第 3 列"))
        assertTrue(r.errors[2].startsWith("第 4 列"))
    }

    @Test fun missingRequiredColumnsRejected() {
        val r = parse("date,weight\n2026-10-19,70")
        assertTrue(r.records.isEmpty())
        assertTrue(r.errors.single().contains("標題列"))
    }

    @Test fun emptyFile() = assertEquals("檔案是空的", parse("").errors.single())

    @Test fun bomAndCrLfAndBlankLinesHandled() {
        val r = parse("﻿時間,類型,數值\r\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70\r\n\r\n2026-10-20 08:00,體重,71\r\n")
        assertEquals(2, r.records.size)
    }

    @Test fun quotedFieldsWithCommasAndEscapedQuotes() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值,備註\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70,\"早餐前, \"\"空腹\"\"\"")
        assertEquals("早餐前, \"空腹\"", r.records.single().note)
    }

    @Test fun dateOnlyMeansMidnight() {
        val r = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026-10-19,體重,70")
        assertEquals(java.time.Instant.parse("2026-10-18T16:00:00Z").toEpochMilli(), r.records.single().measuredAt)
    }

    @Test fun epochMillisAccepted() {
        assertEquals(1_760_000_000_000L, CsvImporter.parseTime("1760000000000", TAIPEI))
        assertNull(CsvImporter.parseTime("12345", TAIPEI))
    }

    @Test fun sameRowGivesSameIdAndDifferentRowDifferentId() {
        val a = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70").records.single().id
        val b = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70").records.single().id
        val c = parse("時間,類型,數值\n2026-10-19 08:00,體重,70.1").records.single().id
        assertEquals(a, b)
        assertNotEquals(a, c)
    }
}

class HealthConnectMappingTest {
    private fun sample(id: String, type: RecordType, v: Double, origin: String = "com.example.scale") =
        HcSample(id, type, v, 1_000, origin)

    @Test fun mapsWeightWithDeterministicIdAndOriginNote() {
        val r = HealthConnectMapping.toRecord(sample("hc-1", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.2), NOW)!!
        assertEquals(ImportIds.fromHealthConnect("hc-1"), r.id)
        assertEquals(RecordSource.HEALTH_CONNECT, r.source)
        assertEquals("來源：com.example.scale", r.note)
        assertEquals(SyncState.PENDING, r.syncState)
    }

    @Test fun sameHealthConnectIdAlwaysSameRecordId() {
        assertEquals(ImportIds.fromHealthConnect("x"), ImportIds.fromHealthConnect("x"))
        assertNotEquals(ImportIds.fromHealthConnect("x"), ImportIds.fromHealthConnect("y"))
    }

    @Test fun absurdValuesAreDropped() {
        assertNull(HealthConnectMapping.toRecord(sample("a", RecordType.WEIGHT, 7050.0), NOW))
        assertNull(HealthConnectMapping.toRecord(sample("b", RecordType.BODY_FAT, 0.2), NOW))
    }

    @Test fun countsPerOrigin() {
        val counts = HealthConnectMapping.countByOrigin(
            listOf(
                sample("1", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.0, "b.app"),
                sample("2", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.0, "a.app"),
                sample("3", RecordType.BODY_FAT, 20.0, "b.app"),
            ),
        )
        assertEquals(mapOf("a.app" to 1, "b.app" to 2), counts)
        assertEquals(listOf("a.app", "b.app"), counts.keys.toList())
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\mijia\MijiaScreenParserTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.mijia

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordSource
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneId

private val TAIPEI = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")
private val NOW = Instant.parse("2026-10-19T04:00:00Z").toEpochMilli()

private fun t(text: String, l: Int, tp: Int, r: Int, b: Int) = UiText(text, l, tp, r, b)

/** 依真實 S400 頁面的元件位置結構編寫（數值為虛構）。 */
private fun s400Page(intPart: String = "68", dec: String = ".4", time: String = "2026/10/19 06:33", fat: String = "22", title: String = "Xiaomi 體脂計 S400") = listOf(
    t(title, 365, 165, 867, 236),
    t("中斷連線", 216, 236, 1016, 291),
    t("$intPart ", 425, 612, 733, 899),
    t("kg ", 704, 666, 770, 731),
    t("$dec ", 676, 731, 798, 875),
    t(time, 439, 869, 782, 925),
    t("偏高", 572, 943, 650, 995),
    t("相比1日前上升0.2kg", 432, 1001, 789, 1053),
    t("25.1", 96, 2169, 415, 2281), t("BMI", 96, 2281, 415, 2335),
    t(fat, 451, 2169, 547, 2281), t("%", 553, 2214, 587, 2281), t("體脂率", 451, 2281, 769, 2335),
    t("51.1", 805, 2169, 937, 2281), t("kg", 943, 2210, 989, 2266), t("肌肉量", 805, 2281, 1124, 2335),
)

class MijiaScreenParserTest {
    private fun parse(texts: List<UiText>) = MijiaScreenParser.parse(texts, TAIPEI, NOW)

    @Test fun readsWeightFatAndTime() {
        val r = parse(s400Page())!!
        assertEquals(68.4, r.weightKg!!, 0.0)
        assertEquals(22.0, r.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)
        assertEquals(Instant.parse("2026-10-18T22:33:00Z").toEpochMilli(), r.measuredAt) // 台北 06:33
    }

    @Test fun bmiAndMuscleMassAreNotMistakenForBodyFat() {
        assertEquals(22.0, parse(s400Page(fat = "22"))!!.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun threeDigitWeight() = assertEquals(102.3, parse(s400Page(intPart = "102", dec = ".3"))!!.weightKg!!, 0.0)

    @Test fun decimalBodyFat() = assertEquals(21.7, parse(s400Page(fat = "21.7"))!!.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)

    @Test fun singleNodeWeightAlsoWorks() {
        val page = s400Page().filterNot { it.text.trim() in listOf("68", ".4") } + t("68.4", 425, 612, 733, 899)
        assertEquals(68.4, parse(page)!!.weightKg!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun otherPagesAreIgnored() {
        assertNull(parse(s400Page(title = "我的家")))
        assertNull(parse(listOf(t("米家", 0, 0, 100, 50), t("70", 0, 100, 50, 200))))
    }

    @Test fun missingTimeMeansNoReading() = assertNull(parse(s400Page().filterNot { it.text.startsWith("2026") }))

    @Test fun futureTimestampRejected() = assertNull(parse(s400Page(time = "2026/10/25 06:33")))

    @Test fun noNumbersMeansNoReading() {
        val page = s400Page().filter { it.text.trim() !in listOf("68", ".4", "22") }
        assertNull(parse(page))
    }

    @Test fun weightOnlyWhenFatLabelMissing() {
        val r = parse(s400Page().filterNot { it.text == "體脂率" })!!
        assertNotNull(r.weightKg)
        assertNull(r.bodyFatPercent)
    }

    @Test fun englishLabelsSupported() {
        val page = s400Page(title = "Xiaomi Body Composition Scale S400").map { if (it.text == "體脂率") it.copy(text = "Body fat") else it }
        assertEquals(22.0, parse(page)!!.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun recordsHaveStableIdsAndMijiaSource() {
        val reading = parse(s400Page())!!
        val a = MijiaScreenParser.toRecords(reading, NOW)
        val b = MijiaScreenParser.toRecords(reading, NOW + 5_000)
        assertEquals(listOf(RecordType.WEIGHT, RecordType.BODY_FAT), a.map { it.type })
        assertEquals(a.map { it.id }, b.map { it.id })
        assertTrue(a.all { it.source == RecordSource.MIJIA })
    }

    @Test fun absurdValuesDroppedWhenConverting() {
        val recs = MijiaScreenParser.toRecords(MijiaReading(NOW, 700.0, 1.0), NOW)
        assertTrue(recs.isEmpty())
    }

    // ---- 寬鬆解析：米家常常不把頁面標題放進無障礙畫面，這時仍要能讀 ----

    private fun loose(texts: List<UiText>, fallback: Long = NOW) = MijiaScreenParser.parseLoose(texts, TAIPEI, NOW, fallback)

    @Test fun looseReadsWithoutPageTitle() {
        val r = loose(s400Page().filterNot { it.text.startsWith("Xiaomi") })!!
        assertEquals(68.4, r.weightKg!!, 0.0)
        assertEquals(22.0, r.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)
        assertTrue(!r.timeEstimated)
    }

    @Test fun looseUsesFallbackTimeWhenTimeIsMissing() {
        val page = s400Page().filterNot { it.text.startsWith("2026") }
        val r = loose(page, fallback = NOW - 3_600_000)!!
        assertEquals(NOW - 3_600_000, r.measuredAt)
        assertTrue(r.timeEstimated)
        assertEquals(68.4, r.weightKg!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun dashDotAndChineseTimeFormatsWork() {
        val expected = Instant.parse("2026-10-18T22:33:00Z").toEpochMilli()
        for (time in listOf("2026-10-19 06:33", "2026.10.19 06:33", "10月19日 06:33")) {
            assertEquals(time, expected, loose(s400Page(time = time))!!.measuredAt)
        }
    }

    @Test fun twelveHourTimeWorks() {
        val expected = Instant.parse("2026-10-19T10:33:00Z").toEpochMilli() // 台北 18:33
        assertEquals(expected, loose(s400Page(time = "下午 6:33"))!!.measuredAt)
    }

    @Test fun unitAttachedWeightWorks() {
        val page = s400Page().filterNot { it.text.trim() in listOf("68", ".4") } + t("68.4 kg", 425, 612, 733, 899)
        assertEquals(68.4, parse(page)!!.weightKg!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun bodyFatValueBelowLabelIsAccepted() {
        val page = s400Page().map {
            when (it.text) {
                "22" -> it.copy(top = 2335, bottom = 2447)
                "體脂率" -> it.copy(top = 2169, bottom = 2281)
                else -> it
            }
        }
        assertEquals(22.0, parse(page)!!.bodyFatPercent!!, 0.0)
    }

    @Test fun looksLikeScaleDetectsPageWithoutTitle() {
        assertTrue(MijiaScreenParser.looksLikeScale(s400Page().filterNot { it.text.startsWith("Xiaomi") }))
        assertTrue(!MijiaScreenParser.looksLikeScale(listOf(t("我的家", 0, 0, 100, 50), t("70", 0, 100, 50, 200))))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\sync\SyncSchedulerTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.sync

import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test
import java.time.Duration
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.ZonedDateTime

class SyncSchedulerTest {
    private val taipei = ZoneId.of("Asia/Taipei")
    private fun at(h: Int, m: Int = 0, s: Int = 0) = ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 10, 19, h, m, s, 0, taipei)

    @Test fun beforeEightPmWaitsUntilToday() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(2), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, at(18)))

    @Test fun afterEightPmWaitsUntilTomorrow() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(23), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, at(21)))

    @Test fun exactlyEightPmSchedulesTomorrow() = assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(24), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, at(20)))

    @Test fun secondsAreAccountedFor() = assertEquals(Duration.ofMinutes(29).plusSeconds(30), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, at(19, 30, 30)))

    @Test fun crossesMonthBoundary() {
        val now = ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 10, 31, 22, 0, 0, 0, taipei)
        assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(22), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, now))
    }

    @Test fun dayLightSavingShiftIsRespected() {
        val ny = ZoneId.of("America/New_York") // 2026-11-01 回撥一小時：當天 18:00 到 20:00 實際 3 小時（含重複的 1 點不影響），此處驗證用牆上時間計算
        val now = ZonedDateTime.of(2026, 11, 1, 18, 0, 0, 0, ny)
        assertEquals(Duration.ofHours(2), SyncScheduler.delayUntilNext(20, now))
    }
}


===== app\src\test\kotlin\com\startinsnow\healthtracker\ui\RecordsListTest.kt =====
package com.startinsnow.healthtracker.ui

import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.HealthRecord
import com.startinsnow.healthtracker.data.db.RecordType
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Test

/** 「紀錄 > 全部」必須列出每一種類型（體重／體脂率／血糖／血壓），不可只列部分。 */
class RecordsListTest {
    private fun rec(id: String, type: RecordType, value: Double, at: Long) = HealthRecord(
        id = id, type = type, value = value, measuredAt = at, createdAt = 0, updatedAt = 0,
    )

    private val list = listOf(
        rec("w", RecordType.WEIGHT, 70.0, 300),
        rec("f", RecordType.BODY_FAT, 20.0, 200),
        rec("g", RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE, 100.0, 100),
    )

    @Test fun allTabIncludesEveryType() {
        val all = recordsForTab(list, null)
        assertEquals(listOf("w", "f", "g"), all.map { it.id })
        assertEquals(
            setOf(RecordType.WEIGHT, RecordType.BODY_FAT, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE),
            all.map { it.type }.toSet(),
        )
    }

    @Test fun allTabIsSortedNewestFirst() {
        val shuffled = listOf(list[1], list[2], list[0])
        assertEquals(listOf("w", "f", "g"), recordsForTab(shuffled, null).map { it.id })
    }

    @Test fun typeFilterKeepsOnlyThatType() {
        assertEquals(listOf("f"), recordsForTab(list, RecordType.BODY_FAT).map { it.id })
    }

    @Test fun summaryCountsAllTypes() {
        assertEquals(
            mapOf(RecordType.WEIGHT to 1, RecordType.BODY_FAT to 1, RecordType.BLOOD_GLUCOSE to 1),
            typeCounts(list),
        )
        assertEquals("全部 3 筆：體重 1、體脂率 1、血糖 1", summaryText(list))
    }
}
