===== supabase\add_app_updates.sql =====
-- App 內雲端更新用的私人 bucket。在 Supabase 後台 SQL Editor 整段貼上執行一次。
-- 只開放「登入者讀取」；寫入（上傳新版 APK 與 latest.json）不開放給任何 App 使用者，
-- 只能由你本人在 Supabase 後台 Storage 介面手動上傳，所以就算有人註冊帳號也無法放惡意檔案進來。
-- 上傳的檔案：tools/prepare_update.py 產生的 APK 與 latest.json，兩個檔案放在 bucket 根目錄。

insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public, file_size_limit)
values ('app-updates', 'app-updates', false, 52428800)
on conflict (id) do nothing;

drop policy if exists "app updates read" on storage.objects;
create policy "app updates read" on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'app-updates');


===== supabase\add_context_column.sql =====
-- 升級：為既有的 health_records 加上「血糖測量情境」欄位。
-- 在 Supabase 後台 SQL Editor 貼上整段執行一次即可（重複執行也安全）。
alter table public.health_records add column if not exists context text;
do $$ begin
  alter table public.health_records
    add constraint health_records_context_valid
    check (context is null or context in ('FASTING', 'BEFORE_MEAL', 'AFTER_MEAL', 'BEDTIME'));
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;


===== supabase\schema.sql =====
-- HealthTracker 的 Supabase 結構（在 Supabase 後台 SQL Editor 整段貼上執行一次）。
-- 設計：只有登入者本人能讀寫自己的資料（Row Level Security）；不開放 delete，刪除一律用 deleted_at 軟刪除。
-- 單人自用：建議到 Authentication 設定關閉「Allow new users to sign up」，只保留你自己建立的那個使用者。

create table if not exists public.health_records (
  id           uuid primary key,
  user_id      uuid not null default auth.uid() references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  type         text not null check (type in ('WEIGHT', 'BODY_FAT', 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE', 'BLOOD_PRESSURE')),
  value        double precision not null,
  value2       double precision,
  measured_at  bigint not null,            -- epoch millis（量測時間）
  source       text not null default 'MANUAL',
  note         text not null default '',
  created_at   bigint not null,
  updated_at   bigint not null,            -- 用戶端時間，衝突時以較新者為準
  deleted_at   bigint,
  photo_object text,                       -- Storage 物件路徑：<user_id>/<id>.jpg
  synced_at    timestamptz not null default now()  -- 伺服器時間，供拉取增量用
);

-- 照片路徑只能指向自己的資料夾（Storage 本身也有 RLS，這裡多一道防線，避免寫入指向別人檔案的路徑）。
do $$ begin
  alter table public.health_records
    add constraint health_records_photo_own_folder
    check (photo_object is null or photo_object like user_id::text || '/%');
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;

-- 數值合理範圍（與 App 的輸入檢查一致；擋掉單位或小數點打錯造成的離譜資料）。
do $$ begin
  alter table public.health_records
    add constraint health_records_value_range
    check (
      case type
        when 'WEIGHT' then value between 20 and 300
        when 'BODY_FAT' then value between 2 and 70
        when 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE' then value between 20 and 800
        when 'BLOOD_PRESSURE' then value between 50 and 260 and value2 between 30 and 160 and value2 < value
        else false
      end
    );
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;

-- 血糖測量情境（空腹／餐前／餐後／睡前）。已經建好資料表的人也只要再執行這一段就能升級。
-- 沒加這個欄位時，App 只會讓「有選情境」的紀錄同步失敗，沒選情境的照常同步。
alter table public.health_records add column if not exists context text;
do $$ begin
  alter table public.health_records
    add constraint health_records_context_valid
    check (context is null or context in ('FASTING', 'BEFORE_MEAL', 'AFTER_MEAL', 'BEDTIME'));
exception when duplicate_object then null; end $$;

create index if not exists health_records_user_synced_idx on public.health_records (user_id, synced_at);
create index if not exists health_records_user_measured_idx on public.health_records (user_id, type, measured_at desc);

-- 每次寫入都由伺服器蓋上 synced_at，避免依賴手機時鐘。
create or replace function public.touch_synced_at() returns trigger
language plpgsql as $$
begin
  new.synced_at := now();
  return new;
end $$;

drop trigger if exists health_records_touch on public.health_records;
create trigger health_records_touch before insert or update on public.health_records
  for each row execute function public.touch_synced_at();

alter table public.health_records enable row level security;

drop policy if exists "own select" on public.health_records;
drop policy if exists "own insert" on public.health_records;
drop policy if exists "own update" on public.health_records;

create policy "own select" on public.health_records for select to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "own insert" on public.health_records for insert to authenticated
  with check (user_id = auth.uid());
create policy "own update" on public.health_records for update to authenticated
  using (user_id = auth.uid()) with check (user_id = auth.uid());

-- 私人照片 bucket：非公開，路徑第一段必須是自己的 user id。
insert into storage.buckets (id, name, public)
values ('meter-photos', 'meter-photos', false)
on conflict (id) do nothing;

drop policy if exists "own photos select" on storage.objects;
drop policy if exists "own photos insert" on storage.objects;
drop policy if exists "own photos update" on storage.objects;
drop policy if exists "own photos delete" on storage.objects;

create policy "own photos select" on storage.objects for select to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'meter-photos' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "own photos insert" on storage.objects for insert to authenticated
  with check (bucket_id = 'meter-photos' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "own photos update" on storage.objects for update to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'meter-photos' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);
create policy "own photos delete" on storage.objects for delete to authenticated
  using (bucket_id = 'meter-photos' and (storage.foldername(name))[1] = auth.uid()::text);


===== tools\prepare_update.py =====
"""準備 App 內雲端更新要上傳的兩個檔案（不連網、不需要任何密碼）。

用法（先 gradlew assembleDebug）：
    python tools/prepare_update.py

做的事：
1. 取 app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk，只留 arm64 的原生函式庫（檔案小一半，Supabase 單檔上限 50 MB）；
2. 用 debug 金鑰重新簽章（和手機上已安裝的版本同一把，才能覆蓋更新）；
3. 在 tools/update_out/ 產生 APK 與 latest.json（含 versionCode、SHA-256）。

接著由你本人到 Supabase 後台 Storage → app-updates，把這兩個檔案拖進去（覆蓋舊的）。
App 的「設定 > 關於 > 檢查更新」就會看到新版。
"""
import json
import os
import re
import subprocess
import sys
import zipfile

ROOT = os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)))
SRC = os.path.join(ROOT, 'app', 'build', 'outputs', 'apk', 'debug', 'app-debug.apk')
OUT = os.path.join(ROOT, 'tools', 'update_out')


def sdk_build_tools():
    props = open(os.path.join(ROOT, 'local.properties'), encoding='utf-8').read()
    sdk = re.search(r'^sdk\.dir=(.+)$', props, re.M).group(1).strip().replace('\\\\', '\\').replace('\\:', ':')
    bt = os.path.join(sdk, 'build-tools')
    return os.path.join(bt, sorted(os.listdir(bt))[-1])


def main():
    if not os.path.isfile(SRC):
        sys.exit('找不到 app-debug.apk，請先執行 gradlew assembleDebug')
    bt = sdk_build_tools()
    os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
    raw = os.path.join(OUT, 'raw.apk')
    aligned = os.path.join(OUT, 'aligned.apk')
    with zipfile.ZipFile(SRC) as zin, zipfile.ZipFile(raw, 'w', zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED) as zout:
        for it in zin.infolist():
            n = it.filename
            if n.startswith('lib/') and not n.startswith('lib/arm64-v8a/'):
                continue
            if n.startswith('META-INF/') and n.upper().endswith(('.SF', '.RSA', '.DSA', '.EC')):
                continue
            zi = zipfile.ZipInfo(n, it.date_time)
            zi.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_STORED if it.compress_type == zipfile.ZIP_STORED else zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED
            zout.writestr(zi, zin.read(n))

    badging = subprocess.run([os.path.join(bt, 'aapt.exe'), 'dump', 'badging', raw], capture_output=True, text=True, check=True).stdout
    m = re.search(r"package: name='([^']+)' versionCode='(\d+)' versionName='([^']+)'", badging)
    package, code, name = m.group(1), int(m.group(2)), m.group(3)
    apk_name = f'healthtracker-{name}-arm64.apk'
    final = os.path.join(OUT, apk_name)
    for f in (aligned, final):
        if os.path.exists(f):
            os.remove(f)
    subprocess.run([os.path.join(bt, 'zipalign.exe'), '-p', '4', raw, aligned], check=True)
    subprocess.run(['cmd', '/c', os.path.join(bt, 'apksigner.bat'), 'sign', '--ks', os.path.expanduser(r'~\.android\debug.keystore'),
                    '--ks-pass', 'pass:android', '--key-pass', 'pass:android', '--ks-key-alias', 'androiddebugkey',
                    '--out', final, aligned], check=True)
    subprocess.run(['cmd', '/c', os.path.join(bt, 'apksigner.bat'), 'verify', final], check=True)
    for f in (raw, aligned):
        os.remove(f)

    import hashlib
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(final, 'rb') as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b''):
            h.update(chunk)
    size = os.path.getsize(final)
    if size > 50 * 1024 * 1024:
        sys.exit(f'APK 有 {size / 1e6:.1f} MB，超過 Supabase 單檔 50 MB 上限')
    info = dict(versionCode=code, versionName=name, apk=apk_name, sha256=h.hexdigest(), size=size, notes='', package=package)
    with open(os.path.join(OUT, 'latest.json'), 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        json.dump(info, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    print(f'完成：{final}  {size / 1e6:.1f} MB')
    print(f'latest.json：versionCode={code}, versionName={name}')
    print('請到 Supabase 後台 Storage → app-updates，上傳 tools/update_out 裡的 APK 與 latest.json。')


if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()


===== web\app.js =====
import {
  TYPES, RANGES, rangeStart, normalizeBaseUrl, buildRecordsQuery, sessionNeedsRefresh,
  formatValue, formatTime, sourceLabel, summarize, isSafePhotoPath, fetchAllPages, CONTEXT_LABELS, contextLabel,
} from './lib.js';
import { renderChart } from './chart.js';
import { initBatch } from './batch.js';
import { toRows, toCsv, buildXlsx, exportFileName, EXPORT_HEADER } from './export.js';

const CONFIG_KEY = 'ht.config'; // { url, key }：只存在這個瀏覽器
const SESSION_KEY = 'ht.session'; // sessionStorage：關掉分頁就登出

const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
const show = (id, on) => { $(id).hidden = !on; };
const setMsg = (id, text, isErr = false) => {
  const el = $(id);
  el.textContent = text;
  el.classList.toggle('err', isErr);
};

const state = { type: 'WEIGHT', range: '90', ctx: null, photoUrl: null, loadSeq: 0, photoSeq: 0, opener: null };
const MAX_EXPORT_PAGES = 50; // 5 萬筆；超過就明確告知並取消匯出

const loadConfig = () => {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CONFIG_KEY)); } catch { return null; }
};
const loadSession = () => {
  try { return JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)); } catch { return null; }
};
const saveSession = (s) => (s ? sessionStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(s)) : sessionStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY));

class ApiError extends Error {
  constructor(message, status) { super(message); this.status = status; }
}

function toSession(body) {
  return {
    accessToken: body.access_token,
    refreshToken: body.refresh_token,
    expiresAt: Date.now() + (body.expires_in ?? 3600) * 1000,
    email: body.user?.email ?? '',
  };
}

async function tokenRequest(grantType, payload) {
  const cfg = loadConfig();
  let resp;
  try {
    resp = await fetch(`${cfg.url}/auth/v1/token?grant_type=${grantType}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { apikey: cfg.key, 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError('無法連線，請檢查網路或連線設定', 0);
  }
  if (!resp.ok) {
    const expected = resp.status === 400 || resp.status === 401 || resp.status === 422;
    throw new ApiError(expected ? '帳號或密碼錯誤，或登入已失效' : `登入失敗（HTTP ${resp.status}）`, resp.status);
  }
  return toSession(await resp.json());
}

async function validSession() {
  let s = loadSession();
  if (!s) throw new ApiError('尚未登入', 401);
  if (sessionNeedsRefresh(s)) {
    try {
      s = await tokenRequest('refresh_token', { refresh_token: s.refreshToken });
      saveSession(s);
    } catch (e) {
      if (e.status === 400 || e.status === 401 || e.status === 422) saveSession(null);
      throw e;
    }
  }
  return s;
}

/** 帶登入憑證的請求；伺服器說過期（401）就續期後重試一次。 */
async function authed(path, init = {}, retried = false) {
  const cfg = loadConfig();
  const s = await validSession();
  let resp;
  try {
    resp = await fetch(`${cfg.url}${path}`, {
      ...init,
      headers: { apikey: cfg.key, Authorization: `Bearer ${s.accessToken}`, ...(init.headers ?? {}) },
    });
  } catch {
    throw new ApiError('無法連線到雲端，請檢查網路', 0);
  }
  if (resp.status === 401 && !retried) {
    saveSession({ ...s, expiresAt: 0 }); // 強制下一輪續期
    return authed(path, init, true);
  }
  if (resp.status === 401) { saveSession(null); throw new ApiError('登入已過期，請重新登入', 401); }
  if (resp.status === 403) throw new ApiError('沒有權限（請確認已執行 schema.sql 並用正確帳號登入）', 403);
  if (resp.status === 404) throw new ApiError('找不到資料表或照片（請確認已執行 schema.sql）', 404);
  if (!resp.ok) throw new ApiError(`伺服器錯誤（HTTP ${resp.status}）`, resp.status);
  return resp;
}

// ---------- 畫面切換 ----------
function route() {
  const cfg = loadConfig();
  const s = loadSession();
  show('setup', !cfg);
  show('login', !!cfg && !s);
  show('records', !!cfg && !!s);
  show('logout', !!s);
  if (cfg && s) loadRecords();
}

function buildTabs(containerId, items, current, onPick) {
  const box = $(containerId);
  box.replaceChildren();
  for (const [key, label] of items) {
    const b = document.createElement('button');
    b.type = 'button';
    b.setAttribute('role', 'tab');
    b.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(key === current));
    b.textContent = label;
    b.addEventListener('click', () => onPick(key));
    box.append(b);
  }
}

function renderCtxTabs() {
  const box = $('ctx-tabs');
  box.hidden = state.type !== 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE';
  if (box.hidden) return;
  buildTabs('ctx-tabs', [[null, '全部情境'], ...Object.entries(CONTEXT_LABELS)], state.ctx, (k) => { state.ctx = k; renderCtxTabs(); loadRecords(); });
}

function renderTabs() {
  buildTabs('type-tabs', Object.entries(TYPES).map(([k, v]) => [k, v.label]), state.type, (k) => { state.type = k; state.ctx = null; renderTabs(); loadRecords(); });
  renderCtxTabs();
  buildTabs('range-tabs', RANGES.map((r) => [r.key, r.label]), state.range, (k) => { state.range = k; renderTabs(); loadRecords(); });
}

function renderSummary(records) {
  const box = $('summary');
  box.replaceChildren();
  const s = summarize(records);
  if (!s) return;
  const t = TYPES[state.type];
  const item = (label, text) => {
    const span = document.createElement('span');
    span.append(`${label} `);
    const b = document.createElement('b');
    b.textContent = text;
    span.append(b);
    box.append(span);
  };
  item('最新', formatValue(s.latest));
  item('筆數', String(s.count));
  if (state.type !== 'BLOOD_PRESSURE') {
    const f = (v) => `${v.toFixed(t.digits)} ${t.unit}`;
    item('平均', f(s.avg));
    item('最低', f(s.min));
    item('最高', f(s.max));
    if (s.count > 1) item('最新−最早', `${s.change > 0 ? '+' : s.change < 0 ? '−' : ''}${Math.abs(s.change).toFixed(t.digits)} ${t.unit}`);
  } else if (s.diastolic) {
    // 血壓：收縮壓／舒張壓分別看平均與範圍（就醫時最常被問到）
    const sys = records.map((r) => r.value);
    const avgSys = sys.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / sys.length;
    item('平均', `${Math.round(avgSys)}/${Math.round(s.diastolic.avg)} mmHg`);
    item('收縮壓範圍', `${Math.min(...sys)}–${Math.max(...sys)}`);
    item('舒張壓範圍', `${s.diastolic.min}–${s.diastolic.max}`);
  }
}

function renderRows(records) {
  const tbody = $('rows');
  tbody.replaceChildren();
  for (const r of records) {
    const tr = document.createElement('tr');
    const cell = (text, cls) => {
      const td = document.createElement('td');
      td.textContent = text;
      if (cls) td.className = cls;
      return td;
    };
    tr.append(cell(formatTime(r.measured_at)), cell(formatValue(r), 'val'), cell(contextLabel(r.context)), cell(sourceLabel(r.source)), cell(r.note ?? ''));
    const photoTd = document.createElement('td');
    if (isSafePhotoPath(r.photo_object)) {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.type = 'button';
      b.textContent = '查看';
      b.addEventListener('click', () => openPhoto(r.photo_object));
      photoTd.append(b);
    }
    tr.append(photoTd);
    tbody.append(tr);
  }
}

async function loadRecords() {
  const seq = ++state.loadSeq; // 快速切換分頁時，只採用最後一次請求的結果
  setMsg('list-msg', '載入中…');
  $('chart').classList.add('loading'); // 重新載入時保留上一張圖、降低透明度，不閃爍
  try {
    const q = buildRecordsQuery({ type: state.type, since: rangeStart(state.range) });
    const resp = await authed(`/rest/v1/health_records?${q}`);
    const all = await resp.json();
    if (seq !== state.loadSeq) return;
    const records = state.type === 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE' && state.ctx ? all.filter((r) => r.context === state.ctx) : all;
    renderSummary(records);
    renderRows(records);
    $('chart-title').textContent = records.length ? `${TYPES[state.type].label}趨勢（${TYPES[state.type].unit}）` : '';
    renderChart($('chart'), $('tooltip'), state.type, records);
    const who = loadSession()?.email;
    $('load-info').textContent = records.length
      ? `${who ? `帳號 ${who}　` : ''}已載入 ${records.length} 筆　更新於 ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('zh-TW', { hour12: false })}`
      : '';
    setMsg('list-msg', records.length === 0 ? '這段期間沒有資料' : records.length >= PAGE ? `資料很多，只顯示最近 ${PAGE} 筆` : '');
  } catch (e) {
    if (seq !== state.loadSeq) return;
    // 離線（status 0）時保留畫面上原本的資料，只提示連不上；其他錯誤才清空，避免看到過期或錯誤的資料
    if (e.status !== 0) {
      renderSummary([]);
      renderRows([]);
      renderChart($('chart'), $('tooltip'), state.type, []);
    }
    // 憑證被清掉（續期失敗）或伺服器說未授權：回到登入畫面並說明原因
    if (e.status === 401 || !loadSession()) { route(); setMsg('login-msg', '登入已失效，請重新登入', true); return; }
    setMsg('list-msg', e.status === 0 ? `${e.message}（以下是上次載入的資料）` : e.message, true);
  } finally {
    if (seq === state.loadSeq) $('chart').classList.remove('loading');
  }
}

// ---------- 匯出 ----------
const PAGE = 1000;

/** 分頁抓完（Supabase 預設單次最多 1000 筆）。 */
function fetchAll(type, since) {
  return fetchAllPages(
    async (offset, limit) => {
      const q = buildRecordsQuery({ type, since, limit, offset });
      return (await authed(`/rest/v1/health_records?${q}`)).json();
    },
    { pageSize: PAGE, maxPages: MAX_EXPORT_PAGES },
  );
}

function download(bytesOrText, name, mime) {
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([bytesOrText], { type: mime }));
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = url;
  a.download = name;
  document.body.append(a);
  a.click();
  a.remove();
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10000);
}

async function doExport(format, scope) {
  setMsg('exp-msg', '匯出中…');
  try {
    const type = scope === 'all' ? null : state.type;
    const { rows: records, truncated } = await fetchAll(type, rangeStart(state.range));
    if (truncated) {
      // 寧可不給檔案，也不能給一份看起來完整、其實缺資料的檔案
      setMsg('exp-msg', `資料超過 ${MAX_EXPORT_PAGES * PAGE} 筆上限，為避免匯出不完整已取消。請縮小時間範圍或只匯出單一類型。`, true);
      return;
    }
    if (!records.length) { setMsg('exp-msg', '這段期間沒有資料可匯出'); return; }
    const rows = toRows(records);
    const label = `${scope === 'all' ? '全部' : TYPES[state.type].label}_${state.range === 'all' ? '全部期間' : `${state.range}天`}`;
    if (format === 'csv') download(toCsv(rows), exportFileName(label, 'csv'), 'text/csv;charset=utf-8');
    else download(buildXlsx([EXPORT_HEADER, ...rows]), exportFileName(label, 'xlsx'), 'application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet');
    setMsg('exp-msg', `已匯出 ${records.length} 筆`);
  } catch (e) {
    setMsg('exp-msg', e.message, true);
  }
}

// ---------- 照片 ----------
function closePhoto() {
  state.photoSeq++; // 讓還在路上的照片請求失效
  if (state.photoUrl) URL.revokeObjectURL(state.photoUrl);
  state.photoUrl = null;
  $('photo-img').removeAttribute('src');
  show('photo-img', false);
  const wasOpen = !$('photo-modal').hidden;
  show('photo-modal', false);
  if (wasOpen && state.opener && document.contains(state.opener)) state.opener.focus(); // 焦點還給原本的按鈕
  state.opener = null;
}

async function openPhoto(path) {
  const seq = ++state.photoSeq;
  state.opener = document.activeElement;
  if (state.photoUrl) { URL.revokeObjectURL(state.photoUrl); state.photoUrl = null; }
  $('photo-img').removeAttribute('src');
  show('photo-img', false);
  show('photo-modal', true);
  $('photo-close').focus();
  setMsg('photo-msg', '載入照片…');
  try {
    // 用登入憑證抓成 blob 再顯示：照片是私人 bucket，網址本身不含任何可分享的權限。
    const resp = await authed(`/storage/v1/object/authenticated/meter-photos/${path}`);
    const blob = await resp.blob();
    if (seq !== state.photoSeq || $('photo-modal').hidden) return; // 已關掉或已改開別張，丟掉這份結果
    state.photoUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    $('photo-img').src = state.photoUrl;
    show('photo-img', true);
    setMsg('photo-msg', '');
  } catch (e) {
    if (seq !== state.photoSeq) return;
    setMsg('photo-msg', e.status === 404 ? '照片不存在（可能只存在手機上，尚未上傳）' : e.message, true);
  }
}

// ---------- 事件 ----------
$('cfg-save').addEventListener('click', () => {
  try {
    const url = normalizeBaseUrl($('cfg-url').value, location.hostname);
    const key = $('cfg-key').value.trim();
    if (key.length < 20) throw new Error('金鑰看起來太短');
    if (key.split('.').length === 3) {
      // anon 與 service_role 都是 JWT；service_role 絕不能放在網頁，簡單檢查 payload 內的 role。
      try {
        const role = JSON.parse(atob(key.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'))).role;
        if (role === 'service_role') throw new Error('這是 service_role 金鑰，絕對不能放在網頁，請改用 anon 金鑰');
      } catch (inner) {
        if (inner.message.includes('service_role')) throw inner;
      }
    }
    localStorage.setItem(CONFIG_KEY, JSON.stringify({ url, key }));
    $('cfg-key').value = '';
    setMsg('cfg-msg', '');
    route();
  } catch (e) {
    setMsg('cfg-msg', e.message, true);
  }
});

$('cfg-reset').addEventListener('click', () => {
  localStorage.removeItem(CONFIG_KEY);
  saveSession(null);
  route();
});

$('login-go').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  setMsg('login-msg', '登入中…');
  try {
    const s = await tokenRequest('password', { email: $('login-email').value.trim(), password: $('login-pass').value });
    saveSession(s);
    $('login-pass').value = '';
    setMsg('login-msg', '');
    route();
  } catch (e) {
    setMsg('login-msg', e.message, true);
  }
});

$('login-pass').addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') $('login-go').click(); });
$('logout').addEventListener('click', () => { saveSession(null); closePhoto(); route(); });
$('reload').addEventListener('click', loadRecords);
$('exp-csv').addEventListener('click', () => doExport('csv', 'current'));
$('exp-xlsx').addEventListener('click', () => doExport('xlsx', 'current'));
$('exp-all-csv').addEventListener('click', () => doExport('csv', 'all'));
$('exp-all-xlsx').addEventListener('click', () => doExport('xlsx', 'all'));
$('photo-close').addEventListener('click', closePhoto);
document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => { if (e.key === 'Escape') closePhoto(); });

/** 登入憑證（JWT）裡的使用者 id；照片要放在 <user_id>/ 資料夾下。 */
async function currentUserId() {
  const s = await validSession();
  try {
    const payload = JSON.parse(atob(s.accessToken.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')));
    if (typeof payload.sub === 'string') return payload.sub;
  } catch { /* 落到下面的錯誤 */ }
  throw new ApiError('無法取得使用者 id，請重新登入', 401);
}

initBatch({ authed, userId: currentUserId, onSaved: loadRecords });

renderTabs();
route();


===== web\batch.js =====
// 批次匯入血糖機照片：讀照片資訊（EXIF 拍攝時間）、本機 OCR 數值，使用者確認後寫入雲端。
import { readExifTime } from './exif.js';
import { glucoseCandidates, recognizeLines, MIN_MGDL, MAX_MGDL } from './ocr.js';

const MAX_FILES = 60;
const MAX_SIDE = 1800;
const DUP_WINDOW_MS = 2 * 60000;
const TAIPEI_MIN = 8 * 60;

/** 毫秒 → <input type="datetime-local"> 的值（台北時間）。 */
export const toLocalInput = (ms) => new Date(ms + TAIPEI_MIN * 60000).toISOString().slice(0, 16);
/** datetime-local 的值（台北時間）→ 毫秒；不合法回傳 NaN。 */
export const fromLocalInput = (text) => Date.parse(`${text}:00+08:00`);

/** 是否與既有紀錄重複：數值相同且時間差在 ±2 分鐘內（與手機 App 相同）。 */
export function isDuplicate(existing, value, ms) {
  return existing.some((r) => Math.abs(r.value - value) < 0.05 && Math.abs(r.measured_at - ms) <= DUP_WINDOW_MS);
}

/** 檢查一列要寫入的資料；回傳錯誤訊息或 null。 */
export function validateRow(value, ms, now) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < MIN_MGDL || value > MAX_MGDL) return `數值需在 ${MIN_MGDL}–${MAX_MGDL}`;
  if (!Number.isFinite(ms)) return '時間不正確';
  if (ms > now + 5 * 60000) return '時間不能在未來';
  return null;
}

async function shrink(file) {
  const bmp = await createImageBitmap(file, { imageOrientation: 'from-image' });
  const f = Math.min(1, MAX_SIDE / Math.max(bmp.width, bmp.height));
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
  canvas.width = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.width * f));
  canvas.height = Math.max(1, Math.round(bmp.height * f));
  canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(bmp, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  bmp.close?.();
  const blob = await new Promise((res) => canvas.toBlob(res, 'image/jpeg', 0.85));
  if (!blob) throw new Error('無法處理這張照片');
  return { canvas, blob };
}

/**
 * deps: { authed(path, init), userId(): Promise<string>, onSaved() }
 * 畫面元素（index.html 的 #batch 區塊）由這裡接上。
 */
export function initBatch({ authed, userId, onSaved }) {
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);
  const input = $('batch-files');
  const body = $('batch-rows');
  const saveBtn = $('batch-save');
  let rows = [];
  let busy = false;

  const say = (text, err = false) => { const el = $('batch-msg'); el.textContent = text; el.classList.toggle('err', err); };

  function render() {
    body.replaceChildren();
    rows.forEach((r) => {
      const tr = document.createElement('tr');
      const td = (...kids) => { const c = document.createElement('td'); c.append(...kids); tr.append(c); return c; };

      const chk = document.createElement('input');
      chk.type = 'checkbox'; chk.checked = r.include; chk.disabled = r.saved;
      chk.addEventListener('change', () => { r.include = chk.checked; });
      td(chk);

      const img = document.createElement('img');
      img.src = r.thumb; img.alt = r.file.name; img.className = 'thumb';
      td(img);

      const val = document.createElement('input');
      val.type = 'number'; val.step = '1'; val.value = r.value ?? ''; val.className = 'num'; val.disabled = r.saved;
      val.setAttribute('aria-label', '血糖數值');
      val.addEventListener('input', () => { r.value = val.value === '' ? null : Number(val.value); r.include = r.value != null; chk.checked = r.include; });
      td(val);

      const when = document.createElement('input');
      when.type = 'datetime-local'; when.value = toLocalInput(r.ms); when.disabled = r.saved;
      when.setAttribute('aria-label', '測量時間');
      when.addEventListener('change', () => { const v = fromLocalInput(when.value); if (Number.isFinite(v)) { r.ms = v; r.timeFrom = 'edit'; } });
      td(when);

      td(document.createTextNode(r.status));
      body.append(tr);
    });
    saveBtn.disabled = busy || !rows.some((r) => r.include && !r.saved);
  }

  async function fetchExisting(from, to) {
    const q = `/rest/v1/health_records?select=value,measured_at&type=eq.BLOOD_GLUCOSE&deleted_at=is.null&measured_at=gte.${from}&measured_at=lte.${to}&limit=1000`;
    return (await authed(q)).json();
  }

  async function analyse(files) {
    busy = true; rows = []; render();
    const list = [...files].slice(0, MAX_FILES);
    if (files.length > MAX_FILES) say(`一次最多 ${MAX_FILES} 張，只處理前 ${MAX_FILES} 張。`, true);
    for (let i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
      const file = list[i];
      say(`辨識中 ${i + 1} / ${list.length}（第一次會先載入辨識元件，需要幾秒）…`);
      const row = { file, include: false, saved: false, value: null, ms: file.lastModified, timeFrom: 'file', status: '', blob: null, thumb: '' };
      try {
        const ex = readExifTime(await file.arrayBuffer());
        if (ex != null) { row.ms = ex; row.timeFrom = 'exif'; }
        const { canvas, blob } = await shrink(file);
        row.blob = blob;
        row.thumb = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        let c = glucoseCandidates(await recognizeLines(canvas, '3'))[0];
        if (!c) c = glucoseCandidates(await recognizeLines(canvas, '11'))[0]; // 自動分頁讀不到時，改用稀疏文字再試一次
        row.value = c ? c.value : null;
        row.include = c != null && row.timeFrom === 'exif';
        row.status = [c ? '' : '認不出數值', row.timeFrom === 'exif' ? '' : '無拍攝時間（用檔案時間，請確認）'].filter(Boolean).join('；') || '待確認';
      } catch (e) {
        row.status = `處理失敗：${e.message}`;
        row.thumb = row.thumb || 'data:,';
      }
      rows.push(row);
      render();
    }
    // 與雲端既有紀錄比對，重複的預設不勾
    try {
      const times = rows.map((r) => r.ms).filter(Number.isFinite);
      if (times.length) {
        const existing = await fetchExisting(Math.min(...times) - DUP_WINDOW_MS, Math.max(...times) + DUP_WINDOW_MS);
        for (const r of rows) {
          if (r.value != null && isDuplicate(existing, r.value, r.ms)) { r.include = false; r.status = '雲端已有相同紀錄'; }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      say(`無法比對重複：${e.message}`, true);
    }
    busy = false; render();
    say(`已辨識 ${rows.length} 張。請核對數值與時間，勾選要寫入的列後按「寫入雲端」。辨識可能有誤。`);
  }

  async function save() {
    busy = true; render();
    const uid = await userId();
    const now = Date.now();
    let ok = 0; let fail = 0;
    const todo = rows.filter((r) => r.include && !r.saved);
    for (let i = 0; i < todo.length; i++) {
      const r = todo[i];
      say(`寫入中 ${i + 1} / ${todo.length}…`);
      const err = validateRow(r.value, r.ms, now);
      if (err) { r.status = err; fail++; continue; }
      try {
        const id = crypto.randomUUID();
        const objectPath = `${uid}/${id}.jpg`;
        let photoObject = null;
        try {
          await authed(`/storage/v1/object/meter-photos/${objectPath}`, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'image/jpeg', 'x-upsert': 'true' }, body: r.blob });
          photoObject = objectPath;
        } catch { /* 照片上傳失敗仍寫入數值，狀態欄會註明 */ }
        const rec = { id, type: 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE', value: r.value, value2: null, measured_at: r.ms, source: 'OCR', note: '', created_at: now, updated_at: now };
        if (photoObject) rec.photo_object = photoObject;
        await authed('/rest/v1/health_records', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Prefer: 'resolution=merge-duplicates,return=minimal' }, body: JSON.stringify(rec) });
        r.saved = true; r.include = false; r.status = photoObject ? '已寫入' : '已寫入（照片上傳失敗）';
        ok++;
      } catch (e) {
        r.status = `寫入失敗：${e.message}`; fail++;
      }
      render();
    }
    busy = false; render();
    say(`寫入完成：成功 ${ok} 筆${fail ? `、失敗 ${fail} 筆` : ''}。`, fail > 0);
    if (ok > 0) onSaved();
  }

  input.addEventListener('change', () => { if (input.files.length && !busy) analyse(input.files).catch((e) => { busy = false; say(`批次匯入失敗：${e.message}`, true); render(); }); });
  saveBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { if (!busy) save().catch((e) => { busy = false; say(`寫入失敗：${e.message}`, true); render(); }); });
}


===== web\chart.js =====
// 趨勢圖：幾何與刻度的純函式（可用 node 測試）＋ SVG 繪製。
// 色彩依 dataviz 規格：系列色用 CSS 變數（亮／暗各自選色，已用驗證腳本檢查），文字用文字色，不用系列色。
import { TYPES, formatValue, formatTime } from './lib.js';

const DAY_MS = 86400000;
const TAIPEI_MS = 8 * 3600000;

/** 把 [min,max] 擴成漂亮的刻度（1/2/5 × 10^n）。 */
export function niceScale(min, max, count = 5) {
  if (!Number.isFinite(min) || !Number.isFinite(max)) return { min: 0, max: 1, ticks: [0, 1] };
  if (min === max) { min -= 1; max += 1; }
  const span = max - min;
  const rough = span / Math.max(1, count - 1);
  const pow = 10 ** Math.floor(Math.log10(rough));
  const frac = rough / pow;
  const step = (frac <= 1 ? 1 : frac <= 2 ? 2 : frac <= 5 ? 5 : 10) * pow;
  const lo = Math.floor(min / step) * step;
  const hi = Math.ceil(max / step) * step;
  const ticks = [];
  for (let v = lo; v <= hi + step / 2; v += step) ticks.push(Number(v.toFixed(10)));
  return { min: Number(lo.toFixed(10)), max: Number(hi.toFixed(10)), ticks };
}

/** 依類型把紀錄（新到舊）整理成「舊到新」的系列。血壓有兩條線。 */
export function buildSeries(type, records) {
  const asc = [...records].sort((a, b) => a.measured_at - b.measured_at);
  if (type === 'BLOOD_PRESSURE') {
    return [
      { key: 'sys', label: '收縮壓', points: asc.map((r) => ({ t: r.measured_at, v: r.value, rec: r })) },
      { key: 'dia', label: '舒張壓', points: asc.filter((r) => r.value2 != null).map((r) => ({ t: r.measured_at, v: r.value2, rec: r })) },
    ];
  }
  return [{ key: 'main', label: TYPES[type]?.label ?? type, points: asc.map((r) => ({ t: r.measured_at, v: r.value, rec: r })) }];
}

/** X 軸刻度：落在台北時間的 0 點，間隔挑 1/2/7/14/30/60/90/180/365 天中讓刻度數約 6 的那個。 */
export function timeTicks(minT, maxT, target = 6) {
  const span = Math.max(DAY_MS, maxT - minT);
  const steps = [1, 2, 7, 14, 30, 60, 90, 180, 365].map((d) => d * DAY_MS);
  const step = steps.find((s) => span / s <= target + 1) ?? steps[steps.length - 1];
  const startLocal = Math.ceil((minT + TAIPEI_MS) / DAY_MS) * DAY_MS; // 台北當地午夜
  const ticks = [];
  for (let t = startLocal; t - TAIPEI_MS <= maxT; t += step) ticks.push(t - TAIPEI_MS);
  return ticks;
}

export function formatTick(t) {
  return formatTime(t).slice(5, 10).replace('-', '/'); // MM/DD
}

/** 回傳座標轉換函式與刻度。points 全部系列合併後取範圍。 */
export function layout(series, { width, height, margin }) {
  const all = series.flatMap((s) => s.points);
  const times = all.map((p) => p.t);
  const minT = Math.min(...times);
  const maxT = Math.max(...times);
  const y = niceScale(Math.min(...all.map((p) => p.v)), Math.max(...all.map((p) => p.v)));
  const innerW = width - margin.left - margin.right;
  const innerH = height - margin.top - margin.bottom;
  const tSpan = Math.max(1, maxT - minT);
  return {
    minT, maxT, y, innerW, innerH,
    x: (t) => margin.left + (maxT === minT ? innerW / 2 : ((t - minT) / tSpan) * innerW),
    yPos: (v) => margin.top + innerH - ((v - y.min) / (y.max - y.min)) * innerH,
    xTicks: timeTicks(minT, maxT),
  };
}

/** 找出離 px 最近的時間點索引（xs 由小到大）。 */
export function nearestIndex(xs, px) {
  let best = 0;
  for (let i = 1; i < xs.length; i++) if (Math.abs(xs[i] - px) < Math.abs(xs[best] - px)) best = i;
  return best;
}

// ---------- 繪製 ----------
const NS = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
const W = 720;
const H = 300;
const MARGIN = { top: 16, right: 64, bottom: 28, left: 48 };

function el(tag, attrs = {}, text) {
  const n = document.createElementNS(NS, tag);
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(attrs)) n.setAttribute(k, String(v));
  if (text != null) n.textContent = text;
  return n;
}

/** 在 container 畫圖；沒有資料就清空。 */
export function renderChart(container, tooltipEl, type, records) {
  container.replaceChildren();
  tooltipEl.hidden = true;
  const series = buildSeries(type, records).filter((s) => s.points.length);
  if (!series.length) return;

  const t = TYPES[type];
  const g = layout(series, { width: W, height: H, margin: MARGIN });
  const svg = el('svg', { viewBox: `0 0 ${W} ${H}`, role: 'img', 'aria-label': `${t.label}趨勢圖，共 ${records.length} 筆；完整數值見下方表格` });

  // 格線與 Y 軸刻度（髮絲、實線、低調）
  for (const tick of g.y.ticks) {
    const y = g.yPos(tick);
    svg.append(el('line', { x1: MARGIN.left, x2: W - MARGIN.right, y1: y, y2: y, class: 'grid' }));
    svg.append(el('text', { x: MARGIN.left - 8, y: y + 4, class: 'axis', 'text-anchor': 'end' }, String(tick)));
  }
  for (const tt of g.xTicks) {
    svg.append(el('text', { x: g.x(tt), y: H - 8, class: 'axis', 'text-anchor': 'middle' }, formatTick(tt)));
  }

  // 系列：2px 線；點數不多才畫點（含 2px 底色環），點多只標最後一點
  series.forEach((s, i) => {
    const cls = `s${i + 1}`;
    const d = s.points.map((p, k) => `${k ? 'L' : 'M'}${g.x(p.t).toFixed(1)},${g.yPos(p.v).toFixed(1)}`).join('');
    if (s.points.length > 1) svg.append(el('path', { d, class: `line ${cls}` }));
    const showAll = s.points.length <= 60;
    s.points.forEach((p, k) => {
      if (showAll || k === s.points.length - 1) svg.append(el('circle', { cx: g.x(p.t), cy: g.yPos(p.v), r: 4, class: `dot ${cls}` }));
    });
    const last = s.points[s.points.length - 1];
    // 直接標示：只標最後一筆（文字用文字色，色彩由旁邊的線段短鍵承擔）
    svg.append(el('line', { x1: W - MARGIN.right + 6, x2: W - MARGIN.right + 16, y1: g.yPos(last.v), y2: g.yPos(last.v), class: `key ${cls}` }));
    svg.append(el('text', { x: W - MARGIN.right + 20, y: g.yPos(last.v) + 4, class: 'endlabel' }, String(Number.isInteger(last.v) ? last.v : last.v.toFixed(t.digits))));
  });

  // 十字線與互動層
  const cross = el('line', { y1: MARGIN.top, y2: H - MARGIN.bottom, class: 'cross', visibility: 'hidden' });
  svg.append(cross);
  const hit = el('rect', { x: MARGIN.left, y: MARGIN.top, width: g.innerW, height: g.innerH, class: 'hit', tabindex: 0, role: 'application', 'aria-label': '方向鍵左右移動可查看每筆數值' });
  svg.append(hit);

  // 所有時間點（去重、排序），十字線對齊最近的時間
  const times = [...new Set(series.flatMap((s) => s.points.map((p) => p.t)))].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  const xs = times.map(g.x);
  let current = times.length - 1;

  const showAt = (idx) => {
    current = Math.max(0, Math.min(times.length - 1, idx));
    const tm = times[current];
    cross.setAttribute('x1', xs[current]);
    cross.setAttribute('x2', xs[current]);
    cross.setAttribute('visibility', 'visible');
    tooltipEl.replaceChildren();
    const head = document.createElement('div');
    head.className = 'tt-time';
    head.textContent = formatTime(tm);
    tooltipEl.append(head);
    series.forEach((s, i) => {
      const p = s.points.find((q) => q.t === tm);
      if (!p) return;
      const row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'tt-row';
      const key = document.createElement('span');
      key.className = `tt-key s${i + 1}`;
      const val = document.createElement('b');
      val.textContent = series.length > 1 ? `${p.v}` : formatValue(p.rec);
      row.append(key, val);
      if (series.length > 1) {
        const name = document.createElement('span');
        name.className = 'tt-name';
        name.textContent = ` ${s.label}`;
        row.append(name);
      }
      tooltipEl.append(row);
    });
    tooltipEl.hidden = false;
    const box = container.getBoundingClientRect();
    const px = (xs[current] / W) * box.width;
    tooltipEl.style.left = `${Math.min(Math.max(px + 12, 0), Math.max(0, box.width - 150))}px`;
  };

  hit.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    const box = svg.getBoundingClientRect();
    showAt(nearestIndex(xs, ((e.clientX - box.left) / box.width) * W));
  });
  hit.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => { cross.setAttribute('visibility', 'hidden'); tooltipEl.hidden = true; });
  hit.addEventListener('focus', () => showAt(current));
  hit.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
    if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') { e.preventDefault(); showAt(current - 1); }
    if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') { e.preventDefault(); showAt(current + 1); }
  });

  // 圖例：兩條線以上才有（單一系列由標題說明）
  if (series.length > 1) {
    const legend = document.createElement('div');
    legend.className = 'legend';
    series.forEach((s, i) => {
      const item = document.createElement('span');
      const key = document.createElement('span');
      key.className = `tt-key s${i + 1}`;
      item.append(key, ` ${s.label}`);
      legend.append(item);
    });
    container.append(legend);
  }
  container.append(svg);
}


===== web\exif.js =====
// 讀 JPEG 的 EXIF 拍攝時間（純函式，不依賴瀏覽器 API，可在 Node 測試）。
// 只實作需要的部分：APP1 Exif → TIFF → IFD0 / ExifIFD 的日期欄位。

const TAG_DATETIME = 0x0132;
const TAG_EXIF_IFD = 0x8769;
const TAG_DT_ORIGINAL = 0x9003;
const TAG_DT_DIGITIZED = 0x9004;
const TAG_OFFSET_ORIGINAL = 0x9011;
const TAG_OFFSET_DIGITIZED = 0x9012;
const TAG_OFFSET_TIME = 0x9010;
const DEFAULT_OFFSET_MIN = 8 * 60; // 沒有時差資訊時，視為台北時間（和網站顯示一致）

/** "2026:10:19 09:27:03" + 可選 "+08:00" → 毫秒；格式不對回傳 null。 */
export function parseExifDate(text, offsetText) {
  const m = /^(\d{4}):(\d{2}):(\d{2}) (\d{2}):(\d{2}):(\d{2})/.exec(text ?? '');
  if (!m) return null;
  const [y, mo, d, h, mi, s] = m.slice(1).map(Number);
  if (mo < 1 || mo > 12 || d < 1 || d > 31 || h > 23 || mi > 59 || s > 59 || y < 1990) return null;
  let offset = DEFAULT_OFFSET_MIN;
  const o = /^([+-])(\d{2}):(\d{2})/.exec((offsetText ?? '').trim());
  if (o) offset = (o[1] === '-' ? -1 : 1) * (Number(o[2]) * 60 + Number(o[3]));
  return Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d, h, mi, s) - offset * 60000;
}

function readIfd(view, tiff, ifdOffset, little, wanted) {
  const found = {};
  if (ifdOffset <= 0 || tiff + ifdOffset + 2 > view.byteLength) return found;
  const count = view.getUint16(tiff + ifdOffset, little);
  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const e = tiff + ifdOffset + 2 + i * 12;
    if (e + 12 > view.byteLength) break;
    const tag = view.getUint16(e, little);
    if (!wanted.includes(tag)) continue;
    const type = view.getUint16(e + 2, little);
    const n = view.getUint32(e + 4, little);
    if (type === 4 && n === 1) { found[tag] = view.getUint32(e + 8, little); continue; } // LONG（ExifIFD 指標）
    if (type !== 2) continue; // 其餘只處理 ASCII
    const dataAt = n <= 4 ? e + 8 : tiff + view.getUint32(e + 8, little);
    if (dataAt < 0 || dataAt + n > view.byteLength) continue;
    let str = '';
    for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
      const c = view.getUint8(dataAt + k);
      if (c === 0) break;
      str += String.fromCharCode(c);
    }
    found[tag] = str;
  }
  return found;
}

/** 從 JPEG 位元組取拍攝時間（毫秒）；沒有 EXIF 日期回傳 null。優先順序：原始拍攝 > 數位化 > 檔案修改時間欄位。 */
export function readExifTime(buffer) {
  const view = new DataView(buffer);
  if (view.byteLength < 12 || view.getUint16(0) !== 0xffd8) return null;
  let pos = 2;
  while (pos + 4 <= view.byteLength) {
    if (view.getUint8(pos) !== 0xff) return null;
    const marker = view.getUint8(pos + 1);
    if (marker === 0xda || marker === 0xd9) return null; // 影像資料開始，沒有 EXIF
    const len = view.getUint16(pos + 2);
    if (marker === 0xe1 && pos + 10 <= view.byteLength && view.getUint32(pos + 4) === 0x45786966) { // "Exif"
      const tiff = pos + 10;
      const little = view.getUint16(tiff) === 0x4949;
      if (view.getUint16(tiff + 2, little) !== 42) return null;
      const ifd0 = readIfd(view, tiff, view.getUint32(tiff + 4, little), little, [TAG_DATETIME, TAG_EXIF_IFD]);
      const exif = typeof ifd0[TAG_EXIF_IFD] === 'number'
        ? readIfd(view, tiff, ifd0[TAG_EXIF_IFD], little, [TAG_DT_ORIGINAL, TAG_DT_DIGITIZED, TAG_OFFSET_ORIGINAL, TAG_OFFSET_DIGITIZED, TAG_OFFSET_TIME])
        : {};
      return parseExifDate(exif[TAG_DT_ORIGINAL], exif[TAG_OFFSET_ORIGINAL] ?? exif[TAG_OFFSET_TIME])
        ?? parseExifDate(exif[TAG_DT_DIGITIZED], exif[TAG_OFFSET_DIGITIZED] ?? exif[TAG_OFFSET_TIME])
        ?? parseExifDate(ifd0[TAG_DATETIME], exif[TAG_OFFSET_TIME]);
    }
    pos += 2 + len;
  }
  return null;
}


===== web\export.js =====
// 匯出：CSV 與最小 XLSX 產生器（純函式，無第三方套件）。
import { TYPES, formatTime, sourceLabel, contextLabel } from './lib.js';

export const EXPORT_HEADER = ['時間', '類型', '數值', '數值2', '情境', '備註', '來源'];

/** 紀錄 → 表格列。時間為台北時間 yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm（與 Android App 的 CSV 匯入格式相同，可直接匯回）。 */
export function toRows(records) {
  return records.map((r) => [
    formatTime(r.measured_at),
    TYPES[r.type]?.label ?? r.type,
    r.value,
    r.value2 ?? '',
    contextLabel(r.context),
    r.note ?? '',
    sourceLabel(r.source),
  ]);
}

/** 以 = + - @ Tab CR 開頭的文字，Excel 會當公式執行（CSV 注入）；前面加單引號讓它只是文字。 */
export function guardFormula(text) {
  return /^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(text) ? `'${text}` : text;
}

function csvCell(v) {
  if (typeof v === 'number') return String(v);
  const s = guardFormula(String(v ?? ''));
  return /[",\r\n]/.test(s) ? `"${s.replaceAll('"', '""')}"` : s;
}

/** 回傳含 UTF-8 BOM 的 CSV 字串（Excel 直接開啟中文才不會亂碼）。 */
export function toCsv(rows, header = EXPORT_HEADER) {
  return '﻿' + [header, ...rows].map((row) => row.map(csvCell).join(',')).join('\r\n') + '\r\n';
}

// ---------- XLSX（store-only zip） ----------
const enc = new TextEncoder();

const CRC_TABLE = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let n = 0; n < 256; n++) {
    let c = n;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
    t[n] = c >>> 0;
  }
  return t;
})();

export function crc32(bytes) {
  let c = 0xffffffff;
  for (const b of bytes) c = CRC_TABLE[(c ^ b) & 0xff] ^ (c >>> 8);
  return (c ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

export function escapeXml(s) {
  return String(s)
    .replace(/[\u0000-\u0008\u000B\u000C\u000E-\u001F]/g, '') // XML 不允許的控制字元
    .replaceAll('&', '&amp;').replaceAll('<', '&lt;').replaceAll('>', '&gt;').replaceAll('"', '&quot;');
}

export function columnName(i) {
  let n = i + 1;
  let s = '';
  while (n > 0) { s = String.fromCharCode(65 + ((n - 1) % 26)) + s; n = Math.floor((n - 1) / 26); }
  return s;
}

function sheetXml(rows) {
  const body = rows.map((row, r) => {
    const cells = row.map((v, c) => {
      const ref = `${columnName(c)}${r + 1}`;
      if (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v)) return `<c r="${ref}"><v>${v}</v></c>`;
      if (v === '' || v == null) return '';
      return `<c r="${ref}" t="inlineStr"><is><t xml:space="preserve">${escapeXml(v)}</t></is></c>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<row r="${r + 1}">${cells}</row>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><worksheet xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main"><sheetData>${body}</sheetData></worksheet>`;
}

function zipStore(files) {
  const parts = [];
  const central = [];
  let offset = 0;
  for (const [name, content] of files) {
    const nameBytes = enc.encode(name);
    const data = typeof content === 'string' ? enc.encode(content) : content;
    const crc = crc32(data);
    const local = new Uint8Array(30 + nameBytes.length);
    const lv = new DataView(local.buffer);
    lv.setUint32(0, 0x04034b50, true); lv.setUint16(4, 20, true); lv.setUint16(6, 0x0800, true); // UTF-8 檔名
    lv.setUint16(8, 0, true); lv.setUint16(10, 0, true); lv.setUint16(12, 0x21, true); // store、固定時間
    lv.setUint32(14, crc, true); lv.setUint32(18, data.length, true); lv.setUint32(22, data.length, true);
    lv.setUint16(26, nameBytes.length, true); lv.setUint16(28, 0, true);
    local.set(nameBytes, 30);
    const cen = new Uint8Array(46 + nameBytes.length);
    const cv = new DataView(cen.buffer);
    cv.setUint32(0, 0x02014b50, true); cv.setUint16(4, 20, true); cv.setUint16(6, 20, true); cv.setUint16(8, 0x0800, true);
    cv.setUint16(10, 0, true); cv.setUint16(12, 0, true); cv.setUint16(14, 0x21, true);
    cv.setUint32(16, crc, true); cv.setUint32(20, data.length, true); cv.setUint32(24, data.length, true);
    cv.setUint16(28, nameBytes.length, true); cv.setUint32(42, offset, true);
    cen.set(nameBytes, 46);
    parts.push(local, data);
    central.push(cen);
    offset += local.length + data.length;
  }
  const centralSize = central.reduce((a, c) => a + c.length, 0);
  const end = new Uint8Array(22);
  const ev = new DataView(end.buffer);
  ev.setUint32(0, 0x06054b50, true); ev.setUint16(8, files.length, true); ev.setUint16(10, files.length, true);
  ev.setUint32(12, centralSize, true); ev.setUint32(16, offset, true);
  const all = [...parts, ...central, end];
  const out = new Uint8Array(all.reduce((a, p) => a + p.length, 0));
  let pos = 0;
  for (const p of all) { out.set(p, pos); pos += p.length; }
  return out;
}

/** 產生 .xlsx 位元組。rows 第一列通常是標題；數字存成數字，文字存成文字。 */
export function buildXlsx(rows, sheetName = '健康紀錄') {
  const name = escapeXml(sheetName.replace(/[\\/?*[\]:]/g, '').slice(0, 31) || 'Sheet1');
  return zipStore([
    ['[Content_Types].xml', '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Types xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/content-types"><Default Extension="rels" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-package.relationships+xml"/><Default Extension="xml" ContentType="application/xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/workbook.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet.main+xml"/><Override PartName="/xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml" ContentType="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.worksheet+xml"/></Types>'],
    ['_rels/.rels', '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/officeDocument" Target="xl/workbook.xml"/></Relationships>'],
    ['xl/workbook.xml', `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><workbook xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main" xmlns:r="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships"><sheets><sheet name="${name}" sheetId="1" r:id="rId1"/></sheets></workbook>`],
    ['xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels', '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8" standalone="yes"?><Relationships xmlns="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships"><Relationship Id="rId1" Type="http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships/worksheet" Target="worksheets/sheet1.xml"/></Relationships>'],
    ['xl/worksheets/sheet1.xml', sheetXml(rows)],
  ]);
}

/** 下載檔名：HealthTracker_<範圍>_<yyyyMMdd>.<副檔名>（日期用台北時間）。 */
export function exportFileName(label, ext, now = Date.now()) {
  const d = formatTime(now).slice(0, 10).replaceAll('-', '');
  return `HealthTracker_${label}_${d}.${ext}`;
}

===== web\lib.js =====
// 純函式（不碰 DOM、不連網），方便用 node --test 測試。
export const TYPES = {
  WEIGHT: { label: '體重', unit: 'kg', digits: 1 },
  BODY_FAT: { label: '體脂率', unit: '%', digits: 1 },
  BLOOD_GLUCOSE: { label: '血糖', unit: 'mg/dL', digits: 0 },
  BLOOD_PRESSURE: { label: '血壓', unit: 'mmHg', digits: 0 },
};

export const SOURCE_LABELS = {
  MANUAL: '手動輸入',
  OCR: '拍照辨識',
  MIJIA: '米家畫面',
  IMPORT: '匯入',
  HEALTH_CONNECT: 'Health Connect',
};

export const CONTEXT_LABELS = { FASTING: '空腹', BEFORE_MEAL: '餐前', AFTER_MEAL: '餐後', BEDTIME: '睡前' };

export function contextLabel(code) {
  return CONTEXT_LABELS[code] ?? '';
}

export const RANGES = [
  { key: '30', label: '30 天', days: 30 },
  { key: '90', label: '90 天', days: 90 },
  { key: '365', label: '1 年', days: 365 },
  { key: 'all', label: '全部', days: null },
];

const DAY_MS = 86400000;

/** 起算時間（epoch 毫秒）；null 表示不限。 */
export function rangeStart(key, now = Date.now()) {
  const r = RANGES.find((x) => x.key === key);
  if (!r || r.days == null) return null;
  return now - r.days * DAY_MS;
}

/** 只接受 https://*.supabase.co（或在 localhost 開發時的 http://localhost）。回傳去掉結尾斜線的網址，不合法就丟錯。 */
export function normalizeBaseUrl(input, pageHost = '') {
  let u;
  try {
    u = new URL(String(input).trim());
  } catch {
    throw new Error('網址格式不對');
  }
  const isSupabase = u.protocol === 'https:' && /^[a-z0-9-]+\.supabase\.co$/i.test(u.hostname);
  const isLoopback = (h) => h === 'localhost' || h === '127.0.0.1';
  const isLocalDev = u.protocol === 'http:' && isLoopback(u.hostname) && isLoopback(pageHost);
  if (!isSupabase && !isLocalDev) throw new Error('只接受 https://<專案>.supabase.co 的網址');
  return u.origin;
}

/** 組 PostgREST 查詢字串。type 為 null 表示全部類型。 */
export function buildRecordsQuery({ type = null, since = null, limit = 1000, offset = 0 } = {}) {
  const p = new URLSearchParams();
  p.set('select', '*'); // 用 * 才不會在雲端還沒有 context 欄位時整個查詢失敗
  p.set('deleted_at', 'is.null');
  if (type) p.set('type', `eq.${type}`);
  if (since != null) p.set('measured_at', `gte.${Math.floor(since)}`);
  // 同一時間有多筆時再以 id 排序，offset 分頁才不會跨頁重複或漏筆
  p.set('order', 'measured_at.desc,id.desc');
  p.set('limit', String(limit));
  if (offset > 0) p.set('offset', String(offset));
  return p.toString();
}

export function sessionNeedsRefresh(session, now = Date.now(), marginMs = 60000) {
  return !session || session.expiresAt - marginMs <= now;
}

export function formatValue(r) {
  const t = TYPES[r.type];
  if (!t) return String(r.value);
  if (r.type === 'BLOOD_PRESSURE') return `${Math.round(r.value)}/${Math.round(r.value2 ?? 0)} ${t.unit}`;
  return `${Number(r.value).toFixed(t.digits)} ${t.unit}`;
}

const pad = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');

/** 以台北時間顯示，不受瀏覽器所在時區影響。 */
export function formatTime(ms) {
  const d = new Date(ms + 8 * 3600000);
  return `${d.getUTCFullYear()}-${pad(d.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getUTCDate())} ${pad(d.getUTCHours())}:${pad(d.getUTCMinutes())}`;
}

export function sourceLabel(source) {
  return SOURCE_LABELS[source] ?? source ?? '';
}

function diastolicStats(records) {
  const v = records.map((r) => r.value2).filter((x) => x != null);
  if (!v.length) return null;
  return { min: Math.min(...v), max: Math.max(...v), avg: v.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / v.length };
}

/** 統計（單一數值類型）。沒有資料回傳 null。records 需為新到舊。 */
export function summarize(records) {
  if (!records.length) return null;
  const values = records.map((r) => r.value);
  const sum = values.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0);
  return {
    count: records.length,
    latest: records[0],
    min: Math.min(...values),
    max: Math.max(...values),
    avg: sum / values.length,
    // 區間變化：最新一筆減最早一筆（records 新到舊）。只有一筆時為 0。
    change: records[0].value - records[records.length - 1].value,
    // 血壓：舒張壓（value2）的統計；沒有任何 value2 時為 null
    diastolic: diastolicStats(records),
  };
}

/** 照片物件路徑必須是「<uuid>/<uuid>.jpg」的形狀，避免被塞入奇怪路徑後拿去組網址。 */
/**
 * 分頁抓完所有資料。fetchPage(offset, limit) 回傳該頁的列。
 * 超過 maxPages 頁仍有資料就標示 truncated（呼叫端必須告知使用者，不能默默匯出不完整的檔案）。
 * 剛好滿 maxPages 頁且後面沒有資料時不算截斷（多探一筆確認）。
 */
export async function fetchAllPages(fetchPage, { pageSize = 1000, maxPages = 50 } = {}) {
  const rows = [];
  for (let page = 0; page < maxPages; page++) {
    const batch = await fetchPage(page * pageSize, pageSize);
    rows.push(...batch);
    if (batch.length < pageSize) return { rows, truncated: false };
  }
  const probe = await fetchPage(maxPages * pageSize, 1);
  return { rows, truncated: probe.length > 0 };
}

export function isSafePhotoPath(path) {
  return typeof path === 'string' && /^[0-9a-f-]{36}\/[0-9a-f-]{36}\.jpg$/i.test(path);
}


===== web\ocr.js =====
// 血糖機照片的 OCR：辨識在瀏覽器本機完成（tesseract.js，檔案放在 vendor/，照片不會送到第三方）。
// 挑數值的規則與手機 App 的 GlucoseOcrParser 相同。

export const MIN_MGDL = 20;
export const MAX_MGDL = 800;

const NUMBER = /(?<![\d:/.\-])(\d{2,3})(?![\d:/.\-])/g;
const UNIT = /mg\s*\/?\s*d\s*l/i;

/** 七段顯示器常被讀成字母：在含數字的 2～3 字元片段內，把 O→0、I/l→1 修回來。 */
export function fixDigitLookalikes(text) {
  return text.replace(/[0-9OoIl]{2,3}/g, (t) => (/\d/.test(t) ? t.replace(/[Oo]/g, '0').replace(/[Il]/g, '1') : t));
}

/** lines: [{ text, height }]。回傳候選 [{ value, score, lineText }]，分數高者在前（行越高、靠近 mg/dL 字樣越可信）。 */
export function glucoseCandidates(lines) {
  if (!lines.length) return [];
  const maxH = Math.max(1, ...lines.map((l) => l.height));
  const best = new Map();
  lines.forEach((line, i) => {
    const text = fixDigitLookalikes(line.text);
    const nearUnit = [i - 1, i, i + 1].some((j) => lines[j] && UNIT.test(lines[j].text));
    for (const m of text.matchAll(NUMBER)) {
      const v = Number(m[1]);
      if (v < MIN_MGDL || v > MAX_MGDL) continue;
      const score = line.height / maxH + (nearUnit ? 0.5 : 0);
      const prev = best.get(v);
      if (!prev || score > prev.score) best.set(v, { value: v, score, lineText: line.text });
    }
  });
  return [...best.values()].sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score);
}

const BASE = new URL('./vendor/tesseract/', import.meta.url).href;
let enginePromise = null;

function loadScript(src) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const s = document.createElement('script');
    s.src = src;
    s.onload = resolve;
    s.onerror = () => reject(new Error('OCR 元件載入失敗'));
    document.head.append(s);
  });
}

/** 第一次呼叫才載入（約 7 MB），之後重複使用同一個 worker。 */
export function getEngine() {
  enginePromise ??= (async () => {
    await loadScript(`${BASE}tesseract.min.js`);
    const worker = await globalThis.Tesseract.createWorker('eng', 1, {
      workerPath: `${BASE}worker.min.js`,
      corePath: BASE, // 目錄：自動挑 simd / 一般版本
      langPath: BASE,
      workerBlobURL: false, // 直接用同網域的 worker 檔，符合 CSP
      gzip: true,
    });
    return worker;
  })().catch((e) => { enginePromise = null; throw e; });
  return enginePromise;
}

/** 對一張圖（Blob / canvas）做 OCR，回傳 [{ text, height }]。不設字元白名單（實測會讓 LSTM 引擎整張讀不出來）。 */
export async function recognizeLines(image, psm = '3') {
  const worker = await getEngine();
  await worker.setParameters({ tessedit_pageseg_mode: psm }); // 3 自動分頁；11 稀疏文字（散落的數字）
  const { data } = await worker.recognize(image, {}, { blocks: true });
  const lines = [];
  for (const block of data.blocks ?? []) {
    for (const p of block.paragraphs ?? []) {
      for (const l of p.lines ?? []) {
        const text = (l.text ?? '').trim();
        if (text) lines.push({ text, height: Math.max(1, (l.bbox?.y1 ?? 0) - (l.bbox?.y0 ?? 0)) });
      }
    }
  }
  return lines;
}
