===== web\tests\chart-export.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { niceScale, buildSeries, timeTicks, layout, nearestIndex, formatTick } from '../chart.js';
import { toRows, toCsv, guardFormula, crc32, columnName, escapeXml, buildXlsx, exportFileName, EXPORT_HEADER } from '../export.js';
import { buildRecordsQuery, summarize } from '../lib.js';

const DAY = 86400000;
const rec = (t, v, extra = {}) => ({ id: String(t), type: 'WEIGHT', value: v, value2: null, measured_at: t, source: 'MANUAL', note: '', photo_object: null, ...extra });

test('niceScale：刻度落在 1/2/5 的倍數並涵蓋範圍', () => {
  const s = niceScale(68.6, 71.3);
  assert.ok(s.min <= 68.6 && s.max >= 71.3);
  assert.ok(s.ticks.length >= 3 && s.ticks.length <= 8);
  const step = s.ticks[1] - s.ticks[0];
  assert.ok([0.5, 1, 2, 5].includes(Number(step.toFixed(6))));
});

test('niceScale：全部相同的值也有可畫的範圍', () => {
  const s = niceScale(70, 70);
  assert.ok(s.max > s.min);
});

test('niceScale：無效輸入不丟錯', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(niceScale(NaN, 3).ticks, [0, 1]);
});

test('buildSeries：舊到新排序；血壓拆成收縮壓與舒張壓兩條', () => {
  const s = buildSeries('WEIGHT', [rec(300, 70), rec(100, 71), rec(200, 69)]);
  assert.equal(s.length, 1);
  assert.deepEqual(s[0].points.map((p) => p.t), [100, 200, 300]);
  const bp = buildSeries('BLOOD_PRESSURE', [
    rec(200, 120, { type: 'BLOOD_PRESSURE', value2: 80 }),
    rec(100, 118, { type: 'BLOOD_PRESSURE', value2: null }),
  ]);
  assert.deepEqual(bp.map((x) => x.label), ['收縮壓', '舒張壓']);
  assert.equal(bp[0].points.length, 2);
  assert.equal(bp[1].points.length, 1);
  assert.equal(bp[1].points[0].v, 80);
});

test('timeTicks：落在台北午夜，數量適中且遞增', () => {
  const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 8, 1, 3, 0); // 台北 9/1 11:00
  const ticks = timeTicks(t0, t0 + 90 * DAY);
  assert.ok(ticks.length >= 3 && ticks.length <= 9);
  for (const t of ticks) assert.equal((t + 8 * 3600000) % DAY, 0);
  for (let i = 1; i < ticks.length; i++) assert.ok(ticks[i] > ticks[i - 1]);
  assert.match(formatTick(ticks[0]), /^\d\d\/\d\d$/);
});

test('timeTicks：只有一天資料也不會無窮迴圈', () => {
  const t0 = Date.UTC(2026, 8, 1, 3, 0);
  assert.ok(timeTicks(t0, t0).length <= 3);
});

test('layout：座標落在繪圖區內、較新的在右、較大的值在上', () => {
  const series = buildSeries('WEIGHT', [rec(0, 68), rec(10 * DAY, 72)]);
  const g = layout(series, { width: 720, height: 300, margin: { top: 16, right: 64, bottom: 28, left: 48 } });
  assert.equal(g.x(0), 48);
  assert.equal(g.x(10 * DAY), 720 - 64);
  assert.ok(g.yPos(72) < g.yPos(68));
  assert.ok(g.yPos(g.y.min) <= 300 - 28 + 0.001);
  assert.ok(g.yPos(g.y.max) >= 16 - 0.001);
});

test('layout：單一時間點置中', () => {
  const g = layout(buildSeries('WEIGHT', [rec(5, 70)]), { width: 720, height: 300, margin: { top: 16, right: 64, bottom: 28, left: 48 } });
  assert.equal(g.x(5), 48 + (720 - 48 - 64) / 2);
});

test('nearestIndex：挑最近的點', () => {
  assert.equal(nearestIndex([10, 20, 30], 26), 2);
  assert.equal(nearestIndex([10, 20, 30], 14), 0);
  assert.equal(nearestIndex([10], 999), 0);
});

test('summarize：區間變化 = 最新 − 最早', () => {
  assert.equal(summarize([{ value: 70 }, { value: 72 }, { value: 71 }]).change, -1);
  assert.equal(summarize([{ value: 70 }]).change, 0);
});

test('buildRecordsQuery：offset 只在大於 0 時帶上', () => {
  assert.equal(new URLSearchParams(buildRecordsQuery({ offset: 1000 })).get('offset'), '1000');
  assert.equal(new URLSearchParams(buildRecordsQuery()).has('offset'), false);
});

test('toRows：台北時間、中文類型、數字保持數字', () => {
  const rows = toRows([rec(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 18, 22, 33), 70, { source: 'MIJIA', note: '晨間' })]);
  assert.deepEqual(rows[0], ['2026-10-19 06:33', '體重', 70, '', '', '晨間', '米家畫面']);
});

test('guardFormula：公式開頭字元加單引號，一般文字不動', () => {
  for (const s of ['=1+1', '+cmd', '-2', '@SUM(A1)']) assert.equal(guardFormula(s), `'${s}`);
  assert.equal(guardFormula('早餐前'), '早餐前');
});

test('toCsv：BOM、標題、CRLF、逗號與引號跳脫、公式防護', () => {
  const csv = toCsv([['2026-10-19 06:33', '體重', 70.5, '', '', '含,逗號與"引號"', '手動輸入'], ['t', '體重', 1, '', '', '=HYPERLINK("x")', '']]);
  assert.ok(csv.startsWith('﻿時間,類型,數值,數值2,情境,備註,來源\r\n'));
  assert.ok(csv.includes('"含,逗號與""引號"""'));
  assert.ok(csv.includes(`"'=HYPERLINK(""x"")"`));
  assert.ok(csv.endsWith('\r\n'));
});

test('crc32：標準測試向量', () => {
  assert.equal(crc32(new TextEncoder().encode('123456789')), 0xcbf43926);
});

test('columnName 與 escapeXml', () => {
  assert.deepEqual([0, 25, 26, 27, 701, 702].map(columnName), ['A', 'Z', 'AA', 'AB', 'ZZ', 'AAA']);
  assert.equal(escapeXml('a<b>&"c"\u0001'), 'a&lt;b&gt;&amp;&quot;c&quot;');
});

test('buildXlsx：產出合法 zip（PK 開頭、結尾記錄筆數正確）', () => {
  const bytes = buildXlsx([EXPORT_HEADER, ...toRows([rec(1000, 70)])]);
  assert.equal(bytes[0], 0x50);
  assert.equal(bytes[1], 0x4b);
  const dv = new DataView(bytes.buffer, bytes.byteOffset, bytes.byteLength);
  assert.equal(dv.getUint32(bytes.length - 22, true), 0x06054b50);
  assert.equal(dv.getUint16(bytes.length - 22 + 10, true), 5);
});

test('exportFileName：含範圍與台北日期', () => {
  assert.equal(exportFileName('體重_90天', 'csv', Date.UTC(2026, 9, 19, 12)), 'HealthTracker_體重_90天_20261019.csv');
});


===== web\tests\lib.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import {
  fetchAllPages,
  contextLabel,
  rangeStart, normalizeBaseUrl, buildRecordsQuery, sessionNeedsRefresh,
  formatValue, formatTime, sourceLabel, summarize, isSafePhotoPath,
} from '../lib.js';

const NOW = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 19, 12, 0, 0);

test('rangeStart：天數換算與「全部」不限', () => {
  assert.equal(rangeStart('30', NOW), NOW - 30 * 86400000);
  assert.equal(rangeStart('365', NOW), NOW - 365 * 86400000);
  assert.equal(rangeStart('all', NOW), null);
  assert.equal(rangeStart('bogus', NOW), null);
});

test('normalizeBaseUrl：只接受 supabase.co，去掉路徑與結尾斜線', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBaseUrl('https://abcd1234.supabase.co/'), 'https://abcd1234.supabase.co');
  assert.equal(normalizeBaseUrl(' https://abcd1234.supabase.co/rest/v1 '), 'https://abcd1234.supabase.co');
});

test('normalizeBaseUrl：拒絕不明網址、http、假冒網域', () => {
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('https://evil.example.com'));
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('http://abcd.supabase.co'));
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('https://abcd.supabase.co.evil.com'));
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('https://evil.com/?x=.supabase.co'));
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('not a url'));
});

test('normalizeBaseUrl：localhost 只在頁面本身也是 localhost 時才接受', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBaseUrl('http://localhost:8765', 'localhost'), 'http://localhost:8765');
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('http://localhost:8765', 'wahaha232.github.io'));
});

test('buildRecordsQuery：排除已刪除、依時間新到舊、帶類型與起算時間', () => {
  const q = new URLSearchParams(buildRecordsQuery({ type: 'WEIGHT', since: 1000.9, limit: 50 }));
  assert.equal(q.get('deleted_at'), 'is.null');
  assert.equal(q.get('type'), 'eq.WEIGHT');
  assert.equal(q.get('measured_at'), 'gte.1000');
  assert.equal(q.get('order'), 'measured_at.desc,id.desc');
  assert.equal(q.get('limit'), '50');
  assert.equal(q.get('select'), '*');
});

test('buildRecordsQuery：不指定類型與時間就不帶這兩個條件', () => {
  const q = new URLSearchParams(buildRecordsQuery());
  assert.equal(q.has('type'), false);
  assert.equal(q.has('measured_at'), false);
});

test('sessionNeedsRefresh：一分鐘內到期或沒有 session 就需要續期', () => {
  assert.equal(sessionNeedsRefresh(null, NOW), true);
  assert.equal(sessionNeedsRefresh({ expiresAt: NOW + 30000 }, NOW), true);
  assert.equal(sessionNeedsRefresh({ expiresAt: NOW + 120000 }, NOW), false);
});

test('formatValue：各類型的位數與單位', () => {
  assert.equal(formatValue({ type: 'WEIGHT', value: 70 }), '70.0 kg');
  assert.equal(formatValue({ type: 'BODY_FAT', value: 23 }), '23.0 %');
  assert.equal(formatValue({ type: 'BLOOD_GLUCOSE', value: 112.4 }), '112 mg/dL');
  assert.equal(formatValue({ type: 'BLOOD_PRESSURE', value: 120, value2: 80 }), '120/80 mmHg');
  assert.equal(formatValue({ type: 'UNKNOWN', value: 5 }), '5');
});

test('formatTime：固定用台北時間（UTC+8）', () => {
  assert.equal(formatTime(Date.UTC(2026, 9, 18, 22, 33)), '2026-10-19 06:33');
  assert.equal(formatTime(Date.UTC(2026, 11, 31, 16, 5)), '2027-01-01 00:05');
});

test('sourceLabel：已知來源翻成中文、未知原樣顯示', () => {
  assert.equal(sourceLabel('MIJIA'), '米家畫面');
  assert.equal(sourceLabel('OCR'), '拍照辨識');
  assert.equal(sourceLabel('FUTURE'), 'FUTURE');
  assert.equal(sourceLabel(undefined), '');
});

test('summarize：最新、筆數、平均、最低、最高', () => {
  assert.equal(summarize([]), null);
  const s = summarize([{ value: 70 }, { value: 72 }, { value: 68 }]);
  assert.equal(s.count, 3);
  assert.equal(s.latest.value, 70);
  assert.equal(s.min, 68);
  assert.equal(s.max, 72);
  assert.equal(s.avg, 70);
});

test('normalizeBaseUrl：127.0.0.1 與 localhost 都算本機開發', () => {
  assert.equal(normalizeBaseUrl('http://127.0.0.1:8791', '127.0.0.1'), 'http://127.0.0.1:8791');
  assert.equal(normalizeBaseUrl('http://localhost:8791', '127.0.0.1'), 'http://localhost:8791');
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('http://127.0.0.1:8791', 'wahaha232.github.io'));
  assert.throws(() => normalizeBaseUrl('http://127.0.0.2:8791', '127.0.0.2'));
});

test('summarize：血壓的舒張壓統計', () => {
  const s = summarize([{ value: 120, value2: 80 }, { value: 130, value2: 90 }, { value: 110, value2: null }]);
  assert.deepEqual(s.diastolic, { min: 80, max: 90, avg: 85 });
  assert.equal(summarize([{ value: 70 }]).diastolic, null);
});

// 假的分頁來源：總共 total 筆，依 offset/limit 回傳
const source = (total) => async (offset, limit) =>
  Array.from({ length: Math.max(0, Math.min(limit, total - offset)) }, (_, i) => offset + i);

for (const [total, truncated] of [[0, false], [999, false], [1000, false], [1001, false], [20000, false], [20001, false]]) {
  test(`fetchAllPages：${total} 筆（預設 50 頁上限）完整取回，不截斷`, async () => {
    const r = await fetchAllPages(source(total));
    assert.equal(r.rows.length, total);
    assert.equal(r.truncated, truncated);
    assert.equal(new Set(r.rows).size, total);
  });
}

test('fetchAllPages：超過上限標示截斷，剛好等於上限不算截斷', async () => {
  const exact = await fetchAllPages(source(5000), { pageSize: 1000, maxPages: 5 });
  assert.deepEqual([exact.rows.length, exact.truncated], [5000, false]);
  const over = await fetchAllPages(source(5001), { pageSize: 1000, maxPages: 5 });
  assert.deepEqual([over.rows.length, over.truncated], [5000, true]);
});

test('fetchAllPages：第一頁不滿就只請求一次', async () => {
  let calls = 0;
  await fetchAllPages(async (o, l) => { calls++; return source(10)(o, l); });
  assert.equal(calls, 1);
});

test('contextLabel：已知情境翻成中文，未知或空值回傳空字串', () => {
  assert.equal(contextLabel('FASTING'), '空腹');
  assert.equal(contextLabel('AFTER_MEAL'), '餐後');
  assert.equal(contextLabel('NOPE'), '');
  assert.equal(contextLabel(null), '');
});

test('buildRecordsQuery：select 用 *，雲端缺欄位時也不會整個失敗', () => {
  assert.equal(new URLSearchParams(buildRecordsQuery()).get('select'), '*');
});

test('isSafePhotoPath：只接受 <uuid>/<uuid>.jpg', () => {
  const id = '123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000';
  assert.equal(isSafePhotoPath(`${id}/${id}.jpg`), true);
  assert.equal(isSafePhotoPath(`../${id}.jpg`), false);
  assert.equal(isSafePhotoPath(`${id}/${id}.jpg?x=1`), false);
  assert.equal(isSafePhotoPath(`${id}/../${id}.jpg`), false);
  assert.equal(isSafePhotoPath(null), false);
});


===== web\tests\photo-import.test.mjs =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readExifTime, parseExifDate } from '../exif.js';
import { glucoseCandidates, fixDigitLookalikes } from '../ocr.js';
import { toLocalInput, fromLocalInput, isDuplicate, validateRow } from '../batch.js';

/** 手工組一個只有 EXIF 日期的最小 JPEG（little-endian TIFF）。 */
function jpegWithExif({ original, offset, ifd0Date, big = false }) {
  const bytes = [];
  const le = !big;
  const u16 = (n) => (le ? [n & 255, n >> 8] : [n >> 8, n & 255]);
  const u32 = (n) => (le ? [n & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, (n >> 16) & 255, n >>> 24] : [n >>> 24, (n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255]);
  const ascii = (s) => [...s].map((c) => c.charCodeAt(0)).concat(0);
  const entry = (tag, type, n, val) => [...u16(tag), ...u16(type), ...u32(n), ...val];

  const dates = [];
  const exifEntries = [];
  const ifd0Len = 2 + 12 * (ifd0Date ? 2 : 1) + 4;
  const exifIfdAt = 8 + ifd0Len;
  const exifCount = (original ? 1 : 0) + (offset ? 1 : 0);
  let dataAt = exifIfdAt + 2 + 12 * exifCount + 4;
  const place = (s) => { const at = dataAt; dates.push(...ascii(s)); dataAt += s.length + 1; return at; };
  const ifd0DataAt = ifd0Date ? null : null;
  void ifd0DataAt;
  if (original) exifEntries.push(entry(0x9003, 2, original.length + 1, u32(place(original))));
  if (offset) exifEntries.push(entry(0x9011, 2, offset.length + 1, [...ascii(offset), ...Array(Math.max(0, 4 - (offset.length + 1)))].slice(0, 4)));
  const ifd0 = [...u16(ifd0Date ? 2 : 1)];
  ifd0.push(...entry(0x8769, 4, 1, u32(exifIfdAt)));
  if (ifd0Date) ifd0.push(...entry(0x0132, 2, ifd0Date.length + 1, u32(place(ifd0Date))));
  ifd0.push(...u32(0));
  const tiff = [...(le ? [0x49, 0x49] : [0x4d, 0x4d]), ...u16(42), ...u32(8), ...ifd0,
    ...u16(exifCount), ...exifEntries.flat(), ...u32(0), ...dates];
  const app1 = [0x45, 0x78, 0x69, 0x66, 0, 0, ...tiff];
  bytes.push(0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xe1, (app1.length + 2) >> 8, (app1.length + 2) & 255, ...app1, 0xff, 0xda, 0, 2, 0xff, 0xd9);
  return new Uint8Array(bytes).buffer;
}

const taipei = (y, mo, d, h, mi, s) => Date.UTC(y, mo - 1, d, h - 8, mi, s);

test('parseExifDate：無時差視為台北；有時差照時差；格式錯誤為 null', () => {
  assert.equal(parseExifDate('2026:10:19 09:27:03'), taipei(2026, 10, 19, 9, 27, 3));
  assert.equal(parseExifDate('2026:10:19 09:27:03', '+00:00'), Date.UTC(2026, 9, 19, 9, 27, 3));
  assert.equal(parseExifDate('0000:00:00 00:00:00'), null);
  assert.equal(parseExifDate(''), null);
  assert.equal(parseExifDate(undefined), null);
});

test('readExifTime：讀原始拍攝時間（little / big endian）', () => {
  assert.equal(readExifTime(jpegWithExif({ original: '2026:10:19 09:27:03' })), taipei(2026, 10, 19, 9, 27, 3));
  assert.equal(readExifTime(jpegWithExif({ original: '2026:10:19 09:27:03', big: true })), taipei(2026, 10, 19, 9, 27, 3));
});

test('readExifTime：原始拍攝時間優先於 IFD0 的修改時間', () => {
  const buf = jpegWithExif({ original: '2026:10:19 09:27:03', ifd0Date: '2026:10:20 10:00:00' });
  assert.equal(readExifTime(buf), taipei(2026, 10, 19, 9, 27, 3));
});

test('readExifTime：沒有 EXIF 或不是 JPEG 回傳 null', () => {
  assert.equal(readExifTime(new Uint8Array([0xff, 0xd8, 0xff, 0xda, 0, 2, 0xff, 0xd9, 0, 0, 0, 0]).buffer), null);
  assert.equal(readExifTime(new Uint8Array(20).buffer), null);
  assert.equal(readExifTime(new ArrayBuffer(3)), null);
});

test('glucoseCandidates：挑最大字、靠近 mg/dL 的數字，排除日期時間', () => {
  const lines = [
    { text: '10/19 09:27', height: 20 },
    { text: '104', height: 120 },
    { text: 'mg/dL', height: 30 },
    { text: '2026', height: 25 },
  ];
  const c = glucoseCandidates(lines);
  assert.equal(c[0].value, 104);
  assert.ok(!c.some((x) => x.value === 2026 || x.value === 19));
});

test('glucoseCandidates：空輸入與範圍外的數字', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(glucoseCandidates([]), []);
  assert.deepEqual(glucoseCandidates([{ text: '15', height: 10 }, { text: '999', height: 10 }]), []);
});

test('fixDigitLookalikes：含數字的片段修字母，純字母不動', () => {
  assert.equal(fixDigitLookalikes('1O4'), '104');
  assert.equal(fixDigitLookalikes('l2O'), '120');
  assert.equal(fixDigitLookalikes('Ill'), 'Ill');
});

test('datetime-local 與毫秒互轉（台北時間）', () => {
  const ms = taipei(2026, 10, 19, 9, 27, 0);
  assert.equal(toLocalInput(ms), '2026-10-19T09:27');
  assert.equal(fromLocalInput('2026-10-19T09:27'), ms);
  assert.ok(Number.isNaN(fromLocalInput('')));
});

test('isDuplicate：數值相同且 ±2 分鐘內才算重複', () => {
  const ex = [{ value: 104, measured_at: 1_000_000 }];
  assert.equal(isDuplicate(ex, 104, 1_000_000 + 119_000), true);
  assert.equal(isDuplicate(ex, 104, 1_000_000 + 121_000), false);
  assert.equal(isDuplicate(ex, 105, 1_000_000), false);
});

test('validateRow：數值範圍、時間合理性', () => {
  const now = 2_000_000_000_000;
  assert.equal(validateRow(104, now - 1000, now), null);
  assert.match(validateRow(10, now, now), /20–800/);
  assert.match(validateRow(900, now, now), /20–800/);
  assert.match(validateRow(NaN, now, now), /20–800/);
  assert.match(validateRow(100, NaN, now), /時間/);
  assert.match(validateRow(100, now + 3_600_000, now), /未來/);
});


===== web-dev\mock_server.py =====
"""本機測試用的假 Supabase + 靜態檔伺服器（只在開發時用，不會發佈）。
用法：python web-dev/mock_server.py   然後開 http://localhost:8791/
測試帳號（假的，只對這個假伺服器有效）：demo@example.com / demo-pass
"""
import base64, io, json, os, random, time, uuid
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs

PORT = int(os.environ.get('MOCK_PORT', '8791'))
WEB = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), '..', 'web')
EMAIL, PASSWORD = 'demo@example.com', 'demo-pass'
EXPIRES_IN = int(os.environ.get('MOCK_EXPIRES_IN', '3600'))
PHOTO_ID = '123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000'
USER_ID = '00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001'
PHOTO_PATH = f'{USER_ID}/{PHOTO_ID}.jpg'

now = int(time.time() * 1000)
DAY = 86400000
random.seed(7)
ROWS = []
def add(t, v, v2, days_ago, source='MANUAL', note='', photo=None, deleted=None, context=None):
    ROWS.append(dict(id=str(uuid.uuid4()), type=t, value=v, value2=v2, measured_at=now - int(days_ago * DAY),
                     source=source, note=note, photo_object=photo, deleted_at=deleted, context=context))
for i in range(0, 40, 2):
    add('WEIGHT', round(70 + random.uniform(-1.5, 1.5), 1), None, i, 'MIJIA' if i % 4 == 0 else 'MANUAL')
add('WEIGHT', 99.9, None, 1, note='已刪除的不應出現', deleted=now)
add('WEIGHT', 71.0, None, 200, note='半年多前')
for i in range(0, 30, 5): add('BODY_FAT', round(22 + random.uniform(-1, 1), 1), None, i, 'MIJIA')
add('BLOOD_GLUCOSE', 112, None, 0.2, 'OCR', '早餐前', PHOTO_PATH, context='FASTING')
add('BLOOD_GLUCOSE', 98, None, 1.1, 'MANUAL', '<img src=x onerror=alert(1)> 備註含 HTML')
add('BLOOD_GLUCOSE', 135, None, 3, 'OCR', 'photo 不存在', f'{USER_ID}/{uuid.uuid4()}.jpg', context='AFTER_MEAL')
add('BLOOD_PRESSURE', 120, 80, 2)
add('BLOOD_PRESSURE', 118, 76, 9)

def make_jpeg():
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
    im = Image.new('RGB', (600, 400), (200, 215, 190))
    d = ImageDraw.Draw(im)
    d.text((220, 150), '112 mg/dL (mock photo)', fill=(20, 20, 20))
    buf = io.BytesIO(); im.save(buf, 'JPEG'); return buf.getvalue()
JPEG = make_jpeg()
UPLOADS = {}

def token(email, refresh_n=0):
    payload = base64.urlsafe_b64encode(json.dumps(dict(sub=USER_ID)).encode()).decode().rstrip('=')
    return dict(access_token=f'mock-access-{refresh_n}-{int(time.time())}.{payload}.sig', refresh_token=f'mock-refresh-{refresh_n + 1}',
                expires_in=EXPIRES_IN, token_type='bearer', user=dict(id=USER_ID, email=email))

class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def log_message(self, fmt, *a): print(self.command, self.path.split('?')[0], '->', a[1] if len(a) > 1 else '')
    def _send(self, code, body=b'', ctype='application/json'):
        if isinstance(body, (dict, list)): body = json.dumps(body).encode()
        self.send_response(code); self.send_header('Content-Type', ctype); self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
        self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*'); self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Headers', '*'); self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(body)
    def _authed(self):
        return self.headers.get('apikey') and (self.headers.get('Authorization') or '').startswith('Bearer mock-access-')
    def do_OPTIONS(self): self._send(204)
    def do_POST(self):
        u = urlparse(self.path); q = parse_qs(u.query)
        raw = self.rfile.read(int(self.headers.get('Content-Length', 0)))
        if u.path.startswith('/storage/v1/object/meter-photos/'):  # 照片上傳（只記下路徑與大小）
            if not self._authed(): return self._send(401, {})
            UPLOADS[u.path.removeprefix('/storage/v1/object/meter-photos/')] = len(raw)
            return self._send(200, dict(Key=u.path))
        body = json.loads(raw or b'{}')
        if u.path == '/rest/v1/health_records':  # 寫入紀錄（只在記憶體）
            if not self._authed(): return self._send(401, dict(code='PGRST301'))
            body.setdefault('deleted_at', None); body.setdefault('photo_object', None); body.setdefault('context', None)
            ROWS.append(body)
            return self._send(201, b'')
        if u.path == '/auth/v1/token':
            if q.get('grant_type') == ['password']:
                if body.get('email') == EMAIL and body.get('password') == PASSWORD: return self._send(200, token(EMAIL))
                return self._send(400, dict(error_code='invalid_credentials'))
            if q.get('grant_type') == ['refresh_token'] and str(body.get('refresh_token', '')).startswith('mock-refresh-'):
                return self._send(200, token(EMAIL, int(body['refresh_token'].split('-')[-1])))
            return self._send(400, dict(error_code='bad_refresh'))
        self._send(404, {})
    def do_GET(self):
        u = urlparse(self.path); q = parse_qs(u.query)
        if u.path == '/rest/v1/health_records':
            if not self._authed(): return self._send(401, dict(code='PGRST301'))
            rows = [r for r in ROWS if r['deleted_at'] is None] if q.get('deleted_at') == ['is.null'] else list(ROWS)
            if 'type' in q: rows = [r for r in rows if f"eq.{r['type']}" == q['type'][0]]
            for cond in q.get('measured_at', []):
                n = int(cond.split('.', 1)[1])
                rows = [r for r in rows if (r['measured_at'] >= n if cond.startswith('gte.') else r['measured_at'] <= n)]
            rows.sort(key=lambda r: (r['measured_at'], r['id']), reverse=True)
            rows = rows[:int(q.get('limit', ['1000'])[0])]
            cols = None if q.get('select', ['*'])[0] == '*' else q['select'][0].split(',')
            return self._send(200, [{k: r[k] for k in cols} if cols else r for r in rows])
        if u.path == '/storage/v1/object/authenticated/meter-photos/' + PHOTO_PATH:
            if not self._authed(): return self._send(401, {})
            return self._send(200, JPEG, 'image/jpeg')
        if u.path.startswith('/_testphotos/'):  # 開發測試用：提供自備的測試照片
            f = os.path.join(os.environ.get('MOCK_PHOTOS', ''), os.path.basename(u.path))
            if os.path.isfile(f): return self._send(200, open(f, 'rb').read(), 'image/jpeg')
            return self._send(404, {})
        if u.path.startswith('/storage/'): return self._send(404, dict(error='not_found'))
        rel = 'index.html' if u.path in ('/', '') else u.path.lstrip('/')
        f = os.path.normpath(os.path.join(WEB, rel))
        if not f.startswith(os.path.normpath(WEB)) or not os.path.isfile(f): return self._send(404, b'not found', 'text/plain')
        data = open(f, 'rb').read()
        ctype = {'.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.js': 'text/javascript', '.css': 'text/css', '.gz': 'application/gzip', '.wasm': 'application/wasm'}.get(os.path.splitext(f)[1], 'application/octet-stream')
        if rel == 'index.html':  # 只有開發用的假伺服器放寬 CSP，讓頁面能連到 localhost
            data = data.replace(b"connect-src 'self' https://*.supabase.co", f"connect-src 'self' http://localhost:{PORT} http://127.0.0.1:{PORT} https://*.supabase.co".encode())
        self._send(200, data, ctype)

if __name__ == '__main__':
    print(f'http://localhost:{PORT}/  帳號 {EMAIL} 密碼 {PASSWORD}')
    ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', PORT), H).serve_forever()
